  // counted in full. ⭐ That is a WRONG allowance, not a missing one, which is
  // why this is worth fixing even though the board never looked broken.
  // The pars were in D1 all along: course_tees.pars for the event's tee, then
  // courses.par. Event config still WINS — an organizer who set pars meant
  // them. The v2.66.0 rule is untouched: par_source still says 'none' when
  // nothing honest answers and we still never invent par 4. Fail-soft — any
  // error inside courseHolePars() is "no pars", the exact state the code below
  // already handles (cap = Infinity, par_source 'none').
  if (!pars) {
    const _cwp = await courseHolePars(env, event.course_id || null, event.tee || null);
    if (_cwp.pars) { pars = _cwp.pars; parSource = _cwp.source; }
  }

  const people = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT p.id AS player_id, p.name AS player_name, p.group_id,
            g.name AS group_name, g.start_hole, g.start_unit
       FROM players p LEFT JOIN groups g ON g.id = p.group_id
      WHERE p.event_id = ?`
  ).bind(event.id).all();

  const rows = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT s.player_id, s.hole, s.gross_score FROM scores s WHERE s.event_id = ?`
  ).bind(event.id).all();

  const byPlayer = new Map();
  for (const r of rows.results) {
    if (!byPlayer.has(r.player_id)) byPlayer.set(r.player_id, new Array(18).fill(null));
    if (r.hole >= 1 && r.hole <= 18) byPlayer.get(r.player_id)[r.hole - 1] = r.gross_score;
  }

  const standings = [];
  for (const p of people.results) {
    const holes = byPlayer.get(p.player_id) || new Array(18).fill(null);
    const c = cwCallaway(holes, pars);
    const scores_by_hole = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) if (holes[i] != null) scores_by_hole[i + 1] = holes[i];
    standings.push({
      player_id: p.player_id, player_name: p.player_name,
      group_id: p.group_id || null, group_name: p.group_name || null,
      start_hole: p.start_hole || null, start_unit: p.start_unit || null,
      holes_played: c.played, gross: c.gross, complete: c.complete,
      handicap: c.hcp, net: c.net,
      callaway_deduct: c.deduct != null ? c.deduct : null,
      callaway_adj: c.adj != null ? c.adj : null,
      callaway_holes: c.used || [],
      scores_by_hole,
      par_source: parSource                                            // v2.87.0 — 'event_config' | 'course_tee' | 'course' | 'none'
    });
  }

  // Finishers by net (gross breaks the tie). Partial cards keep their gross and
  // sort BELOW every finisher, most holes first — present, never hidden.
  standings.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.complete !== b.complete) return a.complete ? -1 : 1;
    if (a.complete) return a.net - b.net || a.gross - b.gross;
    return b.holes_played - a.holes_played || a.gross - b.gross;
  });
  return standings;
}

// ── v2.78.0 — D1: handicap allowance ─────────────────────────────────────
// cfg.tb.handicap_allowance is a per-team-position % vector (length =
// players_per_team; position 0 IS the individual case). Absent, malformed or
// out-of-range entries → 100% (full handicap — pre-D1 behavior, exactly).
function tbAllowancePct(cfg, pos) {
  const v = cfg && cfg.tb && Array.isArray(cfg.tb.handicap_allowance) ? cfg.tb.handicap_allowance[pos] : null;
  const n = Number(v);
  return (v != null && isFinite(n) && n >= 0 && n <= 200) ? n : 100;
}
function tbApplyAllowance(courseHcp, cfg, pos) {
  const pct = tbAllowancePct(cfg, pos);
  return pct === 100 ? courseHcp : Math.round(courseHcp * pct / 100 * 10) / 10;
}

async function recomputeLeaderboard(env, eventId) {
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, format, course_id, tee, config FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
  if (!event) return;
  if (event.format === 'mmg') {                                            // v2.63.0 — MMG standings into the same cache; scramble/RBC math below untouched
    try {
      const mmg = await computeMmgStandings(env, event);
      await env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO leaderboard_cache (event_id, computed_at, data) VALUES (?, datetime('now'), ?)
         ON CONFLICT(event_id) DO UPDATE SET computed_at = excluded.computed_at, data = excluded.data`
      ).bind(eventId, JSON.stringify(mmg.standings)).run();
    } catch (_mmgLb) { /* fail-soft: a bad config must never block score submits */ }
    return;
  }
  if (event.format === 'individual' || event.format === 'callaway') {   // v2.71.0 — per-player Callaway board; scramble math below untouched
    try {
      const cw = await computeCallawayStandings(env, event);
      await env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO leaderboard_cache (event_id, computed_at, data) VALUES (?, datetime('now'), ?)
         ON CONFLICT(event_id) DO UPDATE SET computed_at = excluded.computed_at, data = excluded.data`
      ).bind(event.id, JSON.stringify(cw)).run();
    } catch (_cwLb) { /* fail-soft: never block a score submit */ }
    return;
  }
  // ══ v2.99.0 — B-121: DO NOT compute a group board for scores that are not
  //    addressed to this event's groups. ═══════════════════════════════════════
  //
  // `scores.group_id` has NO foreign key and TWO writers:
  //   handleSubmitScores      (classic group code)   -> groups.id
  //   handleSubmitRoundScores (tee-time foursome code) -> tee_times.id
  // The scan below binds groups.id, so on a tee-time event it matches NOTHING
  // and this cache was being filled with holes_played:0 rows against fully
  // scored rounds. Measured s135: 13/13 multi-day rounds carry tee_times ids
  // and ZERO groups ids; the classic RBC scramble (event 23) is 180/180 the
  // other way. The two paths are cleanly disjoint.
  //
  // ⛔ THE FIX IS NOT TO REPAIR THE JOIN. A tee-time event's scoring unit is the
  // PLAYER or the X-of-N TEAM; the row shape below is scramble/RBC machinery
  // (team_handicap, scores_by_hole, tee_violation, ineligible_reason) and there
  // is no correct group-shaped answer to produce. Joining "correctly" would emit
  // a confident, plausible, WRONG best-ball total where there is now an obvious
  // zero — and a believable wrong board gets trusted where a broken one gets
  // reported. round_leaderboard_cache (v2.76.0) is round-scoped and format-aware
  // and is already what every correct surface reads.
  //
  // ⭐ THE TEST IS THE FACT ITSELF, not a proxy for it: "this event has scores,
  // and none of them are keyed to any of its groups." It does not ask about tee
  // times, so a future scoring path inherits the right behaviour for free.
  // ⭐ An event with NO scores yet falls through and computes as before — a
  // scramble board must still show its teams at holes_played:0 before play.
  // ⛔ Do not simplify this to "the event has tee_times": a scramble CAN carry
  // tee times and still score by group code (see the v2.73.0 note in
  // handleSubmitScores), and that board is correct and must keep working.
  let _b121Skip = false;
  try {
    const _b121 = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT (SELECT 1 FROM scores WHERE event_id = ? LIMIT 1) AS any_score,
              (SELECT 1 FROM scores s JOIN groups g ON g.id = s.group_id
                WHERE s.event_id = ? LIMIT 1) AS group_score`
    ).bind(eventId, eventId).first();
    _b121Skip = !!(_b121 && _b121.any_score && !_b121.group_score);
  } catch (_b121e) { _b121Skip = false; }   // fail-soft: on doubt, behave as pre-v2.99.0
  if (_b121Skip) {
    // Clear anything an earlier paste left behind, so the zeros do not sit in
    // D1 waiting for a future reader to trust them. Absent, not empty (FR-08).
    try { await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM leaderboard_cache WHERE event_id = ?').bind(eventId).run(); }
    catch (_b121d) { /* never block a score submit */ }
    return;
  }

  const groups = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name, start_hole, start_unit, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason FROM groups WHERE event_id = ?').bind(eventId).all(); // v2.79.0 — + start_unit (D34)
  const standings = [];
  for (const group of groups.results) {
    const scores = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT s.hole, s.gross_score FROM scores s JOIN players p ON p.id = s.player_id WHERE s.group_id = ? ORDER BY s.hole`
    ).bind(group.id).all();
    const byHole = {};
    for (const row of scores.results) {
      if (!byHole[row.hole] || row.gross_score < byHole[row.hole]) byHole[row.hole] = row.gross_score;
    }
    const holesPlayed = Object.keys(byHole).length;
    const total = Object.values(byHole).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    const team_handicap = group.team_handicap != null ? group.team_handicap : null;
    const net_score = (team_handicap != null && holesPlayed > 0)
      ? Math.round((total - team_handicap) * 10) / 10 : null;
    const ineligible_reason = group.ineligible_reason || null;
    let tee_violation = null;
    if (holesPlayed >= 18) {
      const teeRows = await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT player_name, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM tee_shots WHERE group_id = ? GROUP BY player_name'
      ).bind(group.id).all();
      const violators = teeRows.results.filter(r => r.cnt < 3).map(r => r.player_name);
      if (violators.length > 0) {
        tee_violation = 'Tee violation: ' + violators.join(', ') + (violators.length === 1 ? ' used < 3 drives' : ' used < 3 drives each');
      }
    }
    standings.push({ group_id: group.id, group_name: group.name, start_hole: group.start_hole, start_unit: group.start_unit ?? null, course_id: group.course_id || null, tee: group.tee || null, holes_played: holesPlayed, total, scores_by_hole: byHole, team_handicap, net_score, ineligible_reason, tee_violation }); // v2.79.0 — + start_unit (D34)
  }
  standings.sort((a, b) => b.holes_played !== a.holes_played ? b.holes_played - a.holes_played : a.total - b.total);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO leaderboard_cache (event_id, computed_at, data) VALUES (?, datetime('now'), ?)
     ON CONFLICT(event_id) DO UPDATE SET computed_at = excluded.computed_at, data = excluded.data`
  ).bind(eventId, JSON.stringify(standings)).run();
}

// ── v2.76.0 Q4b — PER-ROUND STANDINGS (ADDITIVE ONLY) ─────────────────────────
// One row per player (arm A) or per team card (arm B) per round, in the NEW
// round_leaderboard_cache table. recomputeLeaderboard above is deliberately
// untouched; this runs AFTER it, never instead of it. Fail-soft everywhere:
// the table may not exist yet (DDL ships separately) and a missing table must
// never break a score submit.
/* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   v2.89.0 · FR-01 — EXTRAORDINARY SCORE ("E.R.") RATER            (Bruce s90)

   Flags a round that is rarer than 1 in 100 for the player's Handicap Index.

     Score Differential      = (113 / Slope) x (Adjusted Gross - Course Rating - PCC)
     net Score Differential  = Score Differential - Handicap Index

   ⭐ PCC does not exist in this app. It is 0 and that is STATED, not invented.

   ODDS TABLE — the USGA Score Frequency/Probability Table. Transcribed s90,
   and VERIFIED CELL-BY-CELL against the USGA Rules of Handicapping source in
   s91: all 16 rows x 9 Handicap Index bands matched. A cell reads "1 in N".

   ⭐⭐ USGA Note: "There is an insufficient volume of scoring data for Handicap
   ranges above 40.0 to provide a statistically meaningful comparison." So an
   index above 39.9 is REFUSED, never extrapolated — the v2.66.0 rule (no
   honest source, no number) applied to rarity.

   ⭐⭐ "Rarer than 1:100" is NOT one net differential. A scratch player needs
   -4; a 36 needs -10. Anything that hardcodes one threshold is wrong, and
   wrong in the direction that flatters high handicaps. Derive from the band.
   ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

// Columns: <0.0 · 0.0-4.9 · 5.0-9.9 · 10.0-14.9 · 15.0-19.9 · 20.0-24.9 ·
//          25.0-29.9 · 30.0-34.9 · 35.0-39.9
const ER_TABLE = {
   3: [    1.8,     1.8,     1.9,    2.0,    2.1,    2.1,   2.2,   2.3,   2.4],
   2: [    2.4,     2.5,     2.5,    2.5,    2.6,    2.7,   2.8,   2.9,   2.9],
   1: [    3.6,     3.6,     3.6,    3.6,    3.5,    3.5,   3.5,   3.5,   3.5],
   0: [    5.9,     5.5,     5.3,    5.2,    5.1,    5.0,   5.0,   4.8,   4.6],
  '-1': [ 11.1,     9.7,     8.9,    8.2,    7.7,    7.4,   7.1,   6.6,   6.0],
  '-2': [ 23.0,    18.5,    16.1,   13.8,   12.4,   11.3,  10.5,   9.4,   8.2],
  '-3': [ 53.5,    38.5,    31.2,   24.7,   21.1,   18.0,  16.0,  13.7,  11.3],
  '-4': [139.8,    87.1,    64.7,   46.9,   37.3,   29.8,  25.1,  20.4,  16.1],
  '-5': [376.5,   210.9,   140.1,   93.3,   68.6,   51.2,  40.4,  31.0,  23.1],
  '-6': [986.8,   535.7,   317.4,  191.5,  130.5,   89.9,  66.5,  47.9,  33.6],
  '-7': [2247.9, 1282.2,   704.6,  391.4,  251.2,  161.8, 110.5,  74.5,  48.8],
  '-8': [6362.8, 3361.9,  1580.6,  743.0,  451.2,  265.7, 170.1, 109.3,  67.1],
  '-9': [13861.8, 8202.6, 3609.9, 1362.7,  828.0,  449.0, 264.2, 159.8,  94.0],
  '-10': [22831.3, 15812.0, 7186.6, 2204.6, 1431.9, 715.9, 398.0, 226.6, 127.7],
  '-11': [32344.3, 28561.4, 13166.4, 3153.5, 2202.2, 1061.6, 559.8, 299.6, 164.5],
  '-12': [58219.8, 44968.9, 21133.7, 4085.7, 3157.2, 1476.7, 748.1, 380.5, 205.1],
};
const ER_MIN_ODDS = 100;       // Bruce s90: "rarer than 1:100"
const ER_ROW_MIN  = -12;       // the table's last row; anything rarer clamps here

// Handicap Index -> column. -1 = REFUSE (no band, therefore no claim).
function erBandIdx(index) {
  // ⭐⭐ A null / blank / absent Handicap Index must be REFUSED, never coerced.
  //    Number(null) is 0, and 0 is a REAL band (scratch) — so a player with no
  //    index would silently be rated as a scratch. That is exactly the s90
  //    lesson: a wrong number nobody reports. No index, no rarity claim.
  if (index === null || index === undefined || index === '') return -1;
  const i = Number(index);
  if (!isFinite(i)) return -1;
  if (!(i <= 39.9)) return -1;                       // USGA: nothing above 40.0
  if (i < 0) return 0;                               // "Less Than 0.0"
  const b = Math.floor(i / 5) + 1;
  return b > 8 ? 8 : b;
}

/* Adjusted Gross Score — WHS net double bogey (Rule 3.1): the maximum score
   that counts on a hole is par + 2 + the handicap strokes the player receives
   there. ⭐ Strokes come off the COURSE Handicap, not the allowance-adjusted
   playing handicap — the adjustment is a scoring-record rule, not a
   competition rule. Returns null when the round cannot be adjusted honestly:
   a missing hole or a missing par means NO claim, never a guess. */
function erAdjustedGross(holes, pars, si, courseHcp) {
  if (!holes || !pars) return null;
  let tot = 0;
  for (let h = 1; h <= 18; h++) {
    const raw = Number(holes[h]);
    if (!(raw > 0)) return null;                     // needs a full 18
    const par = Number(pars[h - 1]);
    if (!(par > 0)) return null;                     // no honest par, no claim
    const cap = par + 2 + hcpStrokesForHole(si ? si[h - 1] : h, courseHcp);
    tot += Math.min(raw, cap);
  }
  return tot;
}

function erLabel(odds) {
  const n = odds >= 1000 ? Math.round(odds / 100) * 100
          : odds >= 100  ? Math.round(odds / 10) * 10
          :                Math.round(odds);
  return '1:' + String(n).replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
}

/* Rate one player's round. Returns null whenever any input is missing or the
   format makes the question meaningless — a null here is the honest answer. */
function erRate(o) {
  const fmt = String(o.format || '').toLowerCase();
  // ⭐ Callaway exists BECAUSE nobody has a Handicap Index. No index, no net
  //   differential, no rarity. Bruce s90: "we don't need E.R. for callaway
  //   events, does not apply." Gate on the FORMAT, never on a value (B-35).
  if (fmt.indexOf('callaway') !== -1) return null;
  if (String(o.eventFormat || '').toLowerCase().indexOf('callaway') !== -1) return null;
  // ⭐ A neutral tee means slope 113 / rating = par were INVENTED to avoid
  //   inventing difficulty (v2.88.0). A differential off invented numbers is a
  //   wrong rarity claim, so a neutral tee earns no mark.
  if (o.teeSource !== 'course_tee') return null;
  const band = erBandIdx(o.index);
  if (band < 0) return null;
  const slope = Number(o.slope), rating = Number(o.rating);
  if (!(slope > 0) || !(rating > 0)) return null;
  const adj = erAdjustedGross(o.holes, o.pars, o.si, o.courseHcp);
  if (adj == null) return null;
  const diff    = (113 / slope) * (adj - rating);          // PCC = 0 (absent)
  const netDiff = diff - Number(o.index);
  /* ⭐⭐ ROUNDING. The table is integer-indexed; real rounds are not. Report the
     LEAST impressive whole row the score actually achieves — Math.ceil. On the
     negative side (every 1:100 threshold) that is "floor toward zero", so the
     rater can only ever UNDER-claim; on the positive side it stops a +2.7 from
     being sold as a +2. One rule, conservative in both directions. */
  let row = Math.ceil(netDiff);
  if (row > 3) return null;                                // not close to rare
  if (row < ER_ROW_MIN) row = ER_ROW_MIN;                  // clamp = under-claim
  const odds = ER_TABLE[String(row)][band];
  if (!(odds > 0)) return null;
  return {
    odds,
    label: erLabel(odds),
    extraordinary: odds >= ER_MIN_ODDS,
    net_diff: Math.round(netDiff * 10) / 10,
    row,
    band,
    differential: Math.round(diff * 10) / 10,
    adjusted_gross: adj,
    gross: Number(o.gross) || null,
    index: Number(o.index),
    pcc: 0,
    source: 'usga_score_frequency',
  };
}

async function recomputeRoundLeaderboard(env, roundId) {
  try {
    const round = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id, r.event_id, r.format, r.course_id, r.tee,
              e.course_id AS event_course_id, e.tee AS event_tee, e.config AS event_config,
              e.format AS event_format
         FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ?`
    ).bind(roundId).first();
    if (!round) return;
    // Round's own course/tee first, event's as fallback — the same precedence
    // handleFinalizeHandicaps applies to groups.
    const courseId = round.course_id || round.event_course_id || null;
    const tee      = round.tee      || round.event_tee       || null;
    const teeData  = await resolveTee(env, courseId, tee);            // v2.88.0 — B-70: D1, never a baked-in rating
    const cfg  = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(round.event_config || '{}'); } catch (_) { return {}; } })();
    let pars = eventHolePars(cfg);     // null when the event has no per-hole pars
    let si   = eventHoleSI(cfg);       // null -> SI falls back to hole order
    let parSource = pars ? 'event_config' : 'none';
    // v2.86.0 — before giving up on points, ask D1 for the course's own pars.
    // ⭐ Event config still WINS: an organizer who set per-hole pars meant them.
    if (!pars) {
      const _cp = await courseHolePars(env, courseId, tee);
      if (_cp.pars) { pars = _cp.pars; if (!si) si = _cp.si; parSource = _cp.source; }
    }
    const fmt  = String(round.format || '');
    const isTeam = fmt.indexOf('scramble') !== -1;   // arm B; everything else arm A
    const wantsPts = fmt.indexOf('stableford') !== -1;
    const entries = [];
    if (!isTeam) {
      // ARM A — one row per PLAYER.
      const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT s.player_id, p.name, p.handicap, s.hole, s.gross_score
           FROM scores s JOIN players p ON p.id = s.player_id
          WHERE s.round_id = ? AND s.gross_score > 0 ORDER BY s.player_id, s.hole`
      ).bind(roundId).all();
      const byPlayer = new Map();
      for (const r of results) {
        if (!byPlayer.has(r.player_id)) byPlayer.set(r.player_id, { name: r.name, handicap: r.handicap, holes: {} });
        byPlayer.get(r.player_id).holes[r.hole] = r.gross_score;
      }
      for (const [pid, rec] of byPlayer) {
        const holeNums = Object.keys(rec.holes).map(Number);
        const gross = holeNums.reduce((t, h) => t + rec.holes[h], 0);
        const courseHcp = calcCourseHandicapFull(Number(rec.handicap) || 0, teeData.slope, teeData.rating, teeData.par);
        const playHcp   = tbApplyAllowance(courseHcp, cfg, 0);            // v2.78.0 — D1: a[0] = the individual case
        const net = holeNums.length ? Math.round((gross - playHcp) * 10) / 10 : null;
        let points = null;
        if (wantsPts && pars) {
          points = 0;
          for (const h of holeNums) {
            points += leagueHolePts(rec.holes[h], null, pars[h - 1], si ? si[h - 1] : h, playHcp); // v2.78.0 — D1
          }
        }
        // v2.89.0 — FR-01: rate the round for rarity. null whenever any input
        // is missing or the format makes the question meaningless.
        const er = erRate({
          index: rec.handicap, slope: teeData.slope, rating: teeData.rating,
          teeSource: teeData.tee_source, holes: rec.holes, pars, si,
          courseHcp, gross, format: fmt, eventFormat: round.event_format,
        });
        entries.push({
          entry_key: 'p:' + pid, entry_type: 'player', name: rec.name,
          gross, net, points, holes_played: holeNums.length,
          data: { player_id: pid, handicap: rec.handicap, course_handicap: courseHcp,
                  playing_handicap: playHcp,                               // v2.78.0 — D1 (additive)
                  scores_by_hole: rec.holes, par_source: parSource,   // v2.86.0 — 'event_config' | 'course_tee' | 'course' | 'none'
                  tee_source: teeData.tee_source, rating: teeData.rating, slope: teeData.slope,  // v2.88.0 — B-70: 'course_tee' | 'neutral'
                  er,                                                 // v2.89.0 — FR-01: null | { odds, label, extraordinary, ... }
                  course_id: courseId, tee, format: fmt },
        });
      }
    } else {
      // ARM B — one row per TEAM CARD, keyed by scores.group_id. Min-per-hole
      // collapse, exactly the shape recomputeLeaderboard's default path uses.
      // The LEFT JOIN carries the event guard (g.event_id = round.event_id) —
      // scores.group_id is polymorphic (groups.id OR tee_times.id, see v2.73.0)
      // so a groups row is decoration when it resolves, never assumed.
      const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT s.group_id, s.hole, s.gross_score, g.name AS group_name, g.team_handicap
           FROM scores s LEFT JOIN groups g ON g.id = s.group_id AND g.event_id = ?
          WHERE s.round_id = ? AND s.gross_score > 0 ORDER BY s.group_id, s.hole`
      ).bind(round.event_id, roundId).all();
      const byGroup = new Map();
      for (const r of results) {
        if (!byGroup.has(r.group_id)) byGroup.set(r.group_id, { name: r.group_name, team_handicap: r.team_handicap, holes: {} });
        const g = byGroup.get(r.group_id);
        if (!g.holes[r.hole] || r.gross_score < g.holes[r.hole]) g.holes[r.hole] = r.gross_score;
      }
      for (const [gid, rec] of byGroup) {
        const holeNums = Object.keys(rec.holes).map(Number);
        const gross = holeNums.reduce((t, h) => t + rec.holes[h], 0);
        const net = (rec.team_handicap != null && holeNums.length)
          ? Math.round((gross - rec.team_handicap) * 10) / 10 : null;
        // points stays NULL on arm B: the worker has no server-side team
        // stableford to reuse (the event cache never computed one either).
        entries.push({
          entry_key: 'g:' + gid, entry_type: 'group',
          name: rec.name || ('Card ' + gid),
          gross, net, points: null, holes_played: holeNums.length,
          data: { group_id: gid, team_handicap: rec.team_handicap,
                  scores_by_hole: rec.holes, par_source: parSource,   // v2.86.0 — 'event_config' | 'course_tee' | 'course' | 'none'
                  tee_source: teeData.tee_source, rating: teeData.rating, slope: teeData.slope,  // v2.88.0 — B-70: 'course_tee' | 'neutral'
                  course_id: courseId, tee, format: fmt },
        });
      }
    }
    entries.sort((a, b) => {
      if (b.holes_played !== a.holes_played) return b.holes_played - a.holes_played;
      if (a.points != null && b.points != null && b.points !== a.points) return b.points - a.points;
      return a.gross - b.gross;
    });
    const stmts = [env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM round_leaderboard_cache WHERE round_id = ?').bind(roundId)];
    for (const e2 of entries) {
      stmts.push(env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO round_leaderboard_cache
           (round_id, entry_key, entry_type, name, gross, net, points, holes_played, data, updated_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))`
      ).bind(roundId, e2.entry_key, e2.entry_type, e2.name, e2.gross, e2.net, e2.points, e2.holes_played, JSON.stringify(e2.data)));
    }
    await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  } catch (e) {
    // FAIL-SOFT BY CONTRACT — missing table, bad config, anything: log and move
    // on. A score submit must never bounce off this cache.
    console.error('recomputeRoundLeaderboard failed (fail-soft, round ' + roundId + '):', e);
  }
}

// ── Auto-settle bets races when scoring completes ─────────────────────────────
async function maybeAutoSettleBetsRace(env, eventId, roundId, tripId, tripRoundId) {
  try {
    // Find open race(s) linked to this event/round or trip/round
    let races = [];
    if (eventId && roundId) {
      // v2.49.0 — TWO GUARDS, BOTH LOAD-BEARING ON A MULTI-RACE EVENT.
      //
      // 1. `auto_settle = 1`. This function used to take every open round race
      //    on the event. Event 49 carries FIVE team races all built from the
      //    same 10 group names, so the first round to finish would have written
      //    one set of placements into all five at once — two of them for rounds
      //    not yet played, one of them the overall — off a whole-event group
      //    cache, silently, on live money. Nothing sets the flag today, so
      //    placement is manual and deliberate. This is the guard, not a tidy-up.
      // 2. `round_id IS NULL OR round_id = ?`. A race pinned to a round settles
      //    only when THAT round completes. NULL keeps the single-day behaviour
      //    (race 5 on event 53) working unchanged.
      //
      // Even with both, the standings below are still the whole-event GROUP
      // leaderboard_cache, which has no round dimension and cannot rank players.
      // That is why the flag stays off: the data to settle a multi_day round
      // correctly does not exist yet. Fixing it is a separate piece of work.
      const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT * FROM bets_races
          WHERE event_id = ? AND status = 'open' AND auto_settle = 1
            AND (race_type = 'round' OR race_type = 'overall')
            AND (round_id IS NULL OR round_id = ?)`
      ).bind(eventId, roundId).all();
      races = results;
    } else if (tripId && tripRoundId) {
      const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT * FROM bets_races WHERE trip_id = ? AND status = 'open'`
      ).bind(tripId).all();
      races = results;
    }
    for (const race of races) {
      // Only auto-settle round races when all groups in round have 18 holes
      if (race.race_type !== 'round') continue;
      let standings = [];
      if (eventId && roundId) {
        // Get leaderboard from cache
        const cache = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT data FROM leaderboard_cache WHERE event_id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
        if (!cache) continue;
        standings = JSON.parse(cache.data);
        // Only settle if top 3 groups have 18 holes
        if (!standings[0] || standings[0].holes_played < 18) continue;
      } else if (tripId && tripRoundId) {
        // Trip round standings by stableford pts
        const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
          `SELECT tp.id AS trip_player_id, r.name,
                  SUM(ts.stableford_pts) AS total_pts
           FROM trip_scores ts
           JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ts.trip_player_id
           JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
           WHERE ts.trip_round_id = ?
           GROUP BY tp.id ORDER BY total_pts DESC`
        ).bind(tripRoundId).all();
        if (!players || players.length < 3) continue;
        standings = players.map(p => ({ name: p.name, trip_player_id: p.trip_player_id }));
      }
      // Map top 3 to bets_teams by name match
      const { results: betsTeams } = await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT * FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ?'
      ).bind(race.id).all();
      const findTeam = (name) => betsTeams.find(t => t.name.toLowerCase() === (name || '').toLowerCase());
      const p1 = findTeam(standings[0]?.group_name || standings[0]?.name);
      const p2 = findTeam(standings[1]?.group_name || standings[1]?.name);
      const p3 = findTeam(standings[2]?.group_name || standings[2]?.name);
      if (!p1 || !p2 || !p3) continue;
      // Upsert places
      await env.DB.batch([
        env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO bets_places (race_id, position, team_id, settled_at) VALUES (?, 1, ?, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(race_id, position) DO UPDATE SET team_id=excluded.team_id, settled_at=excluded.settled_at`).bind(race.id, p1.id),
        env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO bets_places (race_id, position, team_id, settled_at) VALUES (?, 2, ?, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(race_id, position) DO UPDATE SET team_id=excluded.team_id, settled_at=excluded.settled_at`).bind(race.id, p2.id),
        env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO bets_places (race_id, position, team_id, settled_at) VALUES (?, 3, ?, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(race_id, position) DO UPDATE SET team_id=excluded.team_id, settled_at=excluded.settled_at`).bind(race.id, p3.id),
      ]);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('maybeAutoSettleBetsRace error:', e);
  }
}

export default {
  async fetch(request, env) {
    const url = new URL(request.url);
    const method = request.method.toUpperCase();
    const path = url.pathname.replace(/\/$/, '');
    const origin = request.headers.get('Origin') || '';

    if (method === 'OPTIONS') return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers: corsHeaders(origin) });

    try {
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/auth/login')  return await handleLogin(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/auth/logout') return await handleLogout(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET'  && path === '/api/auth/me')     return await handleMe(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/auth/forgot') return await handleForgotPassword(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/events/watch') return await handleWatch(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/events') return await handleCreateEvent(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET'  && path === '/api/events') return await handleListEvents(request, env, origin);
      const eventMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)$/);
      if (eventMatch) {
        const id = Number(eventMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')    return await handleGetEvent(request, env, origin, id);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handleUpdateEvent(request, env, origin, id);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteEvent(request, env, origin, id);
      }
      const roundScoresMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/scores$/);
      if (roundScoresMatch && method === 'GET') {
        return await handleGetRoundScores(request, env, origin, Number(roundScoresMatch[1]), Number(roundScoresMatch[2]));
      }
      const roundLbMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/leaderboard$/);   // v2.76.0
      if (roundLbMatch && method === 'GET') {
        return await handleRoundLeaderboard(request, env, origin, Number(roundLbMatch[1]), Number(roundLbMatch[2]));
      }
      const groupsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/groups$/);
      if (groupsMatch) {
        const eventId = Number(groupsMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleCreateGroup(request, env, origin, eventId);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleListGroupsWithRoster(request, env, origin, eventId);
      }
      const groupMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/groups\/(\d+)$/);
      if (groupMatch) {
        const id = Number(groupMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handleUpdateGroup(request, env, origin, id);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteGroup(request, env, origin, id);
      }
      const grpRosterMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/groups\/(\d+)\/roster$/);
      if (grpRosterMatch) {
        const gid = Number(grpRosterMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleGroupRosterAdd(request, env, origin, gid);
      }
      const grpRosterDelMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/groups\/(\d+)\/roster\/(\d+)$/);
      if (grpRosterDelMatch) {
        const gid = Number(grpRosterDelMatch[1]);
        const rid = Number(grpRosterDelMatch[2]);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleGroupRosterRemove(request, env, origin, gid, rid);
      }
      const captainMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/groups\/(\d+)\/captain$/);
      if (captainMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleSetGroupCaptain(request, env, origin, Number(captainMatch[1]));
      const joinMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/join\/([A-Z0-9]{6})$/i);
      if (joinMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleJoinCode(request, env, origin, joinMatch[1].toUpperCase());
      const lbMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/leaderboard$/);
      if (lbMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleLeaderboard(request, env, origin, Number(lbMatch[1]));
      const mmgLbMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/mmg-leaderboard$/);          // v2.63.0
      if (mmgLbMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleMmgLeaderboard(request, env, origin, Number(mmgLbMatch[1]));
      const mmgImportMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/mmg-import$/);           // v2.63.0
      if (mmgImportMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleMmgImport(request, env, origin, Number(mmgImportMatch[1]));
      const gamesMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/games$/);                  // v2.66.0
      if (gamesMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleEventGames(request, env, origin, Number(gamesMatch[1]));
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/scores') return await handleSubmitScores(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/scores/round') return await handleSubmitRoundScores(request, env, origin);
      const scoresGroupMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/scores\/group\/(\d+)$/);
      if (scoresGroupMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleGetGroupScores(request, env, origin, Number(scoresGroupMatch[1]));
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/tee-shots') return await handleSubmitTeeShot(request, env, origin);
      const teeShotGroupMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/tee-shots\/group\/(\d+)$/);
      if (teeShotGroupMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleGetGroupTeeShots(request, env, origin, Number(teeShotGroupMatch[1]));
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/roster/import') return await handleRosterImport(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET'  && path === '/api/roster')        return await handleRosterList(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/roster')        return await handleRosterUpsert(request, env, origin);
      const rosterIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/roster\/(\d+)$/);
      if (rosterIdMatch) {
        const rid = Number(rosterIdMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handleRosterUpdate(request, env, origin, rid);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleRosterDelete(request, env, origin, rid);
      }
      const rosterHistMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/roster\/(\d+)\/history$/);
      if (rosterHistMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleRosterHistory(request, env, origin, Number(rosterHistMatch[1]));
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/auth/passkey/register') return await handlePasskeyRegister(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/auth/passkey/login')    return await handlePasskeyLogin(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/draw/parse') return await handleDrawParse(request, env, origin);
      const finalizeMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/finalize-handicaps$/);
      if (finalizeMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleFinalizeHandicaps(request, env, origin, Number(finalizeMatch[1]));
      const roundsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/rounds$/);
      if (roundsMatch) {
        const eventId = Number(roundsMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleListRounds(request, env, origin, eventId);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleCreateRound(request, env, origin, eventId);
      }
      const roundMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/rounds\/(\d+)$/);
      if (roundMatch) {
        const id = Number(roundMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handleUpdateRound(request, env, origin, id);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteRound(request, env, origin, id);
      }
      const roundScoresDelMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/rounds\/(\d+)\/scores$/);
      if (roundScoresDelMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteRoundScores(request, env, origin, Number(roundScoresDelMatch[1])); // v2.79.0 — D41
      const teeTimesMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/rounds\/(\d+)\/tee-times$/);
      if (teeTimesMatch) {
        const roundId = Number(teeTimesMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')   return await handleGetTeeTimes(request, env, origin, roundId);
        if (method === 'POST')  return await handleSaveTeeTimes(request, env, origin, roundId);
        if (method === 'PATCH') return await handlePatchTeeTimes(request, env, origin, roundId);
      }
      const teeTimeDelMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/rounds\/(\d+)\/tee-times\/clear$/);
      if (teeTimeDelMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleClearTeeTimes(request, env, origin, Number(teeTimeDelMatch[1]));
      const joinRoundMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/join-round\/([A-Z0-9]{6})$/i);
      if (joinRoundMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleJoinRound(request, env, origin, joinRoundMatch[1].toUpperCase());
      // ⭐ FR-28 (v3.00.0) — the trip twin of /api/join-round/:code.
      // Pards Lite types a 6-digit foursome code and had NOTHING to resolve it
      // with on a trip, so every trip code read "invalid code". The score rail
      // (POST /api/trips/:id/rounds/:rid/scores) has existed since v2.92.0 and
      // needs trip_id + round_id + trip_player_id — none of which a phone can
      // know from the code alone. This route is the only missing piece.
      const joinTripMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/join-trip\/([A-Z0-9]{6})$/i);
      if (joinTripMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleJoinTrip(request, env, origin, joinTripMatch[1].toUpperCase());
      const recomputeMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/recompute$/);
      if (recomputeMatch && method === 'POST') {
        const account = await resolveSession(env, request);
        if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
        await recomputeLeaderboard(env, Number(recomputeMatch[1]));
        return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
      }
      const dupMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/(events|trips|leagues)\/(\d+)\/duplicate$/);
      if (dupMatch && method === 'POST') {
        const dupId = Number(dupMatch[2]);
        if (dupMatch[1] === 'events')  return await handleDuplicateEvent(request, env, origin, dupId);
        if (dupMatch[1] === 'trips')   return await handleDuplicateTrip(request, env, origin, dupId);
        if (dupMatch[1] === 'leagues') return await handleDuplicateLeague(request, env, origin, dupId);
      }
      if (path === '/api/ping' && method === 'GET') return json({ ok: true, service: 'pards-tour-api', version: '3.00.0' }, 200, origin);

      // ── Course library ────────────────────────────────────────
      if (method === 'GET' && path === '/api/courses') return await handleListCourses(request, env, origin);
      const courseIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/courses\/([^/]+)$/);
      if (courseIdMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleGetCourse(request, env, origin, courseIdMatch[1]);

      // ── Trip routes ───────────────────────────────────────────
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/trips')      return await handleCreateTrip(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET'  && path === '/api/trips')      return await handleListTrips(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET' && path.startsWith('/api/trips/by-viewer-pin/')) return await handleTripByViewerPin(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET' && path.startsWith('/api/trips/watch/')) return await handleTripWatchByCode(request, env, origin); // v2.35.0
      const tripMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)$/);
      if (tripMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')    return await handleGetTrip(request, env, origin, tid);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handleUpdateTrip(request, env, origin, tid);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteTrip(request, env, origin, tid);
      }
      const tripConfigMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/config$/);
      if (tripConfigMatch && method === 'PATCH') return await handlePatchTripConfig(request, env, origin, Number(tripConfigMatch[1])); // v2.79.0 — D13/D25e trips
      const tripPlayersMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/players$/);
      if (tripPlayersMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripPlayersMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')    return await handleListTripPlayers(request, env, origin, tid);
        if (method === 'POST')   return await handleAddTripPlayer(request, env, origin, tid);
      }
      const tripPlayerMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/players\/(\d+)$/);
      if (tripPlayerMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleRemoveTripPlayer(request, env, origin, Number(tripPlayerMatch[1]), Number(tripPlayerMatch[2]));
      const tripRoundsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/rounds$/);
      if (tripRoundsMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripRoundsMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleListTripRounds(request, env, origin, tid);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleCreateTripRound(request, env, origin, tid);
      }
      const tripRoundMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)$/);
      if (tripRoundMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripRoundMatch[1]), rid = Number(tripRoundMatch[2]);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handleUpdateTripRound(request, env, origin, tid, rid);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteTripRound(request, env, origin, tid, rid);
      }
      const tripTeeTimesMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/tee-times$/);
      if (tripTeeTimesMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripTeeTimesMatch[1]), rid = Number(tripTeeTimesMatch[2]);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleGetTripTeeTimes(request, env, origin, tid, rid);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleSaveTripTeeTimes(request, env, origin, tid, rid);
      }
      const tripLbMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/leaderboard$/);
      if (tripLbMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleTripLeaderboard(request, env, origin, Number(tripLbMatch[1]));
      // v2.92.0 — D84: trip_events link routes (game days). Link/unlink only —
      // the ＋Game Day client chain creates the event first, then links it here.
      const tripEventsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/events$/);
      if (tripEventsMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleLinkTripEvent(request, env, origin, Number(tripEventsMatch[1]));
      const tripEventIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/events\/(\d+)$/);
      if (tripEventIdMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleUnlinkTripEvent(request, env, origin, Number(tripEventIdMatch[1]), Number(tripEventIdMatch[2]));
      // v2.92.0 — FR-25: casual-round side-game money → the trip ledger.
      const tripRoundMoneyMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/money$/);
      if (tripRoundMoneyMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleSubmitTripRoundMoney(request, env, origin, Number(tripRoundMoneyMatch[1]), Number(tripRoundMoneyMatch[2]));
      // v2.93.0 — FR-26 phase 1: game-day (event) round side-game money → the trip ledger.
      // v2.98.0 — FR-42 §E: the same path now READS as well as writes, so an
      // event-scoped surface (the Game Results report) can show a round's side
      // games without reaching into trip state it has no honest claim on.
      const eventRoundMoneyMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/events\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/money$/);
      if (eventRoundMoneyMatch) {
        const _emEid = Number(eventRoundMoneyMatch[1]), _emRid = Number(eventRoundMoneyMatch[2]);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleGetEventRoundMoney(request, env, origin, _emEid, _emRid);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleSubmitEventRoundMoney(request, env, origin, _emEid, _emRid);
      }
      const tripNassauMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/nassau$/);
      if (tripNassauMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripNassauMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleListTripNassau(request, env, origin, tid);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleUpsertTripNassau(request, env, origin, tid);
      }
      const tripScoresMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/scores$/);
      if (tripScoresMatch) {
        const tid = Number(tripScoresMatch[1]), rid = Number(tripScoresMatch[2]);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleGetTripRoundScores(request, env, origin, tid, rid);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleSubmitTripScores(request, env, origin, tid, rid);
      }
      const tripRefreshHcpMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/trips\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/refresh-handicaps$/); // v2.41.0
      if (tripRefreshHcpMatch && method === 'POST') {
        return await handleRefreshTripHandicaps(request, env, origin, Number(tripRefreshHcpMatch[1]), Number(tripRefreshHcpMatch[2]));
      }
      const joinTripMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/join-trip\/([A-Z0-9]{6})$/i);
      if (joinTripMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleJoinTrip(request, env, origin, joinTripMatch[1].toUpperCase());

      // ── League routes (v2.29.0) ───────────────────────────────
      // v2.36.0 — watch/season FIRST: '/api/leagues/watch/:code' can never match the
      // numeric \d+ patterns below, but keeping public routes ahead of authed ones
      // matches the trip block and is robust if codes ever go alphanumeric.
      if (method === 'GET' && path.startsWith('/api/leagues/watch/')) return await handleLeagueWatchByCode(request, env, origin);
      const leagueSeasonMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/season$/);
      if (leagueSeasonMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleLeagueSeason(request, env, origin, Number(leagueSeasonMatch[1]));
      const leagueBarnesMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/barnes$/);                 // v2.67.0
      if (leagueBarnesMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleLeagueBarnes(request, env, origin, Number(leagueBarnesMatch[1]));
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/leagues') return await handleCreateLeague(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET'  && path === '/api/leagues') return await handleListLeagues(request, env, origin);
      const leagueIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)$/);
      if (leagueIdMatch) {
        const lid = Number(leagueIdMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')    return await handleGetLeague(request, env, origin, lid);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handlePatchLeague(request, env, origin, lid);   // v2.59.0
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteLeague(request, env, origin, lid);
      }
      const leagueMembersMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/members$/);
      if (leagueMembersMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleAddLeagueMember(request, env, origin, Number(leagueMembersMatch[1]));
      const leagueMemberIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/members\/(\d+)$/);
      if (leagueMemberIdMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleRemoveLeagueMember(request, env, origin, Number(leagueMemberIdMatch[1]), Number(leagueMemberIdMatch[2]));
      const leagueRoundsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/rounds$/);
      if (leagueRoundsMatch) {
        const lid = Number(leagueRoundsMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'POST') return await handleCreateLeagueRound(request, env, origin, lid);
        if (method === 'GET')  return await handleListLeagueRounds(request, env, origin, lid);
      }
      const leagueRoundIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)$/);
      if (leagueRoundIdMatch) {
        const lid = Number(leagueRoundIdMatch[1]), lrid = Number(leagueRoundIdMatch[2]);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteLeagueRound(request, env, origin, lid, lrid);
        if (method === 'PATCH')  return await handlePatchLeagueRound(request, env, origin, lid, lrid);
      }
      // v2.68.0 — config writers. SEPARATE routes rather than a `config` key on
      // the two PATCH handlers above, for two reasons:
      //   1. handlePatchLeague hard-requires `name` (it is the rename path).
      //   2. these MERGE at the top level. leagues.config carries barnes AND
      //      game_pars AND game_si together; a whole-object replace from a
      //      setup screen that only knows about barnes would wipe the 18-hole
      //      pars/SI and send handleLeagueBarnes straight to
      //      per_hole_pars_missing. Same read-modify-write trap the v2.58.0
      //      skins comment warns about, one table over.
      // NOTE: these must sit ABOVE leagueRoundIdMatch-style patterns only if
      // they could collide; /config has a literal suffix so ordering is safe.
      const leagueConfigMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/config$/);
      if (leagueConfigMatch && method === 'PATCH') return await handlePatchLeagueConfig(request, env, origin, Number(leagueConfigMatch[1]));
      const leagueRoundConfigMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/config$/);
      if (leagueRoundConfigMatch && method === 'PATCH') return await handlePatchLeagueRoundConfig(request, env, origin, Number(leagueRoundConfigMatch[1]), Number(leagueRoundConfigMatch[2]));
      const leagueRoundLbMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/rounds\/(\d+)\/leaderboard$/);
      if (leagueRoundLbMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleLeagueRoundLeaderboard(request, env, origin, Number(leagueRoundLbMatch[1]), Number(leagueRoundLbMatch[2]));
      // v2.91.0 — D82: league_events link routes (team nights). Link/unlink only —
      // the event itself is never created or deleted here.
      const leagueEventsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/events$/);
      if (leagueEventsMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleLinkLeagueEvent(request, env, origin, Number(leagueEventsMatch[1]));
      const leagueEventIdMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/leagues\/(\d+)\/events\/(\d+)$/);
      if (leagueEventIdMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleUnlinkLeagueEvent(request, env, origin, Number(leagueEventIdMatch[1]), Number(leagueEventIdMatch[2]));

      // ── Pards Bets routes (v2.30.0) ──────────────────────────
      if (method === 'POST' && path === '/api/bets/races') return await handleCreateBetsRace(request, env, origin);
      if (method === 'GET'  && path === '/api/bets/races') return await handleListBetsRaces(request, env, origin);
      const betsRaceMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)$/);
      if (betsRaceMatch) {
        const rid = Number(betsRaceMatch[1]);
        if (method === 'GET')    return await handleGetBetsRace(request, env, origin, rid);
        if (method === 'DELETE') return await handleDeleteBetsRace(request, env, origin, rid);
      }
      const betsAutoPopMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)\/auto-populate$/);
      if (betsAutoPopMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleBetsAutoPopulate(request, env, origin, Number(betsAutoPopMatch[1]));
      const betsBettorsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)\/bettors$/);
      if (betsBettorsMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleBetsAddBettor(request, env, origin, Number(betsBettorsMatch[1])); // v2.79.0 — B-29
      const betsTokenMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)\/token$/);
      if (betsTokenMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleBetsMintRaceToken(request, env, origin, Number(betsTokenMatch[1]));
      const betsWagersMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)\/wagers$/);
      if (betsWagersMatch && method === 'POST') return await handleBetsPlaceWagers(request, env, origin, Number(betsWagersMatch[1]));
      if (betsWagersMatch && method === 'DELETE') return await handleBetsVoidTicket(request, env, origin, Number(betsWagersMatch[1]));
      const betsPlacesMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)\/places$/);
      if (betsPlacesMatch && method === 'PATCH') return await handleBetsSetPlaces(request, env, origin, Number(betsPlacesMatch[1]));
      const betsResultsMatch = path.match(/^\/api\/bets\/races\/(\d+)\/results$/);
      if (betsResultsMatch && method === 'GET') return await handleBetsResults(request, env, origin, Number(betsResultsMatch[1]));

      return err('Not found', 404, origin);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
      return err('Internal server error', 500, origin);
    }
  },
};

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AUTH HANDLERS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function handleLogin(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.email || !body?.password) return err('email and password required', 400, origin);
  const account = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, email, name, club, password_hash FROM accounts WHERE email = ?'
  ).bind(body.email.trim().toLowerCase()).first();
  if (!account) return err('Invalid email or password', 401, origin);
  if (!await verifyPassword(body.password, account.password_hash)) return err('Invalid email or password', 401, origin);
  const token = await createSession(env, account.id);
  return json({ token, organizer: { id: account.id, email: account.email, name: account.name, club: account.club } }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleLogout(req, env, origin) {
  await deleteSession(env, req);
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleMe(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  return json({ organizer: account }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleForgotPassword(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.email) return err('email required', 400, origin);
  const email = body.email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const account = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name FROM accounts WHERE email = ?').bind(email).first();
  console.log(`Password reset requested for: ${email} — account ${account ? 'found' : 'not found'}`);
  return json({ ok: true, message: 'If that email is registered, a reset link will be sent.' }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// EVENT HANDLERS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function handleWatch(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.event_code) return err('event_code required', 400, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT id, name, course_name, course_id, date, format, status, event_code, viewer_pin, config
     FROM events WHERE event_code = ?`
  ).bind(body.event_code.toUpperCase()).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const cache = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT data, computed_at FROM leaderboard_cache WHERE event_id = ?').bind(event.id).first();
  // v2.85.0 — B-64: the current round's setup rides the watch payload so
  // tickers can say 'after N holes' (shotgun) and go 'FINAL' with authority.
  // ⭐⭐ v2.90.0 — B-82 (worker half): the pick is now ACTIVE → LOCKED → DRAFT,
  // then highest round_number. The old B-32 rule ("prefer unlocked, then
  // highest") let an empty DRAFT outrank the watched round twice on live data:
  // event 95 (round 1 ACTIVE, 2-3 draft → reported round 3, Lite read an empty
  // message drawer) and event 96 (round 79 LOCKED with the money on it, round
  // 80 an empty draft → viewers landed on the blank round). A draft has
  // nothing to watch, so it ranks LAST for a VIEWER.
  // ⭐ THE SCORER PATHS ARE DELIBERATELY NOT CHANGED (handleJoinCode /
  // handleSubmitScores): a scorer must be steered to a PLAYABLE round and
  // locked-last is correct there. Viewer pick ≠ scorer pick — do not "unify".
  // Fail-soft: no rounds -> null, clients keep old wording.
  let wRound = null;
  try {
    wRound = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT id, round_number, mode, status, format FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?
       ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'active' THEN 0 WHEN 'locked' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END ASC, round_number DESC LIMIT 1`
    ).bind(event.id).first();
  } catch (_wr) { wRound = null; }
  // v2.90.0 — B-79: reset_epoch rides the watch round when the column exists.
  // Guarded SEPARATELY — folding it into the SELECT above would blank the
  // whole round object on a pre-migration paste.
  let wEpoch = null;
  if (wRound && wRound.id != null) {
    try {
      const _we = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT reset_epoch FROM rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(wRound.id).first();
      wEpoch = (_we && _we.reset_epoch != null) ? Number(_we.reset_epoch) : null;
    } catch (_b79w) { wEpoch = null; }
  }
  return json({
    event: { id: event.id, name: event.name, course_name: event.course_name, date: event.date, format: event.format, status: event.status, config: event.config || null },  // v2.38.0 config -> viewer (scoring modes/flights)
    round: wRound ? { id: wRound.id, round_number: wRound.round_number, mode: wRound.mode || 'tee_times', status: wRound.status,
                     format: wRound.format || null,
                     reset_epoch: wEpoch } : null,   // v2.85.0 — B-64 · v2.86.0 — format · v2.90.0 — reset_epoch (B-79, null pre-migration)
    standings: cache ? JSON.parse(cache.data) : [],
    computed_at: cache?.computed_at || null,
  }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleCreateEvent(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.name || !body?.date) return err('name and date required', 400, origin);
  const event_code = await uniqueEventCode(env);
  const viewer_pin = generatePin();
  const result = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO events (organizer_id, name, course_id, course_name, tee, date, format, status, config, event_code, viewer_pin, rules)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft', ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(
    account.id, body.name.trim(),
    body.course_id || null, body.course_name || null, body.tee || null,
    body.date, body.format || 'scramble',
    JSON.stringify(body.config || {}),
    event_code, viewer_pin,
    body.rules || null
  ).first();
  return json({ event: result }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleListEvents(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  // v2.92.0 — D84: trip_id per event = the hide-from-Events-list flag (a
  // non-null trip_id means the event is a trip game day and the client keeps
  // it off the TB Events list). Falls back to the v2.91.0 SELECT if the
  // trip_events migration has not run yet.
  let results;
  try {
    ({ results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT e.id, e.name, e.course_name, e.course_id, e.tee, e.date, e.format, e.status, e.event_code, e.viewer_pin, e.rules, e.created_at,
        (SELECT te.trip_id FROM trip_events te WHERE te.event_id = e.id) AS trip_id,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM groups g WHERE g.event_id = e.id) AS group_count,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tee_times ttx JOIN rounds er ON er.id = ttx.round_id WHERE er.event_id = e.id) AS tee_time_count
       FROM events e WHERE e.organizer_id = ? ORDER BY e.date DESC`
    ).bind(account.id).all());
  } catch (e) {
    ({ results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT e.id, e.name, e.course_name, e.course_id, e.tee, e.date, e.format, e.status, e.event_code, e.viewer_pin, e.rules, e.created_at,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM groups g WHERE g.event_id = e.id) AS group_count,
        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tee_times ttx JOIN rounds er ON er.id = ttx.round_id WHERE er.event_id = e.id) AS tee_time_count
       FROM events e WHERE e.organizer_id = ? ORDER BY e.date DESC`
    ).bind(account.id).all());
  }
  return json({ events: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetEvent(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, code, team_pin, start_hole, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason, scores_for_group_id, start_unit FROM groups WHERE event_id = ? ORDER BY start_hole, id'
  ).bind(id).all();
  for (const g of groups) {
    const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap, r.ghin, r.home_club, r.age, r.age_recorded_at
       FROM group_roster_players grp JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id
       WHERE grp.group_id = ? ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
    ).bind(g.id).all();
    g.players = players;
  }
  return json({ event, groups }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleUpdateEvent(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, config FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first(); // v2.78.0 — + config (D1 lock)
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  // v2.72.0 — Q1 FIELD LOCKING (Bruce, session 47: "block the maths, warn the rest").
  // The rule is the trips side's, verbatim, in force since v2.34.3: SCORES ARE
  // SACRED — REFUSE IF ANY EXIST. course_id, tee and format feed the
  // course-handicap maths (resolveCourse -> course handicap -> every net score),
  // so PATCHing one after cards are in SILENTLY re-computes results already
  // posted. That is the defect this closes.
  //
  // DELIBERATELY NOT LOCKED: name, date, rules, status, course_name — and
  // config EXCEPT its two allowance fields (v2.78.0 block below; Q1 amended
  // s61: targeted, not blanket — fold view / scoring settings / bets must
  // keep working mid-event). `status` above all — locking it would strand a
  // live event.
  //
  // scores.event_id is unambiguous, so the EVENT-level count is exact. (The
  // ROUND-level count is not, for team formats — see handleUpdateRound.)
  const LOCKED_EVENT_FIELDS = ['course_id', 'tee', 'format'];
  const lockedTouched = LOCKED_EVENT_FIELDS.filter(f => body[f] != null);
  if (lockedTouched.length) {
    const sc = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM scores WHERE event_id = ? AND gross_score > 0'
    ).bind(id).first();
    if (sc && sc.n > 0) {
      return err('This tournament has ' + sc.n + ' posted scores — ' + lockedTouched.join(', ') +
        ' can no longer be changed, because they re-compute every net score already entered. ' +
        'Clear the scores first.', 409, origin);
    }
  }
  // v2.78.0 — D1 / Q1 AMENDED (Bruce, s61): the allowance fields LOCK, the
  // rest of config stays live. They are the only config keys that enter the
  // net arithmetic.
  if (body.config != null) {
    const _pc = (c) => { try { return (typeof c === 'string' ? JSON.parse(c || '{}') : (c || {})) || {}; } catch (_) { return {}; } };
    const _oldTb = _pc(event.config).tb || {};
    const _newTb = _pc(body.config).tb || {};
    const _chg =
      JSON.stringify(_oldTb.handicap_allowance == null ? null : _oldTb.handicap_allowance) !==
      JSON.stringify(_newTb.handicap_allowance == null ? null : _newTb.handicap_allowance) ||
      String(_oldTb.team_allowance_multiplier == null ? '' : _oldTb.team_allowance_multiplier) !==
      String(_newTb.team_allowance_multiplier == null ? '' : _newTb.team_allowance_multiplier);
    if (_chg) {
      const sc2 = await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM scores WHERE event_id = ? AND gross_score > 0'
      ).bind(id).first();
      if (sc2 && sc2.n > 0) {
        return err('This tournament has ' + sc2.n + ' posted scores — the handicap allowance ' +
          'can no longer be changed, because it re-computes every net score already entered. ' +
          'Clear the scores first.', 409, origin);
      }
    }
  }
  const updates = [], values = [];
  if (body.name        != null) { updates.push('name = ?');        values.push(body.name.trim()); }
  if (body.course_id   != null) { updates.push('course_id = ?');   values.push(body.course_id); }
  if (body.course_name != null) { updates.push('course_name = ?'); values.push(body.course_name); }
  if (body.tee         != null) { updates.push('tee = ?');         values.push(body.tee); }
  if (body.date        != null) { updates.push('date = ?');        values.push(body.date); }
  if (body.format      != null) { updates.push('format = ?');      values.push(body.format); }
  if (body.status      != null) { updates.push('status = ?');      values.push(body.status); }
  if (body.config      != null) { updates.push('config = ?');      values.push(JSON.stringify(body.config)); }
  if (body.rules       != null) { updates.push('rules = ?');       values.push(body.rules); }
  if (!updates.length) return err('Nothing to update', 400, origin);
  updates.push("updated_at = datetime('now')");
  values.push(id, account.id);
  await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE events SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?`).bind(...values).run();
  if (body.status === 'active') {
    await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE rounds SET status = 'active' WHERE event_id = ?").bind(id).run();
  }
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(id).first();
  return json({ event: updated }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDeleteEvent(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  try {                                                                    // v2.63.0 — MMG scoped guests die with their event (DECIDED: cascade)
    await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND event_scope_id = ?').bind(account.id, id).run();
  } catch (_mmgCols) { /* pre-migration: column absent, nothing scoped to delete */ }
  // v2.80.0 — B-36. The old batch deleted rounds BEFORE scores and never touched
  // bets_races; both carry a plain (no-cascade) round_id FK -> rounds(id), so the
  // FK failed, D1 rolled the whole batch back, and the event survived deletion
  // (events 49/88/89 were undeletable). Order now: everything that references
  // rounds dies first, THEN rounds, then the event tree. Tee-time children use
  // the DIRECT IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times ...) form — the old JOIN rounds form
  // matched only part of the rows (s72: 60 of 120).
  await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_races WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_races WHERE round_id IN (SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_time_groups WHERE tee_time_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id IN (SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?))').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id IN (SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?))').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_times WHERE round_id IN (SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM scores WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_shots WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM roster_event_links WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM round_leaderboard_cache WHERE round_id IN (SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM event_round_money WHERE round_id IN (SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?)').bind(id), // v2.93.0 — FR-26
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM players WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM group_roster_players WHERE group_id IN (SELECT id FROM groups WHERE event_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM groups WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM leaderboard_cache WHERE event_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(id),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: id }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetRoundScores(req, env, origin, eventId, roundId) {
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, course_id, tee, format FROM rounds WHERE id = ? AND event_id = ?'
  ).bind(roundId, eventId).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(`
    SELECT
      p.id          AS player_id,
      p.name        AS player_name,
      p.handicap,
      s.hole,
      s.gross_score,
      s.group_id    AS tee_time_id,
      rel.roster_id
    FROM scores s
    JOIN players p   ON p.id = s.player_id AND p.event_id = ?
    LEFT JOIN roster_event_links rel ON rel.player_id = p.id AND rel.event_id = ?
    WHERE s.round_id = ?
    ORDER BY p.id, s.hole
  `).bind(eventId, eventId, roundId).all();
  return json({ round_id: roundId, scores: results || [] }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.76.0 — GET /api/events/:id/rounds/:rid/leaderboard. Public read, the same
// (absent) auth gate as handleGetRoundScores directly above. ?recompute=1 is
// organizer-Bearer-only and forces recomputeRoundLeaderboard first.
async function handleRoundLeaderboard(req, env, origin, eventId, roundId) {
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, round_number, format, course_id, tee, status FROM rounds WHERE id = ? AND event_id = ?'
  ).bind(roundId, eventId).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.searchParams.get('recompute') === '1') {
    const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
    if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
    const owns = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(eventId, account.id).first();
    if (!owns) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
    await recomputeRoundLeaderboard(env, roundId);   // fail-soft; a missing table shows up as unavailable below
  }
  let standings = [];
  let unavailable = null;
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT entry_key, entry_type, name, gross, net, points, holes_played, data, updated_at
         FROM round_leaderboard_cache WHERE round_id = ?
        ORDER BY holes_played DESC, CASE WHEN points IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END, points DESC, gross ASC`
    ).bind(roundId).all();
    standings = (results || []).map(r => {
      let d = null; try { d = JSON.parse(r.data || 'null'); } catch (_) {}
      return { entry_key: r.entry_key, entry_type: r.entry_type, name: r.name, gross: r.gross,
               net: r.net, points: r.points, holes_played: r.holes_played, updated_at: r.updated_at, data: d };
    });
  } catch (_e) {
    unavailable = 'round_leaderboard_cache not found — apply the v2.76.0 DDL first';
  }
  return json({ round, standings, unavailable }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleListGroupsWithRoster(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, code, team_pin, start_hole, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason, scores_for_group_id, start_unit FROM groups WHERE event_id = ? ORDER BY start_hole, name'
  ).bind(eventId).all();
  for (const g of groups) {
    const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap, r.ghin, r.age, r.age_recorded_at
       FROM group_roster_players grp
       JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id
       WHERE grp.group_id = ?
       ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
    ).bind(g.id).all();
    g.players = players;
  }
  return json({ groups }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleCreateGroup(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, course_id, tee FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.name) return err('name required', 400, origin);
  const startHole = body.start_hole || 1;
  if (!body.bypass_hole_limit) {
    const holeCount = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM groups WHERE event_id = ? AND start_hole = ?'
    ).bind(eventId, startHole).first();
    if (holeCount.n >= 2) return err(`Hole ${startHole} already has 2 teams. Maximum 2 teams per tee box.`, 409, origin);
  }
  const code = await uniqueCode(env);
  const team_pin = generatePin();
  const course_id    = body.course_id    || event.course_id || null;
  const tee          = body.tee          || event.tee       || null;
  const team_handicap = body.team_handicap != null ? Number(body.team_handicap) : null;
  const ineligible_reason = body.ineligible_reason || null;
  let group;
  if (body.scores_for_group_id !== undefined || body.start_unit !== undefined) {           // v2.63.0 — MMG columns, only when supplied
    group = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO groups (event_id, name, code, team_pin, start_hole, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason, scores_for_group_id, start_unit)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
    ).bind(eventId, body.name.trim(), code, team_pin, startHole, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason,
           body.scores_for_group_id ?? null, body.start_unit ?? null).first();
  } else {
    group = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO groups (event_id, name, code, team_pin, start_hole, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
    ).bind(eventId, body.name.trim(), code, team_pin, startHole, course_id, tee, team_handicap, ineligible_reason).first();
  }
  return json({ group }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleUpdateGroup(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!group) return err('Group not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const updates = [], values = [];
  if (body.name       != null) { updates.push('name = ?');       values.push(body.name.trim()); }
  if (body.start_hole != null) { updates.push('start_hole = ?'); values.push(Number(body.start_hole)); }
  if (body.course_id  != null) { updates.push('course_id = ?');  values.push(body.course_id); }
  if (body.tee        != null) { updates.push('tee = ?');        values.push(body.tee); }
  if (body.scores_for_group_id !== undefined) { updates.push('scores_for_group_id = ?'); values.push(body.scores_for_group_id); }  // v2.63.0 — null clears
  if (body.start_unit          !== undefined) { updates.push('start_unit = ?');          values.push(body.start_unit); }           // v2.63.0
  if (!updates.length) return err('Nothing to update', 400, origin);
  values.push(id);
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE groups SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...values).first();
  return json({ group: updated }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDeleteGroup(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(id, account.id).first();

  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM group_roster_players WHERE group_id = ?').bind(id).run();
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM scores WHERE group_id = ?').bind(id).run();
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM players WHERE group_id = ?').bind(id).run();
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_shots WHERE group_id = ?').bind(id).run();
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM groups WHERE id = ?').bind(id).run();
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: id }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGroupRosterAdd(req, env, origin, groupId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(groupId, account.id).first();
  if (!group) return err('Group not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.roster_id) return err('roster_id required', 400, origin);
  const count = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM group_roster_players WHERE group_id = ?'
  ).bind(groupId).first();
  if (count.n >= 4) return err('Group already has 4 players', 409, origin);
  const player = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, handicap, ghin FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ?'
  ).bind(body.roster_id, account.id).first();
  if (!player) return err('Player not found in roster', 404, origin);
  const existingCount = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM group_roster_players WHERE group_id = ?'
  ).bind(groupId).first();
  // v2.35.1 — honor the client's explicit sort_order (0 = captain, 1-3 = draw
  // order). The count-based fallback below raced under parallel assigns: all 4
  // requests could observe an empty group and every player became rank 0, so
  // the alphabetically-first player displayed as captain (2025 Last Call bug).
  const sortOrder = Number.isInteger(body.sort_order) ? body.sort_order : (existingCount.n === 0 ? 0 : 1);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO group_roster_players (group_id, roster_id, sort_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?)'
  ).bind(groupId, body.roster_id, sortOrder).run();
  return json({ ok: true, player }, 200, origin);
}
