async function handleGroupRosterRemove(req, env, origin, groupId, rosterId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(groupId, account.id).first();
  if (!group) return err('Group not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    'DELETE FROM group_roster_players WHERE group_id = ? AND roster_id = ?'
  ).bind(groupId, rosterId).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleSetGroupCaptain(req, env, origin, groupId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(groupId, account.id).first();
  if (!group) return err('Group not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.roster_id) return err('roster_id required', 400, origin);
  const member = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT grp.roster_id FROM group_roster_players grp WHERE grp.group_id = ? AND grp.roster_id = ?'
  ).bind(groupId, body.roster_id).first();
  if (!member) return err('Player not in this group', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE group_roster_players SET sort_order = 1 WHERE group_id = ?').bind(groupId).run();
  await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE group_roster_players SET sort_order = 0 WHERE group_id = ? AND roster_id = ?').bind(groupId, body.roster_id).run();
  const captain = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT r.name FROM roster r WHERE r.id = ?').bind(body.roster_id).first();
  if (captain) {
    await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE groups SET name = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(captain.name + ' Team', groupId).run();
  }
  return json({ ok: true, captain_name: captain?.name || null }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleJoinCode(req, env, origin, code) {
  let group;
  try {                                                                    // v2.63.0 — MMG columns; legacy retry below keeps joins alive pre-migration
    group = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT g.id, g.name, g.code, g.team_pin, g.start_hole, g.course_id, g.tee, g.team_handicap,
              g.scores_for_group_id, g.start_unit,
              e.id AS event_id, e.name AS event_name, e.date,
              e.course_id AS event_course_id, e.course_name, e.tee AS event_tee,
              e.format, e.status AS event_status, e.rules, e.event_code,
              e.config AS event_config
       FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id
       WHERE g.code = ?`
    ).bind(code).first();
  } catch (_mmgCols) {
    group = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT g.id, g.name, g.code, g.team_pin, g.start_hole, g.course_id, g.tee, g.team_handicap,
              e.id AS event_id, e.name AS event_name, e.date,
              e.course_id AS event_course_id, e.course_name, e.tee AS event_tee,
              e.format, e.status AS event_status, e.rules, e.event_code,
              e.config AS event_config
       FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id
       WHERE g.code = ?`
    ).bind(code).first();
    if (group) group.scores_for_group_id = null;
  }
  if (!group) return err('Invalid code', 404, origin);
  const pin = req.headers.get('X-Team-Pin') || '';
  const pinValid = pin && String(group.team_pin) === String(pin);
  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap, r.ghin
     FROM group_roster_players grp JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id
     WHERE grp.group_id = ? ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
  ).bind(group.id).all();
  const effective_course_id = group.course_id || group.event_course_id || null;
  const effective_tee = group.tee || group.event_tee || null;
  // v2.79.0 — B-32: the team's current round, so the client can decide whether
  // this code may join (multi_day scramble YES, multi_day individual NO). The
  // group may sit in several rounds' tee sheets; prefer an unlocked round,
  // then the highest round_number. Fail-soft: a groups-only event (no rounds,
  // no tee sheet) simply carries nulls.
  // v2.81.0 — B-45: MOVED ABOVE the pairing block — the swap policy below
  // needs the round before the pair is derived.
  // ⭐ v2.90.0 — B-82 note: this ORDER BY is the SCORER pick — locked-last is
  // CORRECT here (never steer a scorer onto a locked round). Do NOT copy the
  // handleWatch viewer pick into this query.
  let joinRound = null;
  try {
    joinRound = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id AS round_id, r.round_number, r.format AS round_format,
              r.mode AS round_mode, r.session AS round_session, r.status AS round_status
         FROM tee_time_players ttp
         JOIN tee_times t ON t.id = ttp.tee_time_id
         JOIN rounds r    ON r.id = t.round_id
        WHERE ttp.group_id = ? AND r.event_id = ?
        ORDER BY CASE WHEN r.status = 'locked' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END ASC, r.round_number DESC
        LIMIT 1`
    ).bind(group.id, group.event_id).first();
  } catch (_jr) { joinRound = null; }
  // v2.90.0 — B-79: the epoch a future client will post back. Guarded
  // separately for the same pre-migration reason as handleWatch.
  let joinEpoch = null;
  if (joinRound && joinRound.round_id != null) {
    try {
      const _je = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT reset_epoch FROM rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(joinRound.round_id).first();
      joinEpoch = (_je && _je.reset_epoch != null) ? Number(_je.reset_epoch) : null;
    } catch (_b79j) { joinEpoch = null; }
  }
  // v2.81.0 — B-45 (Bruce s76, Randy Goulet r3): the join now OBEYS the
  // organizer's Card Swap setting (client cfg.ttb_swap[round_id], written by
  // the D29 round editor). Until now this handler paired by same-start-hole
  // regardless — on a tee-times round EVERY team sits at start_hole 1 in the
  // groups table, so .first() grabbed an arbitrary team (Tim Kantor's QR
  // offered Donald Coulter's card on a round set to NO SWAP).
  // Round precedence: ?rid= (printed on the card's QR by client v397.0+,
  // exact) → the B-32 joinRound resolution → null. Policy semantics MATCH the
  // client's ptEvCardSwap/ptEvScorerMap exactly:
  //   'none'/'marker' → never pair · 'pointer' → pointer only, never guess ·
  //   'pair' → same-hole derivation only · absent/auto → pointer first, then
  //   derivation. The derivation now ALSO carries the client's exactly-two
  //   guard (3+ on a hole → refuse to guess) in EVERY mode — that alone fixes
  //   B-45 even for hand-typed codes with no round context.
  let swapMode = null;
  try {
    const _cfg = typeof group.event_config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(group.event_config || '{}') : (group.event_config || {});
    let _rid = null;
    const _ridQ = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get('rid');
    if (_ridQ && /^[0-9]{1,8}$/.test(_ridQ)) _rid = _ridQ;
    else if (joinRound && joinRound.round_id != null) _rid = String(joinRound.round_id);
    const _sw = _rid && _cfg.ttb_swap ? _cfg.ttb_swap[_rid] : null;
    if (_sw && ['none', 'pointer', 'pair', 'marker'].includes(_sw.mode)) swapMode = _sw.mode;
  } catch (_swErr) { swapMode = null; }
  let pairedGroup = null;                                                 // v2.63.0 — pointer first (MMG 3-cycle), derivation fallback (RBC unchanged)
  const _usePtr  = swapMode !== 'none' && swapMode !== 'marker' && swapMode !== 'pair';
  const _usePair = swapMode !== 'none' && swapMode !== 'marker' && swapMode !== 'pointer';
  if (_usePtr && group.scores_for_group_id) {
    pairedGroup = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT g.id, g.name, g.code, g.team_pin FROM groups g
       WHERE g.id = ? AND g.event_id = ?`
    ).bind(group.scores_for_group_id, group.event_id).first();
  } else if (group.format === 'mmg') {
    pairedGroup = null;   // MMG with no pointer set = fail-soft self-scoring; never guess among 2 other teams on the unit
  } else if (_usePair) {
    // v2.81.0 — exactly-two guard (client parity, ptEvScorerMap): pair ONLY
    // when precisely one other team shares the hole. 3+ was always arbitrary.
    const _holeCount = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM groups g WHERE g.event_id = ? AND g.start_hole = ?`
    ).bind(group.event_id, group.start_hole).first();
    if (_holeCount && Number(_holeCount.n) === 2) {
      pairedGroup = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT g.id, g.name, g.code, g.team_pin FROM groups g
         WHERE g.event_id = ? AND g.start_hole = ? AND g.id != ?`
      ).bind(group.event_id, group.start_hole, group.id).first();
    }
  }
  let pairedPlayers = [];
  if (pairedGroup) {
    const { results: pp } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap FROM group_roster_players grp
       JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id WHERE grp.group_id = ?
       ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
    ).bind(pairedGroup.id).all();
    pairedPlayers = pp;
  }
  const { results: teeShots } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT hole, player_name, recorded_by FROM tee_shots WHERE group_id = ? ORDER BY hole'
  ).bind(group.id).all();
  let pairedTeeShots = [];
  if (pairedGroup) {
    const { results: pts } = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT hole, player_name, recorded_by FROM tee_shots WHERE group_id = ? ORDER BY hole'
    ).bind(pairedGroup.id).all();
    pairedTeeShots = pts;
  }
  return json({
    group: {
      id: group.id, name: group.name, code: group.code, start_hole: group.start_hole,
      course_id: effective_course_id, tee: effective_tee, event_id: group.event_id,
      event_name: group.event_name, date: group.date, course_name: group.course_name,
      format: group.format, event_status: group.event_status, rules: group.rules || null,
      team_handicap: group.team_handicap ?? null,
      start_unit: group.start_unit ?? null, // v2.63.0 — MMG start unit label (e.g. '4A')
      event_code: group.event_code || null, // v2.52.0 — lets the scorer client poll /api/events/watch (live ticker)
      round_id:      joinRound ? joinRound.round_id      : null, // v2.79.0 — B-32 round context (additive)
      round_number:  joinRound ? joinRound.round_number  : null,
      round_format:  joinRound ? joinRound.round_format  : null,
      round_mode:    joinRound ? joinRound.round_mode    : null,
      round_session: joinRound ? joinRound.round_session : null,
      round_status:  joinRound ? joinRound.round_status  : null,
      round_reset_epoch: joinEpoch, // v2.90.0 — B-79 (null pre-migration; additive)
    },
    players, pin_valid: pinValid,
    paired_group: pairedGroup ? { id: pairedGroup.id, name: pairedGroup.name, code: pairedGroup.code } : null,
    paired_players: pairedPlayers, tee_shots: teeShots, paired_tee_shots: pairedTeeShots,
  }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleLeaderboard(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const cache = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT data, computed_at FROM leaderboard_cache WHERE event_id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name, course_name, date, format, status FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  return json({ event, standings: cache ? JSON.parse(cache.data) : [], computed_at: cache?.computed_at || null }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleSubmitScores(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.group_code || !body?.player_name || !Array.isArray(body.scores)) {
    return err('group_code, player_name, and scores[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  if (!body.scores.length) return err('scores[] must not be empty', 400, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id AS group_id, g.event_id, e.status, e.organizer_id
     FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.code = ?`
  ).bind(body.group_code.toUpperCase()).first();
  if (!group) return err('Invalid group code', 404, origin);
  if (group.status !== 'active') return err('Event is not active', 409, origin);
  // v2.73.0 — resolve the round for a team/scramble card. groups has no round_id,
  // but tee_time_players links a group to a tee time, and tee_times.round_id gives
  // the round.
  // ⭐⭐ v2.75.0 — THE ORIGINAL JUSTIFICATION HERE WAS WRONG AND IS STRUCK OUT.
  // It read "UNIQUE(tee_time_id, group_id) makes this one-to-one". It does not:
  // that constraint permits ONE GROUP ACROSS MANY TEE TIMES, and therefore across
  // many rounds of the same event. There is NO ROUND SCOPING in the WHERE below,
  // no ORDER BY and no LIMIT — so if a group ever appears in two rounds of one
  // event, .first() returns an ARBITRARY one of them.
  // ⭐ SAFE TODAY BY DATA CONVENTION ONLY. Re-verified against live D1 in session
  // 51: zero groups appear in more than one round of a single event. The query is
  // left EXACTLY as-is on purpose — the caller holds no round context, so every
  // available tie-break would be arbitrary too. Re-measure before reusing a group.
  // v2.79.0 — B-32: the v2.75.0 "arbitrary .first()" landmine is retired.
  // A group on two rounds' tee sheets now resolves to the unlocked round,
  // then the highest round_number — the round being played. Same single-row
  // answer as before on every group that sits in exactly one round.
  // ⭐ v2.90.0 — B-82 note: SCORER pick — locked-last is correct here; see
  // the handleWatch comment before "unifying" anything.
  const ttRound = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT t.round_id, r.status AS round_status FROM tee_time_players ttp
       JOIN tee_times t ON t.id = ttp.tee_time_id
       JOIN rounds r    ON r.id = t.round_id
      WHERE ttp.group_id = ? AND r.event_id = ?
      ORDER BY CASE WHEN r.status = 'locked' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END ASC, r.round_number DESC
      LIMIT 1`
  ).bind(group.group_id, group.event_id).first();
  const resolvedRoundId = ttRound ? ttRound.round_id : null;
  // v2.79.0 — D34 §9.2 / B-32: a locked round refuses the team submit exactly
  // like handleSubmitRoundScores refuses the tee-time path. Only fires when
  // EVERY round this group belongs to is locked (the ORDER BY above already
  // steered to an unlocked one if any exists).
  if (ttRound && ttRound.round_status === 'locked') {
    return err('Scoring is locked for this round \u2014 see the organizer.', 409, origin);
  }
  // ⭐ v2.90.0 — B-79, team-card leg (same rule as handleSubmitRoundScores).
  // Separate guarded SELECT — the ttRound query above must not gain a column
  // that would 500 every team submit on a pre-migration paste.
  if (Number.isFinite(body.reset_epoch) && resolvedRoundId != null) {
    try {
      const _re = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT reset_epoch FROM rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(resolvedRoundId).first();
      if (_re && _re.reset_epoch != null && Number(_re.reset_epoch) > Number(body.reset_epoch)) {
        return err('Scores were reset by the organizer after this card was opened — leave and rejoin the round before posting again.', 409, origin);
      }
    } catch (_b79t) { /* pre-migration: column absent — accept, v2.89.0 behaviour */ }
  }
  let player = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id FROM players WHERE group_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE'
  ).bind(group.group_id, body.player_name.trim()).first();
  if (!player) {
    player = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO players (event_id, group_id, name, handicap, tee) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING id`
    ).bind(group.event_id, group.group_id, body.player_name.trim(), body.handicap ?? 0, body.tee || 'white').first();
  }
  const stmts = body.scores.map(s =>
    env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO scores (event_id, group_id, player_id, hole, gross_score, round_id, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
       ON CONFLICT(player_id, hole, group_id)
       DO UPDATE SET gross_score = excluded.gross_score, round_id = COALESCE(excluded.round_id, scores.round_id), updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
    // ⭐ v2.99.0 (B-121) — NAMESPACE A: group_id here is a `groups.id`. The
    // other writer of this column, handleSubmitRoundScores, stores a
    // `tee_times.id` instead. The column has no FK and nothing distinguishes
    // them at rest — any reader joining on it MUST know which path wrote the row.
    ).bind(group.event_id, group.group_id, player.id, s.hole, s.gross_score, resolvedRoundId)
  );
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  const rosterRecord = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1`
  ).bind(group.organizer_id, body.player_name.trim()).first();
  if (rosterRecord) {
    await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roster_event_links (roster_id, event_id, player_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)`
    ).bind(rosterRecord.id, group.event_id, player.id).run();
    // v2.95.0 -- B-100: stamp the same id onto the player row itself. The
    // cross-rail leaderboard joins on players.roster_id, and until now this
    // was never written. "AND roster_id IS NULL" makes it a fill, not an
    // overwrite. Fail-soft: pre-migration DBs lack the column, and a failure
    // here must never cost the golfer their scores (already batched above).
    try {
      await env.DB.prepare(
        'UPDATE players SET roster_id = ? WHERE id = ? AND roster_id IS NULL'
      ).bind(rosterRecord.id, player.id).run();
    } catch (_b100a) { /* column absent -- pre-migration, accept */ }
  }
  await recomputeLeaderboard(env, group.event_id);
  if (resolvedRoundId) await recomputeRoundLeaderboard(env, resolvedRoundId);   // v2.76.0 — additive, fail-soft, AFTER the event cache
  return json({ ok: true, player_id: player.id }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetGroupScores(req, env, origin, groupId) {
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT p.name AS player_name, p.handicap, p.tee, s.hole, s.gross_score, s.updated_at
     FROM scores s JOIN players p ON p.id = s.player_id
     WHERE s.group_id = ? ORDER BY p.name, s.hole`
  ).bind(groupId).all();
  const byPlayer = {};
  let latestUpdatedAt = null;
  let scorerName = null;
  for (const row of results) {
    if (!byPlayer[row.player_name]) {
      byPlayer[row.player_name] = { name: row.player_name, handicap: row.handicap, tee: row.tee, holes: {} };
    }
    byPlayer[row.player_name].holes[row.hole] = row.gross_score;
    if (!latestUpdatedAt || row.updated_at > latestUpdatedAt) {
      latestUpdatedAt = row.updated_at;
      scorerName = row.player_name;
    }
  }
  const group = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT start_hole, team_handicap, ineligible_reason FROM groups WHERE id = ?').bind(groupId).first();
  const { results: rosterPlayers } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap, r.ghin
     FROM group_roster_players grp JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id
     WHERE grp.group_id = ? ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
  ).bind(groupId).all();
  return json({
    players: Object.values(byPlayer), roster_players: rosterPlayers, scorer: scorerName,
    start_hole: group?.start_hole || 1, team_handicap: group?.team_handicap ?? null,
    ineligible_reason: group?.ineligible_reason ?? null, par: null,
  }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleFinalizeHandicaps(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name, course_id, tee FROM groups WHERE event_id = ?').bind(eventId).all();
  const eventRow = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT course_id, tee, config FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(eventId).first(); // v2.78.0 — + config (D1)
  let updated = 0;
  const results = [];
  const _teeCache = new Map();                                        // v2.88.0 — one D1 read per distinct course/tee
  for (const group of groups) {
    const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.handicap FROM group_roster_players grp JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id
       WHERE grp.group_id = ? AND r.handicap IS NOT NULL ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
    ).bind(group.id).all();
    if (players.length < 4) { results.push({ id: group.id, name: group.name, skipped: true, reason: 'Fewer than 4 players with handicaps' }); continue; }
    const courseId = group.course_id || eventRow?.course_id || null;
    const tee      = group.tee      || eventRow?.tee      || null;
    const teeData  = await resolveTee(env, courseId, tee, _teeCache);  // v2.88.0 — B-70
    const courseHcps = players.map(p => calcCourseHandicap(p.handicap, teeData.slope, teeData.rating, teeData.par));
    courseHcps.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    // v2.78.0 — D1: the RBC weights stop being hardcoded. Stored vector ×
    // team multiplier when config.tb carries them; ABSENT → 25/20/15/10 × 75,
    // which reproduces the pre-D1 numbers exactly (RBC is a number now, s46).
    const _tb = (() => { try { return (JSON.parse(eventRow?.config || '{}') || {}).tb || {}; } catch (_) { return {}; } })();
    const _vec = (Array.isArray(_tb.handicap_allowance) && _tb.handicap_allowance.length >= 4)
      ? _tb.handicap_allowance.slice(0, 4).map(Number) : [25, 20, 15, 10];
    const _mult = (_tb.team_allowance_multiplier != null && isFinite(Number(_tb.team_allowance_multiplier)))
      ? Number(_tb.team_allowance_multiplier) : 75;
    const raw = _vec.reduce((t, w, i) => t + (isFinite(w) ? w : 0) / 100 * courseHcps[i], 0);
    const team_handicap = Math.round(raw * (_mult / 100) * 10) / 10;
    const idxs = players.map(p => p.handicap);
    const agg = idxs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
    const singles = idxs.filter(x => x <= 9.9).length;
    const errors = [];
    if (agg < 32.0) errors.push(`Agg hdcp ${agg.toFixed(1)} < 32.0 min`);
    if (agg > 112.0) errors.push(`Agg hdcp ${agg.toFixed(1)} > 112.0 max`);
    if (singles > 2) errors.push(`${singles} single-digit players (max 2)`);
    const ineligible_reason = errors.length ? errors.join('; ') : null;
    await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE groups SET team_handicap = ?, ineligible_reason = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(team_handicap, ineligible_reason, group.id).run();
    updated++;
    results.push({ id: group.id, name: group.name, team_handicap, ineligible_reason });
  }
  await recomputeLeaderboard(env, eventId);
  return json({ ok: true, updated, results }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MMG — Member/Guest two-man team event (v2.63.0, spec claude/mmg-spec-v1.md)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// THE MMG math — one implementation. recomputeLeaderboard (cache/watch) and
// handleMmgLeaderboard (organizer live view) both call this; nothing else may
// compute MMG standings. Per-hole points go through leagueHolePts +
// hcpStrokesForHole (the standard ramp) — never a local copy.
// DECIDED (spec §10): FULL course handicap; blank handicap = 0 strokes;
// tiebreak per config.mmg_tiebreak 'share' (default) | 'back9' (combined
// B9 → B6 → B3 → 18th countback).
async function computeMmgStandings(env, event) {
  let cfg = {};
  try { cfg = typeof event.config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(event.config || '{}') : (event.config || {}); } catch (_) { cfg = {}; }
  const si   = Array.isArray(cfg.mmg_si)   && cfg.mmg_si.length   === 18 ? cfg.mmg_si   : null;
  const pars = Array.isArray(cfg.mmg_pars) && cfg.mmg_pars.length === 18 ? cfg.mmg_pars : null;
  const tiebreak = cfg.mmg_tiebreak === 'back9' ? 'back9' : 'share';
  const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, start_hole, start_unit, course_id, tee FROM groups WHERE event_id = ?'
  ).bind(event.id).all();
  const _teeCache = new Map();                                        // v2.88.0 — B-70: memoised tee lookups
  const standings = [];
  for (const group of groups) {
    const { results: roster } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap FROM group_roster_players grp
       JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id WHERE grp.group_id = ?
       ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
    ).bind(group.id).all();
    const { results: scoreRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT p.name AS player_name, s.hole, s.gross_score FROM scores s
       JOIN players p ON p.id = s.player_id WHERE s.group_id = ?`
    ).bind(group.id).all();
    const teeData = await resolveTee(env, group.course_id || event.course_id, group.tee || event.tee, _teeCache);  // v2.88.0 — B-70
    const players = roster.map(r => {
      const playing_handicap = r.handicap != null
        ? Math.round(calcCourseHandicap(r.handicap, teeData.slope, teeData.rating, teeData.par))
        : 0;                                                               // DECIDED: blank handicap = 0 strokes
      const hole_gross = new Array(18).fill(null);
      const hole_pts   = new Array(18).fill(null);
      for (const s of scoreRows) {
        if ((s.player_name || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== (r.name || '').trim().toLowerCase()) continue;
        const h = s.hole - 1;
        if (h < 0 || h > 17) continue;
        hole_gross[h] = s.gross_score;
        const par    = pars ? (pars[h] || 4) : 4;
        const holeSi = si   ? (si[h]  || h + 1) : h + 1;
        hole_pts[h] = leagueHolePts(s.gross_score, null, par, holeSi, playing_handicap);
      }
      const f9_pts   = hole_pts.slice(0, 9).reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);
      const b9_pts   = hole_pts.slice(9, 18).reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);
      const b6_pts   = hole_pts.slice(12, 18).reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);
      const b3_pts   = hole_pts.slice(15, 18).reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);
      const h18_pts  = hole_pts[17] ?? 0;
      const f9_gross = hole_gross.slice(0, 9).reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);
      const b9_gross = hole_gross.slice(9, 18).reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);
      return {
        roster_id: r.roster_id, name: r.name,
        handicap: r.handicap ?? null,                                      // current index — _hdcpBadge on the MMG report
        playing_handicap,
        holes_played: hole_gross.filter(v => v != null && v > 0).length,
        total_pts: f9_pts + b9_pts, f9_pts, b9_pts, b6_pts, b3_pts, h18_pts,
        total_gross: f9_gross + b9_gross, f9_gross, b9_gross,
        hole_pts, hole_gross,
      };
    });
    const sum = k => players.reduce((a, p) => a + (p[k] || 0), 0);
    standings.push({
      group_id: group.id, name: group.name,
      start_hole: group.start_hole, start_unit: group.start_unit ?? null,
      players,
      combined_pts: sum('total_pts'), combined_f9: sum('f9_pts'), combined_b9: sum('b9_pts'),
      combined_b6: sum('b6_pts'), combined_b3: sum('b3_pts'), combined_h18: sum('h18_pts'),
      combined_gross: sum('total_gross'),
      thru: players.length ? Math.min(...players.map(p => p.holes_played)) : 0,
    });
  }
  const cb = t => [t.combined_pts, t.combined_b9, t.combined_b6, t.combined_b3, t.combined_h18];
  standings.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.combined_pts !== a.combined_pts) return b.combined_pts - a.combined_pts;
    if (tiebreak === 'back9') {
      const ca = cb(a), cbb = cb(b);
      for (let i = 1; i < ca.length; i++) if (cbb[i] !== ca[i]) return cbb[i] - ca[i];
    }
    return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
  });
  applyTieAwareRanks(standings, (p, q) => tiebreak === 'back9'
    ? cb(p).every((v, i) => v === cb(q)[i])
    : p.combined_pts === q.combined_pts);
  return { standings, tiebreak };
}

// GET /api/events/:id/mmg-leaderboard — organizer live view, computed fresh.
async function handleMmgLeaderboard(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, date, course_id, course_name, tee, format, status, config FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?'
  ).bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  if (event.format !== 'mmg') return err('Not an MMG event', 400, origin);
  const { standings, tiebreak } = await computeMmgStandings(env, event);
  return json({
    event: { id: event.id, name: event.name, date: event.date, course_name: event.course_name, tee: event.tee, status: event.status },
    standings, tiebreak,
  }, 200, origin);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// WAVE-1 GAMES (v2.66.0, spec claude/games-expansion-spec-v1.md §1)
// Part 1 of 3: Quota. Rabbit and Nines land in later versions and hang off
// the same endpoint and the same config.games object.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Per-hole pars for an EVENT, or null when we genuinely do not know them.
// THE one place event per-hole pars are resolved — if Rabbit or Nines ever
// need pars, they call this, they do not re-derive it.
//
// Order: config.game_pars (written by the client for any event with a wave-1
// game enabled) -> config.mmg_pars (already written for MMG events) -> null.
// NULL IS A REAL ANSWER AND MUST BE HONOURED. Do not add a par-4 fallback
// here: see the v2.66.0 header note. A wrong par silently produces a wrong
// game, which is the exact class of failure preflight exists to catch.
function eventHolePars(cfg) {
  const ok = a => Array.isArray(a) && a.length === 18 && a.every(v => Number(v) > 0);
  if (ok(cfg && cfg.game_pars)) return cfg.game_pars.map(Number);
  if (ok(cfg && cfg.mmg_pars))  return cfg.mmg_pars.map(Number);
  return null;
}

// ⭐⭐ v2.86.0 — THE PAR FALLBACK (found s87, built s89).
// eventHolePars() reads per-hole pars out of the EVENT CONFIG only. Bruce's
// event 95 has none, so EVERY stableford round on it computed points === null —
// server-side, for the full app exactly as much as for lite. The pars were in
// D1 the whole time: course_tees.pars for the round's own tee, and courses.par
// as the course-wide array (verified s89: tower-ranch-gc carries both, and they
// agree with the client's own PARS table byte for byte).
//
// ⭐ THIS IS A REAL SOURCE, NOT AN INVENTION. The v2.66.0 rule is untouched:
// when we genuinely do not know the pars we still return null and still say so
// in par_source. We are widening WHERE we look, never guessing par 4.
//
// Precedence: event config -> the round's own tee -> the course. SI rides the
// SAME precedence, so points and stroke allocation can never come from two
// different rows. Fail-soft throughout: any error is "no pars", which is a
// real answer the callers already handle.
async function courseHolePars(env, courseId, tee) {
  const out = { pars: null, si: null, source: 'none' };
  if (!courseId) return out;
  const ok18 = a => Array.isArray(a) && a.length === 18 && a.every(v => Number(v) > 0);
  const parse = v => { try { const a = typeof v === 'string' ? JSON.parse(v) : v; return ok18(a) ? a.map(Number) : null; } catch (_) { return null; } };
  try {
    if (tee) {
      const t = await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT pars, handicaps FROM course_tees WHERE course_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1'
      ).bind(courseId, tee).first();
      const tp = t ? parse(t.pars) : null;
      if (tp) { out.pars = tp; out.si = parse(t.handicaps); out.source = 'course_tee'; return out; }
    }
    const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT par, si FROM courses WHERE id = ?').bind(courseId).first();
    const cp = c ? parse(c.par) : null;
    if (cp) { out.pars = cp; out.si = parse(c.si); out.source = 'course'; }
  } catch (_chp) { /* fail-soft — "no pars" is a real answer, never a throw */ }
  return out;
}

// Per-hole stroke index for an EVENT, or null. Quota does NOT need this
// (gross points allocate no strokes), but Rabbit and Nines will, and this
// keeps the resolution in one place from the start.
function eventHoleSI(cfg) {
  const ok = a => Array.isArray(a) && a.length === 18 && a.every(v => Number(v) >= 1);
  if (ok(cfg && cfg.game_si)) return cfg.game_si.map(Number);
  if (ok(cfg && cfg.mmg_si))  return cfg.mmg_si.map(Number);
  return null;
}

// THE Quota math — one implementation. Anything that ever shows a Quota
// number calls this; nothing computes Quota anywhere else.
//
// CLASSIC QUOTA (Bruce, DECIDED):
//   quota  = base - course handicap        (base 36, re-derived — see header)
//   points = GROSS points vs par, standard ramp, NO strokes allocated
//   result = points - quota                (positive = beat your quota)
//
// The handicap enters exactly once, through the target. Passing playingHcp = 0
// to leagueHolePts is what makes the points gross: hcpStrokesForHole(si, 0)
// returns 0, so net === gross and the ramp is untouched. storedPts is ALWAYS
// null here — the stored stableford_pts column holds NET points and would
// quietly turn this into a different game.
async function computeQuotaStandings(env, event) {
  let cfg = {};
  try { cfg = typeof event.config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(event.config || '{}') : (event.config || {}); } catch (_) { cfg = {}; }

  const games = (cfg && cfg.games) || {};
  const qcfg  = games.quota || {};
  const base  = Number.isFinite(Number(qcfg.base)) ? Number(qcfg.base) : 36;

  // v2.66.0 — the roster path below is group_roster_players -> roster, which is
  // the MMG shape. On any other format it returns nothing, and available:true
  // with an empty standings array reads as "nobody has scored" — a plausible
  // wrong answer, the exact failure class the pars refusal exists to avoid.
  // Same response shape as per_hole_pars_missing, so the client needs no new
  // branch: available:false + reason + message.
  if (event.format !== 'mmg') {
    return {
      available: false,
      reason: 'unsupported_format',
      message: 'Quota currently runs on Member/Guest (MMG) events only. This event is a ' + (event.format || 'different') + ' event.',
      base, standings: [],
    };
  }

  const pars = eventHolePars(cfg);
  if (!pars) {
    // Refuse, with a reason the organizer can act on. Never a plausible number.
    return {
      available: false,
      reason: 'per_hole_pars_missing',
      message: 'Quota needs the per-hole pars for this course. Open the event in the organizer and re-save the course/tee to publish them, then try again.',
      base, standings: [],
    };
  }
  const si = eventHoleSI(cfg);   // may be null — Quota does not use it

  const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, course_id, tee FROM groups WHERE event_id = ?'
  ).bind(event.id).all();
  const _teeCache = new Map();                                        // v2.88.0 — B-70: memoised tee lookups

  const standings = [];
  for (const group of groups) {
    const { results: roster } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap FROM group_roster_players grp
       JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id WHERE grp.group_id = ?
       ORDER BY grp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
    ).bind(group.id).all();
    const { results: scoreRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT p.name AS player_name, s.hole, s.gross_score FROM scores s
       JOIN players p ON p.id = s.player_id WHERE s.group_id = ?`
    ).bind(group.id).all();

    const teeData = await resolveTee(env, group.course_id || event.course_id, group.tee || event.tee, _teeCache);  // v2.88.0 — B-70

    for (const r of roster) {
      // Blank handicap = 0 strokes, matching computeMmgStandings (spec §10).
      // Here that means quota = base, i.e. a scratch target — the honest
      // reading of "we do not know this player's handicap".
      const course_handicap = r.handicap != null
        ? Math.round(calcCourseHandicap(r.handicap, teeData.slope, teeData.rating, teeData.par))
        : 0;
      const quota = base - course_handicap;

      const hole_gross = new Array(18).fill(null);
      const hole_pts   = new Array(18).fill(null);
      for (const s of scoreRows) {
        if ((s.player_name || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== (r.name || '').trim().toLowerCase()) continue;
        const h = s.hole - 1;
        if (h < 0 || h > 17) continue;
        if (!s.gross_score || s.gross_score <= 0) continue;
        hole_gross[h] = s.gross_score;
        const holeSi = si ? (si[h] || h + 1) : h + 1;              // unused at hcp 0; passed for shape
        hole_pts[h]  = leagueHolePts(s.gross_score, null, pars[h], holeSi, 0);   // 0 => GROSS
      }

      const holes_played = hole_gross.filter(v => v != null && v > 0).length;
      const points = hole_pts.reduce((a, v) => a + (v ?? 0), 0);

      // Quota is a FULL-ROUND target. Mid-round, `result` compares a partial
      // points total against the whole quota and will read deeply negative —
      // correct but useless on a live board. pace_quota pro-rates the target
      // to holes played so the client can show a running position; the client
      // decides which to display, the worker ships both and never guesses.
      const pace_quota = holes_played > 0
        ? Math.round((quota * holes_played / 18) * 10) / 10
        : 0;

      standings.push({
        roster_id: r.roster_id,
        name: r.name,
        group_id: group.id,
        group_name: group.name,
        handicap: r.handicap ?? null,          // index as held on the roster
        course_handicap,
        quota,
        points,
        result: points - quota,                // full-round, the settled number
        pace_quota,
        pace_result: Math.round((points - pace_quota) * 10) / 10,
        holes_played,
        hole_pts,
        hole_gross,
      });
    }
  }

  // Best result first. Ties broken on points, then on holes played — MORE
  // holes ranks ahead, so a player who has finished is not displaced by one
  // who reached the same number with holes still to play (Bruce, DECIDED
  // July 28 2026; the pre-merge draft sorted this ascending, which did the
  // opposite of what its own comment claimed). Name last, for stability.
  standings.sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.result !== a.result) return b.result - a.result;
    if (b.points !== a.points) return b.points - a.points;
    if (a.holes_played !== b.holes_played) return b.holes_played - a.holes_played;
    return String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name));
  });
  applyTieAwareRanks(standings, (p, q) => p.result === q.result && p.points === q.points);

  return { available: true, base, standings };
}

// GET /api/events/:id/games?game=quota — organizer live view, computed fresh.
//
// Auth mirrors handleMmgLeaderboard exactly: session + organizer_id. A public
// watch variant (the no-auth path the ticker uses) is deliberately NOT added
// here — it needs the same event-code gate /api/events/watch uses, and that is
// its own decision. Wire the organizer view first, prove the numbers, then
// open it up.
// Derives its own URL rather than taking one as an argument — that is the
// established pattern here (handleRosterList, handleRosterSearch and three
// others all do `const url = new URL(req.url)` internally, even though the
// router has one in scope). Matching it keeps the handler signature uniform.
async function handleEventGames(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, date, course_id, course_name, tee, format, status, config FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?'
  ).bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);

  const game = String(url.searchParams.get('game') || 'quota').toLowerCase();

  let cfg = {};
  try { cfg = typeof event.config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(event.config || '{}') : (event.config || {}); } catch (_) { cfg = {}; }
  const games = (cfg && cfg.games) || {};

  if (game === 'quota') {
    // OFF by default (spec §3). An organizer who has not enabled Quota gets a
    // clean "off", not an empty board that looks like nobody has scored.
    if (!games.quota || !games.quota.on) {
      return json({ game: 'quota', enabled: false, available: false, reason: 'game_off', standings: [] }, 200, origin);
    }
    const out = await computeQuotaStandings(env, event);
    return json({
      game: 'quota',
      enabled: true,
      event: { id: event.id, name: event.name, date: event.date, course_name: event.course_name, tee: event.tee, status: event.status },
      ...out,
    }, 200, origin);
  }

  // rabbit / nines land here in v2.67.0 / v2.68.0.
  return err('Unknown or not-yet-implemented game: ' + game, 400, origin);
}

// POST /api/events/:id/mmg-import — bulk Member/Guest pair import.
// body: { teams: [{ name?, member: { roster_id? | name, ghin?, create? }, guest: { name, handicap? } }] }
// member: link by roster_id → exact unscoped match → create (only when create:true;
//         the client pre-flights fuzzy matches so an unmatched member is DELIBERATE).
// guest:  matched/created WITHIN this event's scope (roster.event_scope_id) — never
//         touches the permanent roster. Blank handicap by design; Bruce fills later.
// Idempotent-ish: a duplicate team name for the event is skipped (safe re-import).
async function handleMmgImport(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, format FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  if (event.format !== 'mmg') return err('Not an MMG event', 400, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.teams || !Array.isArray(body.teams)) return err('teams[] required', 400, origin);
  if (body.teams.length > 120) return err('Max 120 teams per import', 400, origin);
  let created_teams = 0, skipped_teams = 0, linked_members = 0, created_members = 0, created_guests = 0, matched_guests = 0, linked_guests = 0;  // v2.64.0
  const details = [];
  for (const t of body.teams) {
    const memberName = t?.member?.name ? String(t.member.name).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ') : '';
    const guestName  = t?.guest?.name  ? String(t.guest.name).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')  : '';
    if ((!memberName && !t?.member?.roster_id) || !guestName) { skipped_teams++; details.push({ team: t?.name || memberName || '?', skipped: 'member and guest name required' }); continue; }
    const teamName = (t.name ? String(t.name).trim() : '') || `${memberName} / ${guestName}`;
    const dup = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM groups WHERE event_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE').bind(eventId, teamName).first();
    if (dup) { skipped_teams++; details.push({ team: teamName, skipped: 'team already exists' }); continue; }
    // member
    let memberId = null;
    if (t.member.roster_id) {
      const m = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ?').bind(Number(t.member.roster_id), account.id).first();
      if (m) { memberId = m.id; linked_members++; }
    }
    if (!memberId && memberName) {
      const m = await rosterMatchUnscoped(env, account.id, t.member.ghin ? String(t.member.ghin).trim() : null, memberName);
      if (m) { memberId = m.id; linked_members++; }
    }
    if (!memberId && t.member.create && memberName) {
      const m = await env.DB.prepare(
        'INSERT INTO roster (account_id, name, handicap) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING id'
      ).bind(account.id, memberName, t.member.handicap ?? null).first();
      memberId = m.id; created_members++;
    }
    if (!memberId) { skipped_teams++; details.push({ team: teamName, skipped: 'member unmatched (pass roster_id or create:true)' }); continue; }
    // guest — v2.64.0: link the permanent roster row when the client matched one;
    // only true strangers get event-scoped rows.
    let guestId = null;
    if (t.guest.roster_id) {
      const pg = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ? AND event_scope_id IS NULL').bind(Number(t.guest.roster_id), account.id).first();
      if (pg) { guestId = pg.id; linked_guests++; }
    }
    const g = guestId ? null : await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND event_scope_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE'
    ).bind(account.id, eventId, guestName).first();
    if (g) { guestId = g.id; matched_guests++; }
    else if (!guestId) {                                                   // v2.64.0 — a LINKED guest must never fall through to scoped creation
      const ng = await env.DB.prepare(
        'INSERT INTO roster (account_id, name, handicap, event_scope_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING id'
      ).bind(account.id, guestName, t.guest.handicap ?? null, eventId).first();
      guestId = ng.id; created_guests++;
    }
    // team group — direct insert (no hole-cap check; start_hole placeholder 1 until the sixsome builder assigns)
    const code = await uniqueCode(env);
    const team_pin = generatePin();
    const group = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO groups (event_id, name, code, team_pin, start_hole) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 1) RETURNING id`
    ).bind(eventId, teamName, code, team_pin).first();
    await env.DB.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO group_roster_players (group_id, roster_id, sort_order) VALUES (?, ?, 0)').bind(group.id, memberId).run();
    await env.DB.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO group_roster_players (group_id, roster_id, sort_order) VALUES (?, ?, 1)').bind(group.id, guestId).run();
    created_teams++;
  }
  return json({ ok: true, created_teams, skipped_teams, linked_members, created_members, created_guests, matched_guests, linked_guests, details }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleSubmitTeeShot(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.group_code || !body?.hole || !body?.player_name || !body?.recorded_by) {
    return err('group_code, hole, player_name, and recorded_by required', 400, origin);
  }
  const hole = Number(body.hole);
  if (!Number.isInteger(hole) || hole < 1 || hole > 18) return err('hole must be an integer between 1 and 18', 400, origin);
  const group = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT g.id AS group_id, g.event_id, e.status FROM groups g JOIN events e ON e.id = g.event_id WHERE g.code = ?`
  ).bind(body.group_code.toUpperCase()).first();
  if (!group) return err('Invalid group code', 404, origin);
  if (group.status !== 'active') return err('Event is not active', 409, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO tee_shots (event_id, group_id, hole, player_name, recorded_by, updated_at)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
     ON CONFLICT(group_id, hole)
     DO UPDATE SET player_name = excluded.player_name, recorded_by = excluded.recorded_by, updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
  ).bind(group.event_id, group.group_id, hole, body.player_name.trim(), body.recorded_by.trim()).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetGroupTeeShots(req, env, origin, groupId) {
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT hole, player_name, recorded_by, updated_at FROM tee_shots WHERE group_id = ? ORDER BY hole'
  ).bind(groupId).all();
  return json({ tee_shots: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handlePasskeyRegister(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.credential_id) return err('credential_id required', 400, origin);
  // v2.77.0 B-17 — N passkeys per account (one per device). Conflict key is
  // credential_id (UNIQUE after the v2.77.0 migration): re-registering the same
  // device is idempotent; a NEW device INSERTS a new row instead of stealing the
  // old device's credential. REQUIRES the v2.77.0 passkeys table rebuild.
  await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO passkeys (account_id, credential_id, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'))
     ON CONFLICT(credential_id) DO UPDATE SET account_id = excluded.account_id, created_at = excluded.created_at`
  ).bind(account.id, body.credential_id).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handlePasskeyLogin(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.credential_id || !body?.email) return err('credential_id and email required', 400, origin);
  const account = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, email, name, club FROM accounts WHERE email = ?').bind(body.email.trim().toLowerCase()).first();
  if (!account) return err('Account not found', 401, origin);
  const passkey = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM passkeys WHERE account_id = ? AND credential_id = ?').bind(account.id, body.credential_id).first();
  if (!passkey) return err('Passkey not recognised', 401, origin);
  const token = await createSession(env, account.id);
  return json({ token, organizer: { id: account.id, email: account.email, name: account.name, club: account.club } }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDrawParse(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.pdf_base64) return err('pdf_base64 required', 400, origin);
  if (!env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) return err('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY not configured', 500, origin);
  const prompt = 'Extract the golf tournament draw from this document. Return ONLY a JSON array, no markdown, no explanation. Each element: {"hole": <number>, "captain": "<full name>", "players": ["<full name>", "<full name>", "<full name>"]}. The captain is the first/lead player per team. Include exactly 3 other players per team. If a column is labelled Seconds/Thirds/Anchors those are the other 3 players in order.';
  const resp = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', max_tokens: 2000, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'document', source: { type: 'base64', media_type: 'application/pdf', data: body.pdf_base64 } }, { type: 'text', text: prompt }] }] })
  });
  const data = await resp.json();
  const text = data?.content?.[0]?.text || '';
  try {
    const clean = text.replace(/```json|```/g, '').trim();
    const teams = JSON.parse(clean);
    return json({ teams }, 200, origin);
  } catch(e) {
    return err('Could not parse Claude response: ' + text.slice(0, 200), 500, origin);
  }
}

// v2.63.0 — THE unscoped roster match (upsert + import both use it). Guards
// event-scoped MMG guest rows out of permanent-roster matching so an unscoped
// upsert of "Sean Hill" can never capture-and-mutate an event's guest row.
// Legacy retry keeps both paths alive if the column migration hasn't run yet.
async function rosterMatchUnscoped(env, accountId, ghin, name) {
  try {
    let existing = null;
    if (ghin) existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND ghin = ? AND event_scope_id IS NULL').bind(accountId, ghin).first();
    if (!existing) existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE AND event_scope_id IS NULL').bind(accountId, name).first();
    return existing;
  } catch (_mmgCols) {
    let existing = null;
    if (ghin) existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND ghin = ?').bind(accountId, ghin).first();
    if (!existing) existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE').bind(accountId, name).first();
    return existing;
  }
}

async function handleRosterList(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const q = (url.searchParams.get('q') || '').trim();
  const limit = Math.min(Number(url.searchParams.get('limit') || 100), 500);
  const eventScope = url.searchParams.get('event_scope');                  // v2.63.0 — list ONE event's scoped guests
  let result;
  try {                                                                    // v2.63.0 — scoped-guest filter; legacy retry keeps the roster alive pre-migration
    const scopeSql = eventScope ? 'event_scope_id = ?' : 'event_scope_id IS NULL';
    const cols = 'id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, age, age_recorded_at, home_club, email, notes, buddy, event_scope_id, created_at, updated_at';
    if (q) {
      const vals = eventScope ? [account.id, Number(eventScope), `%${q}%`, limit] : [account.id, `%${q}%`, limit];
      result = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT ${cols} FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND ${scopeSql} AND name LIKE ? ORDER BY buddy DESC, name LIMIT ?`
      ).bind(...vals).all();
    } else {
      const vals = eventScope ? [account.id, Number(eventScope), limit] : [account.id, limit];
      result = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT ${cols} FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND ${scopeSql} ORDER BY buddy DESC, name LIMIT ?`
      ).bind(...vals).all();
    }
  } catch (_mmgCols) {
    if (q) {
      result = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, age, age_recorded_at, home_club, email, notes, buddy, created_at, updated_at
         FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND name LIKE ? ORDER BY buddy DESC, name LIMIT ?`
      ).bind(account.id, `%${q}%`, limit).all();
    } else {
      result = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, age, age_recorded_at, home_club, email, notes, buddy, created_at, updated_at
         FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? ORDER BY buddy DESC, name LIMIT ?`
      ).bind(account.id, limit).all();
    }
  }
  return json({ players: result.results, total: result.results.length }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleRosterUpsert(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.name) return err('name required', 400, origin);
  const name = body.name.trim(), ghin = body.ghin ? String(body.ghin).trim() : null;
  const gender = body.gender || null, handicap = body.handicap ?? null;
  const home_club = body.home_club || null, email = body.email ? body.email.trim().toLowerCase() : null;
  const notes = body.notes || null;
  const buddy = body.buddy != null ? (body.buddy ? 1 : 0) : null;
  const age = body.age != null && body.age !== '' ? Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(body.age))) || null : null;  // v2.38.0
  const eventScopeId = body.event_scope_id != null ? Number(body.event_scope_id) : null;  // v2.63.0 — MMG event-scoped guests
  if (eventScopeId != null) {
    let existing = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT id FROM roster WHERE account_id = ? AND event_scope_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE'
    ).bind(account.id, eventScopeId, name).first();
    if (existing) {
      const updates = ['name = ?', "updated_at = datetime('now')"]; const vals = [name];
      if (handicap !== null) { updates.push('handicap = ?'); vals.push(handicap); }
      if (gender   !== null) { updates.push('gender = ?');   vals.push(gender); }
      if (notes    !== null) { updates.push('notes = ?');    vals.push(notes); }
      vals.push(existing.id, account.id);
      const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE roster SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...vals).first();
      return json({ player: updated, created: false }, 200, origin);
    }
    const player = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO roster (account_id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, home_club, email, notes, buddy, event_scope_id)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?) RETURNING *`
    ).bind(account.id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, home_club, email, notes, eventScopeId).first();
    return json({ player, created: true }, 201, origin);
  }
  const existing = await rosterMatchUnscoped(env, account.id, ghin, name);  // v2.63.0 — scoped-guest-safe match
  if (existing) {
    const updates = ['name = ?', "updated_at = datetime('now')"]; const vals = [name];
    if (ghin      !== null) { updates.push('ghin = ?');      vals.push(ghin); }
    if (gender    !== null) { updates.push('gender = ?');    vals.push(gender); }
    if (handicap  !== null) { updates.push('handicap = ?');  vals.push(handicap); }
    if (home_club !== null) { updates.push('home_club = ?'); vals.push(home_club); }
    if (email     !== null) { updates.push('email = ?');     vals.push(email); }
    if (notes     !== null) { updates.push('notes = ?');     vals.push(notes); }
    if (buddy     !== null) { updates.push('buddy = ?');     vals.push(buddy); }
    if (age       !== null) { updates.push('age = ?', "age_recorded_at = datetime('now')"); vals.push(age); }  // v2.38.0
    vals.push(existing.id, account.id);
    const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE roster SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...vals).first();
    return json({ player: updated, created: false }, 200, origin);
  } else {
    const player = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO roster (account_id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, home_club, email, notes, buddy, age, age_recorded_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CASE WHEN ? IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE datetime('now') END) RETURNING *`
    ).bind(account.id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, home_club, email, notes, buddy || 0, age, age).first();
    return json({ player, created: true }, 201, origin);
  }
}

async function handleRosterImport(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.players || !Array.isArray(body.players)) return err('players[] required', 400, origin);
  if (body.players.length > 500) return err('Max 500 players per import', 400, origin);
  let created = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;
  for (const row of body.players) {
    if (!row.name) { skipped++; continue; }
    const name = String(row.name).trim(), ghin = row.ghin ? String(row.ghin).trim() : null;
    const gender = row.gender ? String(row.gender).trim() : null;
    const handicap = row.handicap != null ? Number(row.handicap) : null;
    const club = row.home_club || row.club || null;
    const email = row.email ? String(row.email).trim().toLowerCase() : null;
    const age = row.age != null && row.age !== '' ? Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(row.age))) || null : null;  // v2.38.0
    if (!name) { skipped++; continue; }
    const existing = await rosterMatchUnscoped(env, account.id, ghin, name);  // v2.63.0 — scoped-guest-safe match
    if (existing) {
      const upds = ['name = ?', "updated_at = datetime('now')"]; const vals = [name];
      if (ghin     !== null) { upds.push('ghin = ?');      vals.push(ghin); }
      if (gender   !== null) { upds.push('gender = ?');    vals.push(gender); }
      if (handicap !== null) { upds.push('handicap = ?');  vals.push(handicap); }
      if (club     !== null) { upds.push('home_club = ?'); vals.push(club); }
      if (email    !== null) { upds.push('email = ?');     vals.push(email); }
      if (age      !== null) { upds.push('age = ?', "age_recorded_at = datetime('now')"); vals.push(age); }  // v2.38.0
      vals.push(existing.id, account.id);
      await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE roster SET ${upds.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ?`).bind(...vals).run();
      updated++;
    } else {
      await env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO roster (account_id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, home_club, email, age, age_recorded_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CASE WHEN ? IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE datetime('now') END)`
      ).bind(account.id, name, ghin, gender, handicap, club, email, age, age).run();
      created++;
    }
  }
  return json({ ok: true, created, updated, skipped }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleRosterUpdate(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!existing) return err('Player not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const updates = [], values = [];
  if (body.name      != null) { updates.push('name = ?');      values.push(String(body.name).trim()); }
  if (body.ghin      != null) { updates.push('ghin = ?');      values.push(String(body.ghin).trim()); }
  if (body.gender    != null) { updates.push('gender = ?');    values.push(body.gender); }
  if (body.handicap  != null) { updates.push('handicap = ?');  values.push(Number(body.handicap)); }
  if (body.home_club != null) { updates.push('home_club = ?'); values.push(body.home_club); }
  if (body.email     != null) { updates.push('email = ?');     values.push(body.email.trim().toLowerCase()); }
  if (body.notes     != null) { updates.push('notes = ?');     values.push(body.notes); }
  if (body.buddy     != null) { updates.push('buddy = ?');     values.push(body.buddy ? 1 : 0); }
  if (body.age       != null) { updates.push('age = ?', "age_recorded_at = datetime('now')"); values.push(Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(body.age))) || null); }  // v2.38.0
  if (!updates.length) return err('Nothing to update', 400, origin);
  updates.push("updated_at = datetime('now')");
  values.push(id, account.id);
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE roster SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...values).first();
  return json({ player: updated }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleRosterDelete(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const result = await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ? RETURNING id').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!result) return err('Player not found', 404, origin);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: result.id }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleRosterHistory(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const player = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!player) return err('Player not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: links } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT rel.event_id, rel.player_id, e.name AS event_name, e.date, e.course_name, e.format, g.name AS group_name
     FROM roster_event_links rel JOIN events e ON e.id = rel.event_id
     JOIN players p ON p.id = rel.player_id JOIN groups g ON g.id = p.group_id
     WHERE rel.roster_id = ? ORDER BY e.date DESC`
  ).bind(id).all();
  const history = [];
  for (const link of links) {
    const { results: scores } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT hole, gross_score FROM scores WHERE player_id = ? ORDER BY hole`
    ).bind(link.player_id).all();
    const total = scores.reduce((a, s) => a + s.gross_score, 0);
    history.push({
      event_id: link.event_id, event_name: link.event_name, date: link.date,
      course_name: link.course_name, format: link.format, group_name: link.group_name,
      holes_played: scores.length, total_strokes: total,
      scores: Object.fromEntries(scores.map(s => [s.hole, s.gross_score])),
    });
  }
  return json({ player, history }, 200, origin);
}

// ── Round handlers ────────────────────────────────────────────
async function handleListRounds(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  let event;
  if (account) {
    event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  } else {
    event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
  }
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  // v2.46.0 — per-round scoring progress for the round-card badge.
  // v2.75.0 — THE TWO SCORE COUNTERS LOSE THEIR FORMAT GATE. v2.46.0 scoped them
  // to individual_stableford because `scores.group_id` is polymorphic — a
  // tee_times.id on the tee-sheet path, a groups.id from handleSubmitScores — and
  // the two sequences are independent, so joining scores -> tee_times would
  // mis-attribute a team round. scores.round_id (v2.73.0) removes the need to join
  // at all, so team rounds get a real count instead of NULL. Measured s51: the new
  // counters return exactly today's numbers on every individual round (4/4, 16/16,
  // 15/15, 0/0) and turn event 49 round 21 from NULL into 1 scoring / 1 complete.
  // v2.77.0 — the gate widens from = 'individual_stableford' to the three
  // individual formats (D21): entry is identical for all three, only ranking
  // differs, so the field count is equally valid. Team rounds still fall through.
  // ⭐ field_players KEEPS ITS GATE, deliberately. Team tee-sheet slots carry
  // group_id with roster_id NULL, so `roster_id IS NOT NULL` counts 0 for them and
  // the badge would read "1 of 0 scoring". Counting group slots is a separate
  // decision (Bruce, s51: not taking it now). Those rounds keep the NULL field
  // count and the client keeps showing the existing "N groups set" badge.
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.*,
       CASE WHEN r.format IN ('individual_stableford','individual_low_net','individual_low_gross') THEN (
         SELECT COUNT(*) FROM tee_time_players ttp
         JOIN tee_times t1 ON t1.id = ttp.tee_time_id
         WHERE t1.round_id = r.id AND ttp.roster_id IS NOT NULL
       ) END AS field_players,
       (
         SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s.player_id) FROM scores s
         WHERE s.round_id = r.id AND s.gross_score > 0
       ) AS scoring_players,
       (
         SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
           SELECT s.player_id FROM scores s
           WHERE s.round_id = r.id AND s.gross_score > 0
           GROUP BY s.player_id HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT s.hole) >= 18
         )
       ) AS complete_players
     FROM rounds r WHERE r.event_id = ? ORDER BY r.round_number ASC`
  ).bind(eventId).all();
  return json({ rounds: results }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.79.0 — D41 (Bruce s71: "under simulate scores - can you add a reset all
// scores for all players/teams button"). Organizer session only. Client-side
// zeros were NOT an option: recomputeLeaderboard takes MIN per hole, so a 0
// row poisons every team total forever. Row deletion is the only clean reset.
// Deletes the same set handleDeleteRound cleans up (round_id leg + the
// tee-time group_id leg), then recomputes both caches so the boards empty
// immediately. Rows with NULL round_id in roundless events are untouched —
// those events have no round to reset.
async function handleDeleteRoundScores(req, env, origin, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id, r.event_id, r.status FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id
      WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(roundId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  if (round.status === 'locked') return err('Round is locked \u2014 unlock it before resetting scores.', 409, origin);
  const del = await env.DB.prepare(
    `DELETE FROM scores WHERE round_id = ?1 OR group_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?1)`
  ).bind(roundId).run();
  // ⭐ v2.90.0 — B-79: every reset advances the round's reset_epoch so a phone
  // holding a pre-reset card can be refused at post time (both submit
  // handlers). Guarded: before the migration runs, the reset behaves exactly
  // as v2.79.0 did and reset_epoch reports null.
  let newEpoch = null;
  try {
    await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE rounds SET reset_epoch = COALESCE(reset_epoch, 0) + 1 WHERE id = ?').bind(roundId).run();
    const _re = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT reset_epoch FROM rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(roundId).first();
    newEpoch = (_re && _re.reset_epoch != null) ? Number(_re.reset_epoch) : null;
  } catch (_b79r) { newEpoch = null; }
  await recomputeLeaderboard(env, round.event_id);
  await recomputeRoundLeaderboard(env, roundId);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted: (del && del.meta && del.meta.changes) || 0, round_id: roundId, event_id: round.event_id, reset_epoch: newEpoch }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleCreateRound(req, env, origin, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(eventId, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const count = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM rounds WHERE event_id = ?').bind(eventId).first();
  if (count.n >= 4) return err('Maximum 4 rounds per event', 400, origin);
  const round_number = (count.n || 0) + 1;
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO rounds (event_id, round_number, date, course_id, tee, format, status)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft') RETURNING *`
  ).bind(eventId, body.round_number || round_number, body.date || null, body.course_id || null, body.tee || null,
       (body.format === '' ? '' : (body.format || 'individual_stableford'))).first(); // v2.94.0 — FR-32: '' is a real choice ("no games"), not a missing one
  return json({ round }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleUpdateRound(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  // v2.72.0 — Q1 needs r.format and r.event_id to scope the score count. Was `SELECT r.id`.
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id, r.course_id, r.tee, r.format, r.event_id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  // v2.72.0 — Q1 FIELD LOCKING, ROUND HALF (Bruce, session 47). The IDENTICAL
  // defect to handleUpdateEvent, on a handler nobody had checked: this guarded
  // on `SELECT r.id` alone and then PATCHed course_id, tee and format with no
  // score count. Changing a round's course after cards are in re-computes every
  // net score IN THAT ROUND.
  //
  // ⭐⭐ v2.75.0 — ONE COUNT, NOT TWO BRANCHES. v2.72.0 could only count exactly on
  // individual_stableford rounds, because scores.group_id is polymorphic with no
  // discriminator — a tee_times.id on the tee-sheet path, a groups.id from
  // handleSubmitScores — so team formats fell back to an EVENT-WIDE count.
  // ⭐⭐⭐ THAT FALLBACK WAS NOT AN UNDER-COUNT. It is a strict SUPERSET of the
  // round's own scores, so it could never permit an edit it should refuse. It
  // OVER-BLOCKED: round 21 of event 49 has 18 scores and was quoted 90, and a team
  // round with none of its own was un-editable whenever a SIBLING round had scores.
  // scores.round_id (v2.73.0) makes the exact count available to every format.
  // ⭐ THE LEGACY LEG STAYS. `group_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times ...)` still
  // catches any row written before round_id was stamped, which is what makes this
  // NEVER LESS STRICT on an individual round. Measured s51 — identical counts on
  // all five individual rounds that have scores; only round 21 changes, 90 -> 18.
  // ⭐⭐ R1: a team round with no scores of its own now ALLOWS what it refused.
  // That is the point, and it is the irreversible direction. Safe because zero
  // NULL-round_id score rows belong to an event that has any rounds (measured s51).
  const LOCKED_ROUND_FIELDS = ['course_id', 'tee', 'format'];
  // v2.91.0 (B-84): lock compares stored vs sent — presence alone no longer trips it (old clients echo unchanged values)
  const lockedTouched = LOCKED_ROUND_FIELDS.filter(f => body[f] != null && String(body[f] || '') !== String(round[f] || ''));
  if (lockedTouched.length) {
    const sc = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM scores s
        WHERE s.event_id = ? AND s.gross_score > 0
          AND (s.round_id = ? OR s.group_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?))`
    ).bind(round.event_id, id, id).first();
    if (sc && sc.n > 0) {
      return err('This round has ' + sc.n +
        ' posted scores — ' + lockedTouched.join(', ') + ' can no longer be changed, because they ' +
        're-compute every net score already entered. Clear the scores first.', 409, origin);
    }
  }
  const updates = [], values = [];
  if (body.date         != null) { updates.push('date = ?');         values.push(body.date); }
  if (body.course_id    != null) { updates.push('course_id = ?');    values.push(body.course_id); }
  if (body.tee          != null) { updates.push('tee = ?');          values.push(body.tee); }
  if (body.format       != null) { updates.push('format = ?');       values.push(body.format); }
  if (body.status       != null) { updates.push('status = ?');       values.push(body.status); }
  if (body.round_number != null) { updates.push('round_number = ?'); values.push(Number(body.round_number)); }
  if (!updates.length) return err('Nothing to update', 400, origin);
  values.push(id);
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE rounds SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...values).first();
  return json({ round: updated }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDeleteRound(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  // v2.72.0 — needs r.format and r.event_id to scope the score guard. Was `SELECT r.id`.
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id, r.format, r.event_id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  // v2.72.0 — SCORES ARE SACRED, HERE TOO (Bruce, session 47). Same rule as Q1
  // and the trips side: a round with posted scores is not deletable. This also
  // makes the cleanup below unreachable in the only case where it could destroy
  // anything.
  // v2.75.0 — ONE COUNT, NOT TWO BRANCHES. Same query and same reasoning as the
  // handleUpdateRound guard above, including the legacy leg that keeps this from
  // ever becoming less strict on an individual round. See that comment in full.
  const delSc = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM scores s
      WHERE s.event_id = ? AND s.gross_score > 0
        AND (s.round_id = ? OR s.group_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?))`
  ).bind(round.event_id, id, id).first();
  if (delSc && delSc.n > 0) {
    return err('This round has ' + delSc.n + ' posted scores — remove the scores first.', 409, origin);
  }
  await env.DB.batch([
    // v2.72.0 — THE v2.33 CLEANUP MATCHED NOTHING. It read scores.group_id as a
    // tee_time_groups.id; the WRITE path binds a tee_times.id (handleSubmit...,
    // `.bind(eventId, teeTimeId, ...)`), and handleListRounds says the same.
    // Checked against live D1: of 1,522 score rows, 558 match a tee_times.id,
    // 964 match a groups.id, and ZERO match a tee_time_groups.id — so the old
    // DELETE was a no-op, and every deleted round left its scores behind,
    // still counting toward the event leaderboard. Exactly what it meant to stop.
    // ⭐ Joined on the correct sequence now. With the guard above, this only ever
    // runs on a round with nothing posted.
    // v2.73.0 — round_id = ? catches rows stamped by the new write paths; the
    // legacy group_id-in-tee_times clause stays so pre-backfill rows (and the
    // 964 that could never be attributed) are still swept. Two binds now.
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM scores WHERE round_id = ? OR group_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?)').bind(id, id),
    // ⭐ players is NOT cleaned here, on purpose. players.group_id is a groups.id
    // (live: 199 of 217 rows, 18 NULL, ZERO matching tee_times) and `groups` has
    // no round_id — so a player row cannot be attributed to a round at all. The
    // old line pretended otherwise and, like the scores line, matched nothing.
    // Cleaning it up needs per-round standings. Left explicit rather than fake.
    // v2.33: THE FIX — tee_time_groups rows must go before their tee_times
    // (enforced FK tee_time_groups.tee_time_id → tee_times.id).
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_time_groups WHERE tee_time_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id IN (SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM event_round_money WHERE round_id = ?').bind(id), // v2.93.0 — FR-26
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(id),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: id }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetTeeTimes(req, env, origin, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(roundId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: teeTimes } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ? ORDER BY start_time ASC, hole ASC'
  ).bind(roundId).all();
  const result = [];
  for (const tt of teeTimes) {
    const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT * FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY slot_order ASC'
    ).bind(tt.id).all();
    let ttg = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT code, pin FROM tee_time_groups WHERE tee_time_id = ?').bind(tt.id).first();
    if (!ttg) {
      let ttCode = null;
      for (let ci = 0; ci < 10; ci++) {
        const candidate = generateCode();
        const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM tee_time_groups WHERE code = ?').bind(candidate).first();
        if (!exists) { ttCode = candidate; break; }
      }
      if (ttCode) {
        await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_groups (tee_time_id, code, pin) VALUES (?, ?, NULL)').bind(tt.id, ttCode).run();
        ttg = { code: ttCode, pin: null };
      }
    }
    result.push({ ...tt, players, foursome_code: ttg?.code || null, foursome_pin: null });
  }
  return json({ tee_times: result }, 200, origin);
}

async function handlePatchTeeTimes(req, env, origin, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id, r.event_id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(roundId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.slots || !Array.isArray(body.slots)) return err('slots[] required', 400, origin);
  let changed = 0;
  let times_updated = 0;                                                   // v2.44.0
  for (const slot of body.slots) {
    const teeTimeId = slot.tee_time_id;
