    if (!teeTimeId) continue;
    const tt = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, hole, start_time FROM tee_times WHERE id = ? AND round_id = ?').bind(teeTimeId, roundId).first();
    if (!tt) continue;
    // v2.44.0 — apply time edits BEFORE the membership-signature short-circuit,
    // so a time-only change persists without rotating the foursome code.
    if (slot.start_time && slot.start_time !== tt.start_time) {
      await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE tee_times SET start_time = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(slot.start_time, teeTimeId).run();
      times_updated++;
    }
    const { results: currentPlayers } = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT roster_id, group_id, slot_order FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY slot_order'
    ).bind(teeTimeId).all();
    const currentSig = currentPlayers.map(p => (p.roster_id ? 'r'+p.roster_id : 'g'+p.group_id)).sort().join(',');
    const newPlayers = (slot.players || []).filter(Boolean);
    const newSig = newPlayers.map(p => (p.roster_id ? 'r'+p.roster_id : 'g'+p.group_id)).sort().join(',');
    if (currentSig === newSig) continue;
    await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id = ?').bind(teeTimeId).run();
    const stmts = newPlayers.map((p, i) =>
      p.roster_id
        ? env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_players (tee_time_id, roster_id, slot_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(teeTimeId, p.roster_id, i)
        : env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_players (tee_time_id, group_id, slot_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(teeTimeId, p.group_id, i)
    );
    if (stmts.length) await env.DB.batch(stmts);
    let newCode = null;
    for (let ci = 0; ci < 10; ci++) {
      const candidate = generateCode();
      const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM tee_time_groups WHERE code = ?').bind(candidate).first();
      if (!exists) { newCode = candidate; break; }
    }
    if (newCode) {
      await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_time_groups WHERE tee_time_id = ?').bind(teeTimeId).run();
      await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_groups (tee_time_id, code, pin) VALUES (?, ?, NULL)').bind(teeTimeId, newCode).run();
    }
    for (const p of newPlayers) {
      if (p.group_id) {
        await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE groups SET start_hole = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(tt.hole || 1, p.group_id).run();
      }
    }
    changed++;
  }
  return json({ ok: true, changed, times_updated }, 200, origin);   // v2.44.0
}

async function handleSaveTeeTimes(req, env, origin, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id, r.event_id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(roundId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.slots || !Array.isArray(body.slots)) return err('slots[] required', 400, origin);
  const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?').bind(roundId).all();
  if (existing.results.length) {
    const ids = existing.results.map(r => r.id);
    await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM tee_time_groups WHERE tee_time_id IN (${ids.map(()=>'?').join(',')})`).bind(...ids).run();
    await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id IN (${ids.map(()=>'?').join(',')})`).bind(...ids).run();
    await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?').bind(roundId).run();
  }
  if (body.session || body.mode) {
    const upds = [], vals = [];
    if (body.session) { upds.push('session = ?'); vals.push(body.session); }
    if (body.mode)    { upds.push('mode = ?');    vals.push(body.mode); }
    if (upds.length) { vals.push(roundId); await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE rounds SET ${upds.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).bind(...vals).run(); }
  }
  let inserted = 0;
  for (const slot of body.slots) {
    if (!slot.players || slot.players.filter(Boolean).length === 0) continue;
    const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO tee_times (round_id, start_time, hole) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
    ).bind(roundId, slot.start_time || '', slot.hole || 1).first();
    let ttCode = null;
    for (let ci = 0; ci < 10; ci++) {
      const candidate = generateCode();
      const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM tee_time_groups WHERE code = ?').bind(candidate).first();
      if (!exists) { ttCode = candidate; break; }
    }
    if (ttCode) {
      await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_groups (tee_time_id, code, pin) VALUES (?, ?, NULL)').bind(tt.id, ttCode).run();
    }
    const playerStmts = [];
    (slot.players || []).forEach(function(p, i) {
      if (!p) return;
      const stmt = p.roster_id
        ? env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_players (tee_time_id, roster_id, slot_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(tt.id, p.roster_id, i)
        : env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO tee_time_players (tee_time_id, group_id, slot_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(tt.id, p.group_id, i);
      playerStmts.push(stmt);
    });
    if (playerStmts.length) await env.DB.batch(playerStmts);
    for (const p of (slot.players || []).filter(Boolean)) {
      if (p.group_id) {
        await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE groups SET start_hole = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(slot.hole || 1, p.group_id).run();
      }
    }
    inserted++;
  }
  return json({ ok: true, inserted }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleClearTeeTimes(req, env, origin, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT r.id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ? AND e.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(roundId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?').bind(roundId).all();
  if (existing.results.length) {
    const ids = existing.results.map(r => r.id);
    await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id IN (${ids.map(()=>'?').join(',')})`).bind(...ids).run();
    await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM tee_times WHERE round_id = ?').bind(roundId).run();
  }
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleJoinRound(req, env, origin, code) {
  const ttg = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM tee_time_groups WHERE code = ?').bind(code).first();
  if (!ttg) return err('Invalid code', 404, origin);
  // No PIN on event tee-time codes — the 6-digit code alone grants access.
  const pinValid = true;
  const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT t.*, r.id AS round_id, r.round_number, r.date, r.course_id, r.tee,
            r.format, r.session, r.mode, r.status AS round_status,
            e.id AS event_id, e.name AS event_name, e.course_id AS ev_course_id, e.tee AS ev_tee,
            e.status AS event_status, e.config AS event_config, e.event_code AS ev_watch_code
     FROM tee_times t JOIN rounds r ON r.id = t.round_id JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id
     WHERE t.id = ?`
  ).bind(ttg.tee_time_id).first();
  if (!tt) return err('Tee time not found', 404, origin);
  // v2.51.0 — a closed event stops the golfer at the CODE SCREEN, exactly like
  // the v2.46.0 lock below. Without this, an archived event's code handed out
  // a card whose every save was then 409'd by handleSubmitRoundScores — the
  // one state the client's retry loop could never escape. Checked BEFORE the
  // lock: "the event is over" outranks "this round is locked".
  if (tt.event_status !== 'active') {
    const ended = tt.event_status === 'complete' || tt.event_status === 'archived';
    return err(ended
      ? 'This event has ended \u2014 scoring is closed.'
      : 'Scoring is not open yet \u2014 the organizer will activate the event before play begins.',
      409, origin, { closed: true, event_status: tt.event_status, round_number: tt.round_number, event_name: tt.event_name });
  }
  // v2.46.0 — the lock now stops the golfer at the CODE SCREEN. v2.45.0 only
  // rejected the submit, so a locked round still handed out a working card and
  // the "Round in Progress" resume cue told the player to carry on — they
  // re-entered 18 holes before anything failed. 409 (not 404) matters: the
  // client falls through to the trip-code lookup on 404 only.
  if (tt.round_status === 'locked') {
    return err('Scoring is locked for this round \u2014 see the organizer.', 409, origin, { locked: true, round_number: tt.round_number, event_name: tt.event_name });
  }
  const { results: ttpRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM tee_time_players WHERE tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY slot_order ASC'
  ).bind(ttg.tee_time_id).all();
  const players = [];
  for (const row of ttpRows) {
    if (row.roster_id) {
      const rp = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT r.id, r.name, r.handicap, r.ghin,
                g.id AS group_id, g.name AS team_name, g.team_handicap
         FROM roster r
         LEFT JOIN group_roster_players grp ON grp.roster_id = r.id
         LEFT JOIN groups g ON g.id = grp.group_id AND g.event_id = ?
         WHERE r.id = ?`
      ).bind(tt.event_id, row.roster_id).first();
      if (rp) players.push({ roster_id: rp.id, name: rp.name, handicap: rp.handicap, ghin: rp.ghin, group_id: rp.group_id, team_name: rp.team_name, group_code: null });
    } else if (row.group_id) {
      const grp = await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT id, name, code, team_pin, team_handicap, course_id, tee FROM groups WHERE id = ?'
      ).bind(row.group_id).first();
      if (grp) {
        const { results: grpPlayers } = await env.DB.prepare(
          `SELECT r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap FROM group_roster_players gp
           JOIN roster r ON r.id = gp.roster_id WHERE gp.group_id = ?
           ORDER BY gp.sort_order ASC, r.name ASC`
        ).bind(row.group_id).all();
        players.push({
          roster_id: null, group_id: grp.id, name: grp.name, group_code: grp.code,
          group_pin: grp.team_pin, team_handicap: grp.team_handicap, handicap: grp.team_handicap,
          ghin: null, team_name: grp.name, team_players: grpPlayers,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  const existingScores = {};
  for (const p of players) {
    if (!p.roster_id) continue;
    const { results: scores } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT s.hole, s.gross_score FROM scores s JOIN players pl ON pl.id = s.player_id
       WHERE pl.event_id = ? AND pl.name = ? COLLATE NOCASE AND s.group_id = ? ORDER BY s.hole`
    ).bind(tt.event_id, p.name, tt.id).all();
    existingScores[p.roster_id] = {};
    scores.forEach(function(s) { existingScores[p.roster_id][s.hole] = s.gross_score; });
  }
  const effective_course_id = tt.course_id || tt.ev_course_id || null;
  const effective_tee = tt.tee || tt.ev_tee || null;
  // v2.86.0 — D69: the card already knows its course_id; naming it here saves
  // every lite phone a second request on card open. ⭐ The ROUND's course wins
  // over the event's (a multi-course trip round is a different course), so the
  // name is looked up from the EFFECTIVE id, never from events.course_name.
  // Fail-soft: no row -> null, and clients that already fetch simply keep doing it.
  let effective_course_name = null;
  try {
    if (effective_course_id) {
      const _c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT name FROM courses WHERE id = ?').bind(effective_course_id).first();
      effective_course_name = _c?.name || null;
    }
  } catch (_cn) { effective_course_name = null; }
  // v2.81.0 — D49: the round's Card Swap policy rides the join payload so the
  // scorer's phone can show marker rotation ("A marks B · B marks C · last
  // marks A" from slot_order) without organizer auth. Absent/invalid → null,
  // exactly what pre-v397 clients ignore.
  let cardSwap = null;
  try {
    const _jrCfg = typeof tt.event_config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(tt.event_config || '{}') : (tt.event_config || {});
    const _jrSw = _jrCfg.ttb_swap ? _jrCfg.ttb_swap[String(tt.round_id)] : null;
    if (_jrSw && ['none', 'pointer', 'pair', 'marker'].includes(_jrSw.mode)) cardSwap = _jrSw.mode;
  } catch (_jrSwErr) { cardSwap = null; }
  return json({
    foursome: {
      id: ttg.tee_time_id, code: ttg.code, start_time: tt.start_time, hole: tt.hole,
      round_number: tt.round_number, round_id: tt.round_id, date: tt.date,
      course_id: effective_course_id, tee: effective_tee, format: tt.format,
      course_name: effective_course_name,                              // v2.86.0 — D69
      session: tt.session, event_id: tt.event_id, event_name: tt.event_name,
      round_status: tt.round_status,                                   // v2.46.0
      mode: tt.mode || 'tee_times',                                    // v2.85.0 — B-64: shotgun flag for the lite ticker (r.mode was in the SELECT all along)
      card_swap: cardSwap,                                             // v2.81.0 — D49
      event_code: tt.ev_watch_code || null,                            // v2.82.0 — lite ticker: join-round finally names its event's watch code (join has always had it); the joined events row is already in hand, zero extra reads
    },
    players, existing_scores: existingScores, pin_valid: pinValid,
  }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleSubmitRoundScores(req, env, origin) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.group_code || !body?.player_name || !Array.isArray(body.scores)) {
    return err('group_code, player_name, and scores[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  if (!body.scores.length) return err('scores[] must not be empty', 400, origin);
  const ttg = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT ttg.*, tt.round_id, tt.id AS tee_time_id FROM tee_time_groups ttg JOIN tee_times tt ON tt.id = ttg.tee_time_id WHERE ttg.code = ?'
  ).bind(body.group_code.toUpperCase()).first();
  if (!ttg) return err('Invalid foursome code', 404, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT r.*, e.id AS event_id, e.status AS event_status, e.organizer_id FROM rounds r JOIN events e ON e.id = r.event_id WHERE r.id = ?'
  ).bind(ttg.round_id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  if (round.event_status !== 'active') return err('Event is not active', 409, origin);
  if (round.status === 'locked') return err('Scoring is locked for this round — see the organizer.', 409, origin); // v2.45.0
  // ⭐ v2.90.0 — B-79: a phone that sat open across an organizer "Reset ALL
  // scores" must not silently repost the stale card. Clients that send
  // reset_epoch (learned at join, client half not yet built) are refused when
  // the round's epoch has moved past theirs; clients that don't send it —
  // every client today — are accepted unchanged. Pre-migration,
  // round.reset_epoch is undefined (round is r.*) and the check self-disables.
  if (Number.isFinite(body.reset_epoch) && round.reset_epoch != null && Number(round.reset_epoch) > Number(body.reset_epoch)) {
    return err('Scores were reset by the organizer after this card was opened — leave and rejoin the round before posting again.', 409, origin);
  }
  const eventId = round.event_id;
  const teeTimeId = ttg.tee_time_id;
  let player;
  if (body.group_id) {
    player = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT id FROM players WHERE event_id = ? AND group_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1'
    ).bind(eventId, body.group_id, body.player_name.trim()).first();
    if (!player) {
      player = await env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO players (event_id, group_id, name, handicap, tee) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING id`
      ).bind(eventId, body.group_id, body.player_name.trim(), body.handicap ?? 0, body.tee || 'white').first();
    }
  } else {
    player = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT id FROM players WHERE event_id = ? AND group_id IS NULL AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE LIMIT 1'
    ).bind(eventId, body.player_name.trim()).first();
    if (!player) {
      player = await env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO players (event_id, group_id, name, handicap, tee) VALUES (?, NULL, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING id`
      ).bind(eventId, body.player_name.trim(), body.handicap ?? 0, body.tee || 'white').first();
    }
  }
  if (body.roster_id) {
    await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roster_event_links (roster_id, event_id, player_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)`
    ).bind(body.roster_id, eventId, player.id).run();
    // v2.95.0 -- B-100: same stamp on the tee-time card path. See site A.
    try {
      await env.DB.prepare(
        'UPDATE players SET roster_id = ? WHERE id = ? AND roster_id IS NULL'
      ).bind(body.roster_id, player.id).run();
    } catch (_b100b) { /* column absent -- pre-migration, accept */ }
  }
  const stmts = body.scores.map(s =>
    env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO scores (event_id, group_id, player_id, hole, gross_score, round_id, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
       ON CONFLICT(player_id, hole, group_id)
       DO UPDATE SET gross_score = excluded.gross_score, round_id = excluded.round_id, updated_at = excluded.updated_at`
    // ⛔ v2.99.0 (B-121) — NAMESPACE B: group_id here is a `tee_times.id`, NOT a
    // `groups.id`. handleSubmitScores writes the other namespace into the same
    // un-keyed column. This is why recomputeLeaderboard now refuses to build a
    // group board for these rows rather than joining them to the wrong table.
    ).bind(eventId, teeTimeId, player.id, s.hole, s.gross_score, ttg.round_id)
  );
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  await recomputeLeaderboard(env, eventId);
  await recomputeRoundLeaderboard(env, ttg.round_id);   // v2.76.0 — additive, fail-soft, AFTER the event cache
  // Auto-settle bets if applicable
  await maybeAutoSettleBetsRace(env, eventId, ttg.round_id, null, null);
  return json({ ok: true, player_id: player.id, event_id: eventId, round_id: ttg.round_id }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// COURSE LIBRARY HANDLERS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function handleListCourses(req, env, origin) {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const q = (url.searchParams.get('q') || '').trim();
  const province = url.searchParams.get('province') || null;
  const country  = url.searchParams.get('country')  || null;
  let sql = 'SELECT id, name, city, province, country, par, si FROM courses';
  const conds = [], vals = [];
  if (q)        { conds.push('name LIKE ?');     vals.push(`%${q}%`); }
  if (province) { conds.push('province = ?');    vals.push(province); }
  if (country)  { conds.push('country = ?');     vals.push(country); }
  if (conds.length) sql += ' WHERE ' + conds.join(' AND ');
  sql += ' ORDER BY name';
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...vals).all();
  return json({ courses: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetCourse(req, env, origin, id) {
  const course = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, name, city, province, country, par, si FROM courses WHERE id = ?'
  ).bind(id).first();
  if (!course) return err('Course not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: tees } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM course_tees WHERE course_id = ? ORDER BY rating DESC'
  ).bind(id).all();
  return json({
    course: {
      ...course,
      par:  course.par  ? JSON.parse(course.par)  : null,
      si:   course.si   ? JSON.parse(course.si)   : null,
      tees: tees.map(t => ({
        ...t,
        pars:      t.pars      ? JSON.parse(t.pars)      : null,
        yards:     t.yards     ? JSON.parse(t.yards)     : null,
        handicaps: t.handicaps ? JSON.parse(t.handicaps) : null,
      })),
    }
  }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TRIP HANDLERS
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function uniqueTripCode(env) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const code = generateCode();
    if (!await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE trip_code = ?').bind(code).first()) return code;
  }
  throw new Error('Could not generate unique trip code');
}

function calcCourseHandicapFull(index, slope, rating, par) {
  return Math.round(index * (slope / 113) + (rating - par));
}

async function handleCreateTrip(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.name) return err('name required', 400, origin);
  const trip_code = await uniqueTripCode(env);
  const viewer_pin = generatePin();
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trips (organizer_id, name, destination, start_date, end_date, trip_code, viewer_pin, status)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft') RETURNING *`
  ).bind(account.id, body.name.trim(), body.destination || null, body.start_date || null, body.end_date || null, trip_code, viewer_pin).first();
  return json({ trip }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleListTrips(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT t.id, t.name, t.destination, t.start_date, t.end_date, t.trip_code, t.viewer_pin, t.status, t.created_at,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trip_rounds tr WHERE tr.trip_id = t.id) AS round_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trip_players tp WHERE tp.trip_id = t.id) AS player_count
     FROM trips t WHERE t.organizer_id = ? ORDER BY t.start_date DESC`
  ).bind(account.id).all();
  return json({ trips: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetTrip(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tp.id, tp.roster_id, tp.ghin, tp.current_index, tp.last_index_pull,
            r.name, r.handicap, r.home_club, r.email, r.buddy
     FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     WHERE tp.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
  ).bind(id).all();
  const { results: rounds } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.*, c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id = ? ORDER BY tr.round_number ASC`
  ).bind(id).all();
  // v2.92.0 — D84: trip game days (hidden events) ride BESIDE rounds, never
  // merged into them. Pre-migration (trip_events absent) this degrades to []
  // so the worker is safe even if pasted before the migration runs.
  let game_days = [];
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT e.id, e.name, e.date, e.status, e.event_code, e.created_at, te.added_at, te.config AS link_config
       FROM trip_events te JOIN events e ON e.id = te.event_id
       WHERE te.trip_id = ? ORDER BY e.date ASC, e.created_at ASC`
    ).bind(id).all();
    game_days = results || [];
  } catch (e) { game_days = []; }
  // v2.92.0 — FR-25: per-(round, player) side-game money for the trip ledger.
  let round_money = [];
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT trm.trip_round_id, trm.trip_player_id, trm.cents, trm.detail, trm.updated_at
       FROM trip_round_money trm JOIN trip_rounds tr ON tr.id = trm.trip_round_id
       WHERE tr.trip_id = ?`
    ).bind(id).all();
    round_money = results || [];
  } catch (e) { round_money = []; }
  // v2.93.0 — FR-26 phase 1: game-day (event) round money for the trip
  // ledger — same linkage game_days[] uses (trip_events → events → rounds).
  // Pre-migration (event_round_money absent) this degrades to [].
  let event_round_money = [];
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT erm.round_id, r.event_id, erm.player_id, p.name AS player_name,
       erm.cents, erm.detail, erm.updated_at
       FROM event_round_money erm
       JOIN rounds r ON r.id = erm.round_id
       JOIN players p ON p.id = erm.player_id
       JOIN trip_events te ON te.event_id = r.event_id
       WHERE te.trip_id = ?`
    ).bind(id).all();
    event_round_money = results || [];
  } catch (e) { event_round_money = []; }
  return json({ trip, players, rounds, game_days, round_money, event_round_money }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleUpdateTrip(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const updates = [], values = [];
  if (body.name        != null) { updates.push('name = ?');        values.push(body.name.trim()); }
  if (body.destination != null) { updates.push('destination = ?'); values.push(body.destination); }
  if (body.start_date  != null) { updates.push('start_date = ?');  values.push(body.start_date); }
  if (body.end_date    != null) { updates.push('end_date = ?');    values.push(body.end_date); }
  if (body.status      != null) { updates.push('status = ?');      values.push(body.status); }
  if (!updates.length) return err('Nothing to update', 400, origin);
  values.push(id, account.id);
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE trips SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...values).first();
  return json({ trip: updated }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.56.0 — THE trip cascade. One implementation per concern: this is the
// only place that knows every table hanging off a trip. handleDeleteTrip,
// handleDeleteLeagueRound and handleDeleteLeague all consume it — if a new
// trip-scoped table appears, add it HERE and every delete path is honest.
function tripCascade(env, id) {
  return [
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_nassau WHERE trip_round_id IN (SELECT id FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_round_id IN (SELECT id FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_tee_time_players WHERE trip_tee_time_id IN (SELECT tt.id FROM trip_tee_times tt JOIN trip_rounds r ON r.id = tt.trip_round_id WHERE r.trip_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id IN (SELECT id FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_round_money WHERE trip_round_id IN (SELECT id FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)').bind(id), // v2.92.0 — FR-25
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_events WHERE trip_id = ?').bind(id), // v2.92.0 — D84 (link rows only; game-day EVENTS are deleted by the client flow first)
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_players WHERE trip_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trips WHERE id = ?').bind(id),
  ];
}

async function handleDeleteTrip(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.batch(tripCascade(env, id));
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: id }, 200, origin);
}

// Delete ONE game day: the league_rounds link AND its whole wrapped trip
// (tee sheet, players, scores — everything). Organizer-authed via the league.
async function handleDeleteLeagueRound(req, env, origin, leagueId, leagueRoundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const lr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, trip_id FROM league_rounds WHERE id = ? AND league_id = ?').bind(leagueRoundId, leagueId).first();
  if (!lr) return err('Game day not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.batch([
    ...tripCascade(env, lr.trip_id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM league_rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(leagueRoundId),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_league_round_id: leagueRoundId, deleted_trip_id: lr.trip_id }, 200, origin);
}

const LEAGUE_FORMATS = new Set(['stableford', 'overall_skins', 'low_net', 'low_gross', 'match_play', 'best_ball']);

// ⭐ v2.90.0 — THE MATCH_PLAY / BEST_BALL LATCH (s107 queue). These two stay
// in LEAGUE_FORMATS so rounds ALREADY stamped with them keep rendering and
// keep PATCHing — but neither has a scoring engine, so NEW stamps are refused
// at every write site (create, both round PATCHes, games.list additions).
// "Remove the OFFER, never the RENDERING" — v2.69.0 removed the offer from the
// client pickers; this enforces it server-side too. D62 (Ryder Cup) retires
// this set the day match play actually scores.
const LEAGUE_FORMATS_UNSCORED = new Set(['match_play', 'best_ball']);
const _fmtLatchErr = (f) => (f === 'match_play' ? 'Match Play' : 'Best Ball') +
  ' does not score yet and can no longer be set on new rounds — a round already saved with it keeps working.';

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MULTI-GAME ROUNDS (v2.69.0)
// Spec: claude/multi-game-rounds-spec-v1.md §1-§4 (BIND)
//       claude/multi-game-spec-addendum-s18.md §1-§5 (BIND)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// LEAGUE_FORMATS above stays all SIX keys on purpose. It is a WRITE-TIME
// whitelist, and narrowing it would make a round already stamped 'match_play'
// un-PATCHable. Match Play and Best Ball are being removed from the client
// pickers instead — remove the OFFER, never the RENDERING.

// The unit a game scores in. This split is what makes §1 BIND enforceable
// rather than merely documented.
//   HIGH_WINS — more is better. Small integers (skins ~2) or a ramp (~30).
//               May feed the season points column.
//   LOW_WINS  — fewer is better. Total strokes, ~72. May NEVER feed the
//               season column: the table would rank the worst golfer first.
const HIGH_WINS_FORMATS = new Set(['stableford', 'overall_skins']);
const LOW_WINS_FORMATS  = new Set(['low_net', 'low_gross']);

// Formats that actually compute something as of v2.69.0. match_play and
// best_ball are deliberately absent — they are Phase 2. Anything not in here
// scores as stableford, which is what every unhandled format already did.
const SCORING_FORMATS = new Set(['stableford', 'overall_skins', 'low_net', 'low_gross']);

// trip_rounds.games -> plain object. Reuses _cfgParse so a NULL column, an
// empty string and malformed JSON all land on {} identically.
function _gamesParse(raw) {
  const g = _cfgParse(raw);
  return _cfgIsPlain(g) ? g : {};
}

// Every game running on the round, primary first, de-duplicated.
// games.list is authoritative when present; otherwise the round runs exactly
// one game and it is `format`. A round with games NULL is therefore identical
// to the round it was before this version, by construction and not by
// inspection.
function _gamesList(games, format) {
  const out = [];
  const push = (k) => { if (k && out.indexOf(k) === -1) out.push(k); };
  push(format || null);
  if (Array.isArray(games && games.list)) for (const k of games.list) push(k);
  return out;
}

// ⭐ Which game feeds the season points column. Bruce, s18: the ORGANIZER
// picks, per round. The pick is constrained to HIGH_WINS by _gamesValidate,
// which is where §1 BIND actually lives.
//
// The default branch is the whole compatibility story: with games NULL a
// stableford week feeds stableford, a skins week feeds skins (v2.58.0,
// unchanged), and a low_net week feeds STABLEFORD — precisely what it does
// today, the format never having had an engine.
function resolveSeasonGame(games, format) {
  if (games && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(games, 'season')) return games.season || null;
  return HIGH_WINS_FORMATS.has(format) ? format : 'stableford';
}

// Validate a MERGED games object against the format that will be stored.
// Returns an error string, or null when it is good. Every failure is a 400 at
// the call site — never a silent repair. A guess here is the exact failure
// class this whole release exists to close.
function _gamesValidate(games, format) {
  if (!_cfgIsPlain(games)) return 'games must be an object';
  if ('list' in games && games.list !== null && !Array.isArray(games.list)) return 'games.list must be an array';
  const raw = Array.isArray(games.list) ? games.list : [];
  for (const k of raw) {
    if (typeof k !== 'string' || !LEAGUE_FORMATS.has(k)) return 'games.list contains an unknown game: ' + String(k);
  }
  // §1 BIND — "no primary declared is a refusal, not a guess."
  if (raw.length && !format) return 'a primary game is required when games.list is set — set format';
  if (raw.length && format && raw.indexOf(format) === -1) return 'games.list must contain the primary game (' + format + ')';
  if ('season' in games && games.season !== null) {
    const sg = games.season;
    if (typeof sg !== 'string' || !LEAGUE_FORMATS.has(sg)) return 'games.season is not a known game';
    const list = _gamesList(games, format);
    if (list.indexOf(sg) === -1) return 'games.season must be one of the games on the round';
    // ⭐ §1 BIND, enforced rather than assumed.
    if (!HIGH_WINS_FORMATS.has(sg)) {
      return 'games.season cannot be a low-wins game (' + sg + ') — it would rank the worst score first';
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// Merge an incoming games patch onto the stored one. ONE level, via the same
// _cfgMerge the barnes config uses, so there is one merge rule in the app and
// not two. `list` is an array and is therefore replaced WHOLESALE when
// supplied, exactly as barnes.teams is — a half-built list must never merge
// into a whole-looking one. Passing games:null clears the column.
// Returns [mergedOrNull, errorStringOrNull].
function _gamesMerge(currentRaw, patch, format) {
  if (patch === null) return [null, null];
  if (!_cfgIsPlain(patch)) return [null, 'games must be an object or null'];
  const _cur = _gamesParse(currentRaw);                                   // v2.90.0 — latch base
  const [merged] = _cfgMerge(_cur, patch, []);
  const e = _gamesValidate(merged, format);
  if (e) return [null, e];
  if (Array.isArray(merged.list)) {
    const seen = [];
    for (const k of merged.list) if (seen.indexOf(k) === -1) seen.push(k);
    merged.list = seen;                       // de-dupe, first-seen order. Deterministic, not a scoring call.
    // ⭐ v2.90.0 — the LATCH, list leg: an unscored game may STAY on a round
    // that already carries it, but may not be ADDED. Compared against the
    // STORED list, so an unchanged re-send passes.
    const _curList = (_cur && Array.isArray(_cur.list)) ? _cur.list : [];
    for (const k of seen) {
      if (LEAGUE_FORMATS_UNSCORED.has(k) && _curList.indexOf(k) === -1) {
        return [null, _fmtLatchErr(k)];
      }
    }
  }
  return [merged, null];
}

// ⭐ A low-wins board over rows that already carry their own totals.
// `value` is total strokes, so HOLES PLAYED CHANGES THE UNIT — a 9-hole 36
// beats an 18-hole 70 on the raw number and would be nonsense at rank 1.
// Most holes first, then fewest strokes. Stableford and skins are additive
// and never need this.
function lowBoard(rows, valueKey, holesKey) {
  const board = rows
    .filter(r => (r[holesKey] || 0) > 0 && r[valueKey] != null)
    .map(r => ({ key: r.key, name: r.name, value: r[valueKey], holes: r[holesKey] }));
  board.sort((a, b) => (b.holes - a.holes) || (a.value - b.value) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)));
  applyTieAwareRanks(board, (prev, cur) => prev.holes === cur.holes && prev.value === cur.value);
  return board;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SKINS (v2.58.0)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// The organizer picks the money mode per game day. Null/unknown reads as
// 'count' everywhere rather than throwing — an unconfigured skins night is a
// perfectly good skins night, it just has no money on it.
const SKINS_MODES = new Set(['count', 'pool', 'fixed']);

// THE skins engine. One implementation per concern: handleLeagueRoundLeaderboard
// and handleLeagueSeason both call this, so a week's skins count can never
// disagree with the season standings it feeds.
//
// NET, carryover, dead at 18 — see the v2.58.0 changelog for the rules and who
// decided them. Strokes come from hcpStrokesForHole, the stableford allocator;
// this file must never grow a second one.
//
//   players: [{ key, name, hole_gross: [18], playing_handicap }]
//   holeSI:  [18] stroke indexes, or null (falls back to hole order)
//
// A hole nobody scored offers no skin AND does not consume the carry — the
// stack walks on. Whatever is still riding when the card runs out is dead.
function computeSkins(players, holeSI) {
  const holes = [];
  const acc = new Map();
  for (const p of (players || [])) {
    if (!acc.has(p.key)) acc.set(p.key, { key: p.key, name: p.name, skins: 0, holes_won: [] });
  }
  let carry = 0;
  for (let h = 0; h < 18; h++) {
    const si = (holeSI && holeSI[h] != null) ? holeSI[h] : h + 1;
    const contenders = [];
    for (const p of (players || [])) {
      const g = p.hole_gross ? p.hole_gross[h] : null;
      if (!g || g <= 0) continue;                       // no score = not on the hole
      // A player on a different tee strokes off THEIR card's index, not the
      // round's — trip_tee_time_players carries a per-player tee already.
      const psi = (p.si && p.si[h] != null) ? p.si[h] : si;
      contenders.push({
        key: p.key, name: p.name, gross: g,
        net: g - hcpStrokesForHole(psi, p.playing_handicap),
      });
    }
    if (!contenders.length) {
      holes.push({ hole: h + 1, played: false, carried_in: carry, pot: carry,
                   winner_key: null, winner_name: null, tied: false,
                   tied_count: 0, low_net: null });
      continue;
    }
    const pot = carry + 1;
    let low = Infinity;
    for (const c of contenders) if (c.net < low) low = c.net;
    const winners = contenders.filter(c => c.net === low);
    if (winners.length === 1) {
      const w = winners[0];
      const a = acc.get(w.key);
      if (a) { a.skins += pot; a.holes_won.push({ hole: h + 1, skins: pot, net: w.net, gross: w.gross }); }
      holes.push({ hole: h + 1, played: true, carried_in: carry, pot,
                   winner_key: w.key, winner_name: w.name, tied: false,
                   tied_count: 0, low_net: low });
      carry = 0;
    } else {
      holes.push({ hole: h + 1, played: true, carried_in: carry, pot,
                   winner_key: null, winner_name: null, tied: true,
                   tied_count: winners.length, low_net: low });
      carry = pot;                                       // stacks
    }
  }
  const players_out = Array.from(acc.values());
  const total_skins = players_out.reduce((a, p) => a + p.skins, 0);
  return { holes, players: players_out, total_skins, dead_skins: carry };
}

// What a skin is worth. Cents throughout — a pool split rarely divides evenly
// and floating dollars would drift. The residue is reported on its own line
// (the tote's rule) instead of being quietly absorbed into somebody's payout.
//
// fieldSize = players who POSTED SCORES that night, not players checked in:
// the pool should not grow for a man who never teed off.
function skinsMoney(mode, amount, fieldSize, totalSkins) {
  const m = SKINS_MODES.has(mode) ? mode : 'count';
  if (m === 'count') {
    return { mode: 'count', pool_cents: null, per_skin_cents: null,
             total_paid_cents: 0, residue_cents: 0, unclaimed: false };
  }
  const cents = Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(amount) || 0) * 100));
  if (m === 'fixed') {
    return { mode: 'fixed', amount: Number(amount) || 0, pool_cents: null,
             per_skin_cents: cents, total_paid_cents: cents * totalSkins,
             residue_cents: 0, unclaimed: false };
  }
  const field = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(Number(fieldSize) || 0));
  const pool_cents = cents * field;
  if (!totalSkins) {
    // Every hole tied, or nobody posted. No payout is invented — the pool is
    // shown unclaimed and the organizer sorts it out.
    return { mode: 'pool', amount: Number(amount) || 0, buy_in_cents: cents,
             field, pool_cents, per_skin_cents: 0, total_paid_cents: 0,
             residue_cents: pool_cents, unclaimed: true };
  }
  const per_skin_cents = Math.floor(pool_cents / totalSkins);
  return { mode: 'pool', amount: Number(amount) || 0, buy_in_cents: cents,
           field, pool_cents, per_skin_cents,
           total_paid_cents: per_skin_cents * totalSkins,
           residue_cents: pool_cents - per_skin_cents * totalSkins,
           unclaimed: false };
}

// v2.57.0 — THE league-day trip name. One implementation per concern:
// handleCreateLeagueRound builds the name at birth and handlePatchLeagueRound
// rebuilds it when the day moves. If these ever forked, a moved game day would
// keep its old date in season standings forever.
function leagueDayTripName(leagueName, roundDate) {
  const dateLabel = roundDate
    ? new Date(roundDate + 'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('en-CA', { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric', timeZone: 'UTC' })
    : 'Round';
  return `${leagueName} — ${dateLabel}`;
}

// v2.57.0 — a game day is one calendar day. Anything that is not a plain
// YYYY-MM-DD that round-trips through Date is refused rather than written:
// four rows depend on this value agreeing with itself.
function isPlainISODate(v) {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(v)) return false;
  const d = new Date(v + 'T12:00:00Z');
  return !isNaN(d.getTime()) && d.toISOString().slice(0, 10) === v;
}

// Set/change a game day's format after creation. "Format: TBD" used to be
// permanent — the picker only existed on the create sheet. Format lives on
// the wrapped trip's round 1 (the same row handleGetLeague's format
// subquery reads: lowest round_number).
async function handlePatchLeagueRound(req, env, origin, leagueId, leagueRoundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const lr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, trip_id, round_date FROM league_rounds WHERE id = ? AND league_id = ?').bind(leagueRoundId, leagueId).first();
  if (!lr) return err('Game day not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  // v2.57.0 — either key alone is a valid body; both together are one call.
  const wantsFormat = !!(body && 'format' in body);
  const wantsDate   = !!(body && 'round_date' in body);
  // v2.58.0 — the skins money settings move as ONE unit. skins_amount never
  // travels alone: read-modify-write on a money field is how you end up with
  // a $10 buy-in silently attached to a mode that does not use one.
  const wantsSkins  = !!(body && 'skins_mode' in body);
  const wantsGames  = !!(body && 'games' in body);                        // v2.69.0
  if (!wantsFormat && !wantsDate && !wantsSkins && !wantsGames) {
    return err('format, round_date, skins_mode or games required', 400, origin);
  }
  if (!wantsSkins && body && 'skins_amount' in body) {
    return err('skins_amount requires skins_mode', 400, origin);
  }

  const format = wantsFormat ? (body.format || null) : null;
  if (wantsFormat && format !== null && !LEAGUE_FORMATS.has(format)) return err('Unknown format', 400, origin);

  // v2.69.0 — games. Validated against the format that will actually be
  // STORED, not the one that happens to be in the body: format and games can
  // move in the same call, and validating against a stale format is how you
  // end up with a list that does not contain its own primary.
  // v2.90.0 — the cur fetch (previously games-only) hoists one level: the
  // LATCH needs the STORED format whenever a format arrives, and fetching it
  // twice would be the read-modify-write hazard this handler already documents.
  let cur = null;
  if (wantsFormat || wantsGames) {
    cur = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT format, games FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ? AND round_number = (SELECT MIN(round_number) FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)'
    ).bind(lr.trip_id, lr.trip_id).first();
  }
  // ⭐ v2.90.0 — the LATCH: an unchanged re-send of a stored match_play /
  // best_ball passes (so old rounds stay PATCHable); a NEW stamp refuses.
  if (wantsFormat && format !== null && LEAGUE_FORMATS_UNSCORED.has(format) && (!cur || cur.format !== format)) {
    return err(_fmtLatchErr(format), 400, origin);
  }
  let gamesJson = null;
  if (wantsGames) {
    const effFormat = wantsFormat ? format : (cur ? cur.format : null);
    const [merged, gErr] = _gamesMerge(cur ? cur.games : null, body.games, effFormat);
    if (gErr) return err(gErr, 400, origin);
    gamesJson = merged === null ? null : JSON.stringify(merged);
  }

  const round_date = wantsDate ? body.round_date : null;
  // A game day without a date is a day nobody can find. round_date is the
  // schedule's sort key AND its heading — clearing it is not a thing.
  if (wantsDate && !isPlainISODate(round_date)) return err('round_date must be YYYY-MM-DD', 400, origin);

  // v2.58.0 — money mode. null clears back to "no money on it", which is also
  // what an unset column reads as. count carries no amount by definition; the
  // two paying modes must carry a real positive one or the night's payouts
  // would all be zero and look like a bug rather than a setting.
  let skins_mode = null, skins_amount = null;
  if (wantsSkins) {
    skins_mode = body.skins_mode || null;
    if (skins_mode !== null && !SKINS_MODES.has(skins_mode)) return err('Unknown skins_mode', 400, origin);
    if (skins_mode === 'pool' || skins_mode === 'fixed') {
      const amt = Number(body.skins_amount);
      if (!Number.isFinite(amt) || amt <= 0) return err('skins_amount must be a positive number', 400, origin);
      skins_amount = Math.round(amt * 100) / 100;
    }
  }

  const stmts = [];
  if (wantsSkins) {
    stmts.push(env.DB.prepare(
      'UPDATE trip_rounds SET skins_mode = ?, skins_amount = ? WHERE trip_id = ? AND round_number = (SELECT MIN(round_number) FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)'
    ).bind(skins_mode, skins_amount, lr.trip_id, lr.trip_id));
  }
  if (wantsFormat) {
    stmts.push(env.DB.prepare(
      'UPDATE trip_rounds SET format = ? WHERE trip_id = ? AND round_number = (SELECT MIN(round_number) FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)'
    ).bind(format, lr.trip_id, lr.trip_id));
  }
  if (wantsGames) {                                                       // v2.69.0
    stmts.push(env.DB.prepare(
      'UPDATE trip_rounds SET games = ? WHERE trip_id = ? AND round_number = (SELECT MIN(round_number) FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)'
    ).bind(gamesJson, lr.trip_id, lr.trip_id));
  }
  if (wantsDate) {
    // FOUR rows in lockstep — see the v2.57.0 changelog entry. Same batch, so
    // a half-moved game day is not a state this can land in.
    stmts.push(
      env.DB.prepare('UPDATE league_rounds SET round_date = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(round_date, leagueRoundId),
      env.DB.prepare('UPDATE trips SET start_date = ?, end_date = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(round_date, round_date, lr.trip_id),
      env.DB.prepare(
        'UPDATE trip_rounds SET date = ? WHERE trip_id = ? AND round_number = (SELECT MIN(round_number) FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?)'
      ).bind(round_date, lr.trip_id, lr.trip_id)
    );
    // The name is regenerated only if it is still the one create wrote for the
    // OLD date. A custom name the organizer typed is left exactly alone.
    const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT name FROM trips WHERE id = ?').bind(lr.trip_id).first();
    if (trip && trip.name === leagueDayTripName(league.name, lr.round_date)) {
      stmts.push(env.DB.prepare('UPDATE trips SET name = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(leagueDayTripName(league.name, round_date), lr.trip_id));
    }
  }
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  return json({
    ok: true,
    league_round_id: leagueRoundId,
    format: wantsFormat ? format : undefined,
    round_date: wantsDate ? round_date : undefined,
    skins_mode: wantsSkins ? skins_mode : undefined,
    skins_amount: wantsSkins ? skins_amount : undefined,
  }, 200, origin);
}

/* ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   LEAGUE CONFIG WRITERS  (v2.68.0)

   v2.67.0 shipped a Barnes board that READS leagues.config and
   league_rounds.config. Nothing wrote either. The columns were added in one
   session and seeded by direct D1 write in the next, so the gap stayed
   invisible until a setup screen was wanted.

   THREE RULES, all learned the hard way:

   1. MERGE, NEVER REPLACE. leagues.config holds `barnes`, `game_pars` and
      `game_si` side by side, and handleLeagueBarnes REFUSES without the
      latter two. A setup screen that knows only about barnes must not be
      able to wipe them by omission.

      ⭐ v2.68.1 — THE TOP LEVEL WAS NOT DEEP ENOUGH. The rule above guarded
      the sibling arrays and stopped there, so `barnes` itself was still
      replaced wholesale: `{barnes:{on:true}}` stored a one-key barnes and
      dropped teams, stake and carry_in. The guard that was written held; the
      symmetrical hazard one level down had simply not been considered. Both
      sub-objects a setup screen edits field-by-field — `barnes` here and
      `barnes_week` on the round — are now in `deepKeys`. The lesson worth
      keeping: "merge, never replace" is not a property of a function, it is
      a property of a DEPTH, and it has to be re-asked at every level the
      caller is allowed to address.
      Sending an explicit null DELETES a key — that is the one way to remove
      something, and it has to be said out loud rather than implied by absence.

   2. REFUSE AT WRITE TIME, NOT AT READ TIME. A bad game key stored here
      surfaces later as `no_game_set` on the board — which reads as "the
      organizer forgot", not "the save was wrong". Session 13 nearly shipped
      `"game": 1` where the code matches on '1v1_nassau'. The board's refusal
      caught it, but only because a human was reading. Validate on the way IN.

   3. A HUMAN-READABLE LABEL IS NOT THE KEY. BARNES_GAMES is keyed by string;
      the labels are prose. Reject numbers explicitly and name the three legal
      keys in the error, so the next person to hit this gets told the answer
      rather than a 400.
   ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */

// A plain object — something we can merge INTO. Arrays and null are not.
function _cfgIsPlain(v) {
  return !!v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
}

// Merge a patch into a stored config. Returns [merged, changedPaths].
//
//   TOP LEVEL   shallow — out[k] = patch[k]. An explicit null deletes the key.
//   deepKeys    (v2.68.1) the named keys merge ONE level deeper, but only when
//               BOTH sides are plain objects. Sub-keys the patch did not
//               mention survive; an explicit null on a sub-key deletes it.
//
// ONE level, deliberately. A value inside a sub-key — barnes.teams,
// barnes_week.lineup — is still replaced wholesale, and its validator requires
// it to arrive complete (3 teams of 6; A/B/C of 4). Recursing further would
// let a half-built roster merge into a whole-looking one, which is the failure
// this route exists to prevent, not a convenience to add.
//
// changedPaths reports dotted paths for deep merges ('barnes.on'), so the
// response says what actually moved rather than naming the container.
function _cfgMerge(current, patch, deepKeys) {
  const out = Object.assign({}, current || {});
  const changed = [];
  const deep = Array.isArray(deepKeys) ? deepKeys : [];
  for (const k of Object.keys(patch)) {
    if (patch[k] === null) {
      if (k in out) { delete out[k]; changed.push(k); }
      continue;
    }
    if (deep.indexOf(k) !== -1 && _cfgIsPlain(patch[k]) && _cfgIsPlain(out[k])) {
      const sub = Object.assign({}, out[k]);
      for (const sk of Object.keys(patch[k])) {
        if (patch[k][sk] === null) {
          if (sk in sub) { delete sub[sk]; changed.push(k + '.' + sk); }
        } else {
          sub[sk] = patch[k][sk];
          changed.push(k + '.' + sk);
        }
      }
      out[k] = sub;
      continue;
    }
    out[k] = patch[k];
    changed.push(k);
  }
  return [out, changed];
}

function _cfgParse(raw) {
  try { return typeof raw === 'string' ? JSON.parse(raw || '{}') : (raw || {}); }
  catch (_) { return {}; }
}

// A roster_id list of exactly n distinct positive integers.
function _cfgIds(a, n) {
  if (!Array.isArray(a) || a.length !== n) return null;
  const out = [];
  for (const x of a) {
    const v = Number(x);
    if (!Number.isInteger(v) || v <= 0) return null;
    if (out.indexOf(v) !== -1) return null;      // a player cannot fill two slots
    out.push(v);
  }
  return out;
}

// Validate a `barnes` sub-object. Returns an error STRING or null.
function _cfgValidateBarnes(b) {
  if (b === null) return null;                                   // delete is fine
  if (typeof b !== 'object' || Array.isArray(b)) return 'barnes must be an object';
  if ('on' in b && typeof b.on !== 'boolean') return 'barnes.on must be true or false';
  if ('teams' in b) {
    const t = b.teams;
    if (!Array.isArray(t) || t.length !== 3) return 'barnes.teams must be exactly 3 teams';
    const seen = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const tm = t[i];
      if (!tm || typeof tm !== 'object') return `barnes.teams[${i}] must be an object`;
      if ('name' in tm && typeof tm.name !== 'string') return `barnes.teams[${i}].name must be a string`;
      const ids = _cfgIds(tm.roster_ids, 6);
      if (!ids) return `barnes.teams[${i}].roster_ids must be 6 distinct roster ids`;
      for (const id of ids) {
        if (seen.indexOf(id) !== -1) return `roster id ${id} is on more than one team`;
        seen.push(id);
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// Validate a `barnes_week` sub-object. `teams` is the league's configured
// teams when available, so a lineup can be checked against the actual roster
// rather than merely being well-shaped.
function _cfgValidateBarnesWeek(bw, teams) {
  if (bw === null) return null;
  if (typeof bw !== 'object' || Array.isArray(bw)) return 'barnes_week must be an object';

  if ('game' in bw && bw.game !== null) {
    const legal = Object.keys(BARNES_GAMES);
    if (typeof bw.game !== 'string') {
      // The session-13 near-miss, caught at the door. Numbers are the obvious
      // wrong guess because the spec numbers the games 1/2/3 in prose.
      return `barnes_week.game must be one of the string keys ${legal.join(' / ')} — not a number or a label`;
    }
    if (!BARNES_GAMES[bw.game]) return `Unknown barnes_week.game "${bw.game}". Legal keys: ${legal.join(' / ')}`;
  }

  if ('lineup' in bw && bw.lineup !== null) {
    const lu = bw.lineup;
    if (!lu || typeof lu !== 'object' || Array.isArray(lu)) return 'barnes_week.lineup must be an object keyed A/B/C';
    const seen = [];
    for (const t of ['A', 'B', 'C']) {
      const ids = _cfgIds(lu[t], 4);
      if (!ids) return `barnes_week.lineup.${t} must be 4 distinct roster ids, in slot order`;
      for (const id of ids) {
        if (seen.indexOf(id) !== -1) return `roster id ${id} appears in more than one team's lineup`;
        seen.push(id);
      }
      // Cross-check against the league roster when we have one. A player who
      // is not on the team cannot be entered for it — that would produce a
      // board that looks right and is wrong.
      if (teams) {
        const idx = { A: 0, B: 1, C: 2 }[t];
        const roster = (teams[idx] && teams[idx].roster_ids) ? teams[idx].roster_ids.map(Number) : null;
        if (roster) {
          for (const id of ids) {
            if (roster.indexOf(id) === -1) return `roster id ${id} is not on team ${t}`;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

async function handlePatchLeagueConfig(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, config FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) return err('Body must be a JSON object', 400, origin);
  const keys = Object.keys(body);
  if (!keys.length) return err('Nothing to change', 400, origin);

  if ('barnes' in body) {
    const e = _cfgValidateBarnes(body.barnes);
    if (e) return err(e, 400, origin);
  }
  // The 18-hole arrays, if the caller is setting them. Same length rule the
  // board refuses on, applied here so it can never be stored short.
  for (const k of ['game_pars', 'game_si']) {
    if (k in body && body[k] !== null) {
      const a = body[k];
      if (!Array.isArray(a) || a.length !== 18 || a.some(x => !Number.isInteger(Number(x)))) {
        return err(`${k} must be 18 whole numbers`, 400, origin);
      }
    }
  }

  const current = _cfgParse(league.config);
  const [merged, changed] = _cfgMerge(current, body, ['barnes']);

  // v2.68.1 — validate what will actually be STORED, not only what arrived.
  // With a deep merge the stored object is patch-over-existing, so a patch
  // that is legal in isolation can still compose into an illegal whole.
  if ('barnes' in body && body.barnes !== null) {
    const em = _cfgValidateBarnes(merged.barnes);
    if (em) return err(`${em} (after merging into the stored config)`, 400, origin);
  }

  await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE leagues SET config = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(JSON.stringify(merged), id).run();

  // Advisory, never blocking: turning Barnes on without pars/SI is legal to
  // store and will produce a refusing board. Say so at save time rather than
  // letting the organizer discover it on game night.
  const warnings = [];
  const mb = merged.barnes || {};
  if (mb.on) {
    if (!Array.isArray(merged.game_pars) || merged.game_pars.length !== 18 ||
        !Array.isArray(merged.game_si)   || merged.game_si.length   !== 18) {
      warnings.push('Barnes is on but this league has no 18-hole pars/stroke index — the board will refuse with per_hole_pars_missing until they are set.');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(mb.teams) || mb.teams.length !== 3) {
      warnings.push('Barnes is on but the three teams of six are not set — the board will refuse with teams_incomplete.');
    }
  }
  return json({ ok: true, league_id: id, changed, config: merged, warnings }, 200, origin);
}

async function handlePatchLeagueRoundConfig(req, env, origin, leagueId, leagueRoundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, config FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const lr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, config FROM league_rounds WHERE id = ? AND league_id = ?').bind(leagueRoundId, leagueId).first();
  if (!lr) return err('Game day not found', 404, origin);

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) return err('Body must be a JSON object', 400, origin);
  const keys = Object.keys(body);
  if (!keys.length) return err('Nothing to change', 400, origin);

  const lcfg = _cfgParse(league.config);
  const teams = (lcfg.barnes && Array.isArray(lcfg.barnes.teams)) ? lcfg.barnes.teams : null;
  if ('barnes_week' in body) {
    const e = _cfgValidateBarnesWeek(body.barnes_week, teams);
    if (e) return err(e, 400, origin);
  }

  const current = _cfgParse(lr.config);
  const [merged, changed] = _cfgMerge(current, body, ['barnes_week']);

  // v2.68.1 — same reasoning as the league route: re-validate the merged
  // result. `{barnes_week:{game:'2v2_highlow'}}` is legal on its own and used
  // to drop the lineup entirely; now it keeps it, and the kept lineup is
  // re-checked against the roster before anything is written.
  if ('barnes_week' in body && body.barnes_week !== null) {
    const em = _cfgValidateBarnesWeek(merged.barnes_week, teams);
    if (em) return err(`${em} (after merging into the stored config)`, 400, origin);
  }

  await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE league_rounds SET config = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(JSON.stringify(merged), leagueRoundId).run();

  const warnings = [];
  const bw = merged.barnes_week || {};
  if (bw.game && !bw.lineup) warnings.push('Game set but no lineup yet — the board will refuse with no_lineup until four players per team are entered.');
  if (bw.lineup && !bw.game) warnings.push('Lineup set but no game chosen — the board will refuse with no_game_set. There is no default.');

  return json({ ok: true, league_id: leagueId, league_round_id: leagueRoundId, changed, config: merged, warnings }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleListTripPlayers(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tp.id, tp.roster_id, tp.ghin, tp.current_index, tp.last_index_pull,
            r.name, r.handicap, r.home_club, r.email, r.buddy
     FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     WHERE tp.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
  ).bind(tripId).all();
  return json({ players: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleAddTripPlayer(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.roster_id) return err('roster_id required', 400, origin);
  const rp = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, ghin, handicap FROM roster WHERE id = ? AND account_id = ?').bind(body.roster_id, account.id).first();
  if (!rp) return err('Player not found in roster', 404, origin);
  const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_players WHERE trip_id = ? AND roster_id = ?').bind(tripId, body.roster_id).first();
  if (existing) return err('Player already on this trip', 409, origin);
  const player = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trip_players (trip_id, roster_id, ghin, current_index) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(tripId, body.roster_id, rp.ghin || null, rp.handicap || null).first();
  return json({ player }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleRemoveTripPlayer(req, env, origin, tripId, playerId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  // v2.34.3: trip_tee_time_players + trip_scores reference trip_players with NO
  // cascade — the bare DELETE threw an FK error for any player already on a tee
  // sheet (surfaced as a bare 500 after v2.34.2's return-await). Scores are
  // sacred: refuse if any exist. Tee-time slots are pre-round structure: clear
  // them so roster shuffles keep working.
  const tp = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_players WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?').bind(playerId, tripId).first();
  if (!tp) return err('Player not found on this trip', 404, origin);
  const sc = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_player_id = ?').bind(playerId).first();
  if (sc && sc.n > 0) return err('This player has ' + sc.n + ' scored holes on this trip — remove their scores first.', 409, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_tee_time_players WHERE trip_player_id = ?').bind(playerId).run();
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_players WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?').bind(playerId, tripId).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleListTripRounds(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.*, c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id = ? ORDER BY tr.round_number ASC`
  ).bind(tripId).all();
  return json({ rounds: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleCreateTripRound(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const count = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM trip_rounds WHERE trip_id = ?').bind(tripId).first();
  const round_number = body.round_number || (count.n + 1);
  const _fmt = body.format || null;                                       // v2.69.0
  if (_fmt !== null && !LEAGUE_FORMATS.has(_fmt)) return err('Unknown format', 400, origin);
  if (_fmt !== null && LEAGUE_FORMATS_UNSCORED.has(_fmt)) return err(_fmtLatchErr(_fmt), 400, origin); // ⭐ v2.90.0 — latch: a CREATE is always a new stamp
  let _gamesJson = null;                                                  // v2.69.0
  if ('games' in body && body.games !== null) {
    const [merged, gErr] = _gamesMerge(null, body.games, _fmt);
    if (gErr) return err(gErr, 400, origin);
    _gamesJson = merged === null ? null : JSON.stringify(merged);
  }
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trip_rounds (trip_id, round_number, course_id, tee_box_id, date, label, status, format, games)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft', ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(tripId, round_number, body.course_id || null, body.tee_box_id || null, body.date || null, body.label || null, _fmt, _gamesJson).first();
  return json({ round }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleUpdateTripRound(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT tr.id, tr.format, tr.games FROM trip_rounds tr JOIN trips t ON t.id = tr.trip_id WHERE tr.id = ? AND tr.trip_id = ? AND t.organizer_id = ?'
  ).bind(roundId, tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const cur = round;                                                      // v2.69.0 — merge base
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const updates = [], values = [];
  if (body.course_id    != null) { updates.push('course_id = ?');    values.push(body.course_id); }
  if (body.tee_box_id   != null) { updates.push('tee_box_id = ?');   values.push(body.tee_box_id); }
  if (body.date         != null) { updates.push('date = ?');         values.push(body.date); }
  if (body.label        != null) { updates.push('label = ?');        values.push(body.label); }
  if (body.status       != null) { updates.push('status = ?');       values.push(body.status); }
  // ⭐ v2.69.0 — THIS CHECK DID NOT EXIST. handlePatchLeagueRound has always
  // validated format against LEAGUE_FORMATS; this handler never did, so it
  // stored any string it was handed — which then rendered as a blank label and
  // scored as nothing. Same silent-acceptance family as the four dead formats.
  // New writes only: nothing already stored is touched or stops rendering.
  const _fmt = ('format' in body) ? (body.format || null) : undefined;
  if (_fmt !== undefined && _fmt !== null && !LEAGUE_FORMATS.has(_fmt)) return err('Unknown format', 400, origin);
  if (_fmt !== undefined && _fmt !== null && LEAGUE_FORMATS_UNSCORED.has(_fmt) && _fmt !== cur.format) return err(_fmtLatchErr(_fmt), 400, origin); // ⭐ v2.90.0 — latch: unchanged re-sends pass
  if ('format' in body)          { updates.push('format = ?');       values.push(_fmt); }
  if (body.round_number != null) { updates.push('round_number = ?'); values.push(Number(body.round_number)); }
  if ('games' in body) {                                                  // v2.69.0
    const effFormat = (_fmt !== undefined) ? _fmt : (cur ? cur.format : null);
    const [merged, gErr] = _gamesMerge(cur ? cur.games : null, body.games, effFormat);
    if (gErr) return err(gErr, 400, origin);
    updates.push('games = ?'); values.push(merged === null ? null : JSON.stringify(merged));
  }
  if (!updates.length) return err('Nothing to update', 400, origin);
  values.push(roundId);
  const updated = await env.DB.prepare(`UPDATE trip_rounds SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ? RETURNING *`).bind(...values).first();
  return json({ round: updated }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDeleteTripRound(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT tr.id FROM trip_rounds tr JOIN trips t ON t.id = tr.trip_id WHERE tr.id = ? AND tr.trip_id = ? AND t.organizer_id = ?'
  ).bind(roundId, tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_nassau WHERE trip_round_id = ?').bind(roundId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_round_id = ?').bind(roundId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_tee_time_players WHERE trip_tee_time_id IN (SELECT id FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id = ?)').bind(roundId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id = ?').bind(roundId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_rounds WHERE id = ?').bind(roundId),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: roundId }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetTripTeeTimes(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.*, c.name AS course_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.id = ? AND tr.trip_id = ?`
  ).bind(roundId, tripId).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: tts } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id = ? ORDER BY start_time ASC, hole ASC'
  ).bind(roundId).all();
  const result = [];
  for (const tt of tts) {
    const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT ttp.*, tp.roster_id, tp.current_index, tp.ghin,
              r.name, r.handicap, r.home_club,
              ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
       FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
       JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ttp.trip_player_id
       JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
       LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = ttp.tee_box_id
       WHERE ttp.trip_tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY ttp.slot_order`
    ).bind(tt.id).all();
    result.push({ ...tt, players });
  }
  return json({ round, tee_times: result }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleSaveTripTeeTimes(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT tr.id, tr.course_id, tr.tee_box_id FROM trip_rounds tr JOIN trips t ON t.id = tr.trip_id WHERE tr.id = ? AND tr.trip_id = ? AND t.organizer_id = ?'
  ).bind(roundId, tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.slots || !Array.isArray(body.slots)) return err('slots[] required', 400, origin);
  const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id = ?').bind(roundId).all();
  if (existing.results.length) {
    const ids = existing.results.map(r => r.id);
    await env.DB.prepare(`DELETE FROM trip_tee_time_players WHERE trip_tee_time_id IN (${ids.map(()=>'?').join(',')})`).bind(...ids).run();
    await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id = ?').bind(roundId).run();
  }
  let inserted = 0;
  for (const slot of body.slots) {
    const players = (slot.players || []).filter(Boolean);
    if (!players.length) continue;
    let code = null;
    for (let ci = 0; ci < 10; ci++) {
      const candidate = generateCode();
      if (!await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_tee_times WHERE foursome_code = ?').bind(candidate).first()) { code = candidate; break; }
    }
    const pin = generatePin();
    const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO trip_tee_times (trip_round_id, start_time, hole, foursome_code, foursome_pin) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
    ).bind(roundId, slot.start_time || '', slot.hole || 1, code, pin).first();
    for (let i = 0; i < players.length; i++) {
      const p = players[i];
      const teeBoxId = p.tee_box_id || round.tee_box_id || null;
      let courseHcp = null, playingHcp = null;
      let pIdx = p.current_index;                                          // v2.39.0
      if (pIdx == null && p.trip_player_id) {
        const tpr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT current_index FROM trip_players WHERE id = ?').bind(p.trip_player_id).first();
        pIdx = tpr ? tpr.current_index : null;
      }
      if (teeBoxId && pIdx != null) {
        const teeBox = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT rating, slope, par FROM course_tees WHERE id = ?').bind(teeBoxId).first();
        if (teeBox) {
          courseHcp  = calcCourseHandicapFull(pIdx, teeBox.slope, teeBox.rating, teeBox.par);
          playingHcp = courseHcp;
        }
      }
      await env.DB.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO trip_tee_time_players (trip_tee_time_id, trip_player_id, tee_box_id, course_handicap, playing_handicap, slot_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
      ).bind(tt.id, p.trip_player_id, teeBoxId, courseHcp, playingHcp, i).run();
    }
    inserted++;
  }
  const { results: savedTTs } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM trip_tee_times WHERE trip_round_id = ? ORDER BY start_time ASC, hole ASC'
  ).bind(roundId).all();
  const teeTimesOut = [];
  for (const tt of savedTTs) {
    const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT ttp.trip_player_id, ttp.course_handicap, ttp.playing_handicap, ttp.slot_order, r.name, r.handicap
       FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
       JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ttp.trip_player_id
       JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
       WHERE ttp.trip_tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY ttp.slot_order`
    ).bind(tt.id).all();
    teeTimesOut.push({ ...tt, players });
  }
  return json({ ok: true, inserted, tee_times: teeTimesOut }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// v2.94.0 — FR-31: THE GAME-DAY (EVENT) RAIL, FOLDED INTO THE TRIP OVERALL
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Casual trip rounds key players by trip_players.id. Event rounds key them by
// scores.player_id -> players.id. The ONLY bridge between the two key spaces
// is players.roster_id (additive column, s121) -> trip_players.roster_id.
//
// ⭐ NO NAME MATCHING. NOT EVEN AS A FALLBACK. A NULL players.roster_id is a
// MISS: that player folds in NOWHERE and is counted in `unlinked` so the
// client can say "N players not linked". A leaderboard that is quietly wrong
// is the exact failure mode this column was bought to prevent — measured s121,
// only 108 of 139 players name-matched a roster row, so guessing would have
// silently dropped 31 people.
//
// FAIL-SOFT BY CONSTRUCTION: every read sits inside the try. trip_events
// absent, players.roster_id absent, a bad course row — all return an EMPTY
// rail. The casual-round leaderboard must never break because the game-day
// rail had a bad day.
async function tripEventRail(env, tripId) {
  const empty = { rounds: [], cells: {}, unlinked: 0, unlinked_names: [], linked: 0, ok: true };
  const out = { rounds: [], cells: {}, unlinked: 0, unlinked_names: [], linked: 0, ok: true };
  try {
    const { results: evs } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT e.id, e.name, e.date, e.config FROM trip_events te JOIN events e ON e.id = te.event_id
        WHERE te.trip_id = ? ORDER BY e.date ASC, e.created_at ASC`
    ).bind(tripId).all();
    if (!evs || !evs.length) return out;                      // no game days linked — normal

    const { results: tps } = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT id, roster_id FROM trip_players WHERE trip_id = ?'
    ).bind(tripId).all();
    const tpByRoster = new Map();
    for (const tp of (tps || [])) if (tp.roster_id != null) tpByRoster.set(tp.roster_id, tp.id);
    if (!tpByRoster.size) return out;                         // nobody to fold onto

    const teeCache = new Map();
    const seenMiss = new Set();                               // one miss per player, not per hole
    for (const ev of evs) {
      const cfg = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(ev.config || '{}'); } catch (_) { return {}; } })();
      const { results: erounds } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT id, round_number, date, course_id, tee, format, status FROM rounds
          WHERE event_id = ? AND status IN ('active','complete') ORDER BY round_number`
      ).bind(ev.id).all();                                    // same status gate the casual rail uses
      for (const r of (erounds || [])) {
        const fmt = String(r.format || '');
        // Team cards score per GROUP, not per player — they cannot fold into an
        // INDIVIDUAL trip standing at all. Skipped outright, not zeroed.
        if (fmt.indexOf('scramble') !== -1) continue;
        // ⭐ v2.69.0 BIND, HELD: only the stableford family adds to total_pts.
        // A low-wins game round folds in at ZERO and posts to low_boards only.
        // ⭐ D86 (s123) -- the gate is OWN BALL, not the word "stableford".
        // The scramble family already `continue`d above: shared ball = no real
        // individual card, so there is nothing honest to fold. Everything still
