        // here IS a card the player played himself for 18 holes, and its honest
        // individual reading is the net-stableford ramp computed below.
        // ⭐ The v2.69.0 bind is PRESERVED EXACTLY: a low-wins round's scale is
        // strokes, not points -- it still folds at 0 and posts to low_boards only.
        // WHAT CHANGES: own-ball TEAM formats (team_ccc123w_4_net, team_2of4_net)
        // matched neither branch and fell through to 0 -- a fully scored round
        // contributing a column of zeroes, and on a trip whose only round is a
        // team day (trip 56 Scottsdale) a whole field tied at nothing. Those now
        // fold at the real points already being computed into pts_raw.
        const isLowWins = fmt.indexOf('low_net') !== -1 || fmt.indexOf('low_gross') !== -1;
        const foldsPts  = !isLowWins;
        const lowKeys  = [];
        if (fmt.indexOf('low_net')   !== -1) lowKeys.push('low_net');
        if (fmt.indexOf('low_gross') !== -1) lowKeys.push('low_gross');

        const courseId = r.course_id || null, tee = r.tee || null;
        const teeData  = await resolveTee(env, courseId, tee, teeCache);
        let pars = eventHolePars(cfg), si = eventHoleSI(cfg);  // event config WINS, same as recomputeRoundLeaderboard
        if (!pars) {
          const cp = await courseHolePars(env, courseId, tee);
          if (cp.pars) { pars = cp.pars; if (!si) si = cp.si; }
        }

        const { results: srows } = await env.DB.prepare(
          `SELECT s.player_id, p.roster_id, p.name, p.handicap, s.hole, s.gross_score
             FROM scores s JOIN players p ON p.id = s.player_id
            WHERE s.round_id = ? AND s.gross_score > 0 ORDER BY s.player_id, s.hole`
        ).bind(r.id).all();

        const byPlayer = new Map();                           // keyed by trip_player_id
        for (const sc of (srows || [])) {
          if (sc.roster_id == null) {                         // ⭐ MISS — report it, never guess it
            if (!seenMiss.has(sc.player_id)) { seenMiss.add(sc.player_id); out.unlinked++; out.unlinked_names.push(sc.name); }
            continue;
          }
          const tpid = tpByRoster.get(sc.roster_id);
          if (tpid == null) continue;                         // on the event but not on the trip — not a miss
          if (!byPlayer.has(tpid)) byPlayer.set(tpid, { name: sc.name, handicap: sc.handicap, holes: {} });
          byPlayer.get(tpid).holes[sc.hole] = sc.gross_score;
        }

        for (const [tpid, rec] of byPlayer) {
          const courseHcp  = calcCourseHandicapFull(Number(rec.handicap) || 0, teeData.slope, teeData.rating, teeData.par);
          const playingHcp = tbApplyAllowance(courseHcp, cfg, 0);  // the allowance the event's OWN board applies
          const holeNums   = Object.keys(rec.holes).map(Number).sort((a, b) => a - b);
          let pts = 0, gross = 0, netTot = 0;
          const holeScores = {}, holePts = new Array(18).fill(null);
          for (const h of holeNums) {
            const par  = pars ? pars[h - 1] : 4;
            const sidx = si   ? si[h - 1]   : h;
            const net  = rec.holes[h] - hcpStrokesForHole(sidx, playingHcp);
            const p    = Math.max(0, 2 + (par - net));        // ⭐ the SAME ramp as the casual rail — points stay commensurable
            pts += p; gross += rec.holes[h]; netTot += net;
            holeScores[h] = rec.holes[h];
            if (h >= 1 && h <= 18) holePts[h - 1] = p;
          }
          (out.cells[tpid] = out.cells[tpid] || []).push({
            source: 'event',                                  // ⭐ the client tells the rails apart on this
            event_id: ev.id, event_name: ev.name, event_round_id: r.id,
            round_number: r.round_number, folds_pts: foldsPts,
            pts: foldsPts ? pts : 0,                          // low-wins contributes 0 — v2.69.0 bind
            pts_raw: pts, holes: holeNums.length, gross, net: netTot,
            hole_scores: holeScores, hole_pts: holePts, si, pars,
            playing_handicap: playingHcp, hcp_derived: true,
            tee_name: tee, course_name: null,
            date: r.date || ev.date || null,                  // TEXT date — equal strings = same day as a trip_round
          });
        }
        out.linked += byPlayer.size;
        out.rounds.push({
          event_id: ev.id, event_name: ev.name, event_round_id: r.id,
          round_number: r.round_number, date: r.date || ev.date || null,
          format: fmt, status: r.status, folds_pts: foldsPts,
          low_keys: lowKeys, scored_players: byPlayer.size,
        });
      }
    }
  } catch (_rail) { return Object.assign(empty, { ok: false }); }
  return out;
}

async function handleTripLeaderboard(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const pin  = url.searchParams.get('pin') || req.headers.get('X-Team-Pin') || '';
  const code = (url.searchParams.get('code') || '').trim().toUpperCase(); // v2.35.0 — trip_code is now a watch credential too
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM trips WHERE id = ?').bind(tripId).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const pinOk  = pin  !== '' && String(trip.viewer_pin) === String(pin);
  const codeOk = code !== '' && String(trip.trip_code || '').toUpperCase() === code;
  if (!pinOk && !codeOk) return err('Invalid PIN', 401, origin);
  const { results: rounds } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.*, c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.par AS tee_par, ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si, ct.rating, ct.slope
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id = ? AND tr.status IN ('active','complete') ORDER BY tr.round_number`
  ).bind(tripId).all();
  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tp.id AS trip_player_id, tp.current_index, r.name, r.handicap
     FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     WHERE tp.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
  ).bind(tripId).all();
  // v2.35.0 (backlog h): two bulk queries replace the old N-players x M-rounds
  // per-cell loop (~70 queries/call on an 11-player, 3-round trip -> 4 total).
  const roundIds = rounds.map(r => r.id);
  const ttpByKey = {}, scoresByKey = {}, ttSetByRound = {}; // v2.60.0 — distinct tee times WITH players, per round
  if (roundIds.length) {
    const ph = roundIds.map(() => '?').join(',');
    const { results: ttps } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT tt.trip_round_id, ttp.trip_player_id, ttp.playing_handicap, tt.id AS tee_time_id, tt.start_time, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si, ct.par AS tee_par, ct.rating AS tee_rating, ct.slope AS tee_slope
       FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
       JOIN trip_tee_times tt ON tt.id = ttp.trip_tee_time_id
       LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = ttp.tee_box_id
       WHERE tt.trip_round_id IN (${ph})`
    ).bind(...roundIds).all();
    for (const t of ttps) {
      ttpByKey[t.trip_round_id + ':' + t.trip_player_id] = t;
      (ttSetByRound[t.trip_round_id] = ttSetByRound[t.trip_round_id] || new Set()).add(t.tee_time_id); // v2.60.0
    }
    const { results: allScores } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT trip_round_id, trip_player_id, hole, gross_score FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_round_id IN (${ph}) ORDER BY hole`
    ).bind(...roundIds).all();
    for (const s of allScores) {
      const k = s.trip_round_id + ':' + s.trip_player_id;
      (scoresByKey[k] = scoresByKey[k] || []).push(s);
    }
  }
  const standings = [];
  for (const player of players) {
    let totalPts = 0, totalGross = 0, totalNet = 0;                    // v2.65.0
    const roundBreakdown = [];
    for (const round of rounds) {
      const key = round.id + ':' + player.trip_player_id;
      const ttp = ttpByKey[key] || null;
      const scores = scoresByKey[key] || [];
      if (!scores.length) { roundBreakdown.push({ round_number: round.round_number, pts: null, holes: 0, tee_time_id: ttp?.tee_time_id ?? null, start_time: ttp?.start_time ?? null, tee_name: ttp?.tee_name ?? null }); continue; }
      const holePars   = ttp?.pars ? JSON.parse(ttp.pars) : (round.pars ? JSON.parse(round.pars) : null);
      const holeSI     = ttp?.si   ? JSON.parse(ttp.si)   : (round.si   ? JSON.parse(round.si)   : null);
      let playingHcp = ttp?.playing_handicap;                            // v2.42.0 — trip twin of the league fix:
      let hcpDerived = false;                                            // NULL used to become 0 strokes silently.
      if (playingHcp === null || playingHcp === undefined) {
        const idx   = player.current_index ?? player.handicap;
        const slope = ttp?.tee_slope  ?? round.slope;
        const rate  = ttp?.tee_rating ?? round.rating;
        const tpar  = ttp?.tee_par    ?? round.tee_par;
        if (idx != null && slope != null && rate != null && tpar != null) {
          playingHcp = calcCourseHandicapFull(idx, slope, rate, tpar);
          hcpDerived = true;
        } else {
          playingHcp = 0;
        }
      }
      let roundPts = 0, gross = 0, netTot = 0;                        // v2.65.0 — net total (netTot: the hole loop already owns a const net)
      const holeScores = {}; // v2.35.0 — hole map for the client detail expander
      const holePts = new Array(18).fill(null); // v2.37.0 — so the trip grid can
                                                // colour by the SERVER's points
                                                // instead of recomputing them.
      for (const s of scores) {
        const h = s.hole - 1;
        const par = holePars ? holePars[h] : 4;
        const si  = holeSI  ? holeSI[h]   : h + 1;
        const net = s.gross_score - hcpStrokesForHole(si, playingHcp);   // v2.37.0
        const diff = par - net;
        const pts = Math.max(0, 2 + diff);                                 // v2.43.0 standard ramp (ace override dropped)
        roundPts += pts;
        gross += s.gross_score;
        if (s.gross_score > 0) netTot += net;                          // v2.65.0 — reuse the hole's const net; same score>0 gate as league hole_net
        holeScores[s.hole] = s.gross_score;
        if (h >= 0 && h <= 17) holePts[h] = pts;                          // v2.37.0
      }
      totalPts += roundPts;
      totalGross += gross; totalNet += netTot;                             // v2.65.0
      roundBreakdown.push({
        round_number: round.round_number, pts: roundPts, holes: scores.length,
        gross, net: netTot,                                                // v2.65.0 — net beside gross
        hole_scores: holeScores,                                   // v2.35.0
        hole_pts: holePts, si: holeSI,                                    // v2.37.0
        pars: holePars, playing_handicap: playingHcp,                     // v2.35.0 — client renders net/strokes
        hcp_derived: hcpDerived,                                          // v2.42.0
        tee_time_id: ttp?.tee_time_id ?? null, start_time: ttp?.start_time ?? null, // v2.60.0 — ticker tee-time order
        tee_name: ttp?.tee_name ?? null,                                  // v2.61.0 — report: the player's OWN tee
        course_name: round.course_name, date: round.date,
      });
    }
    standings.push({ trip_player_id: player.trip_player_id, name: player.name, handicap: player.current_index ?? player.handicap, total_pts: totalPts, total_gross: totalGross, total_net: totalNet, rounds: roundBreakdown }); // v2.62.0 — current GC index for the reports · v2.65.0 — gross/net totals
  }
  // ⭐ v2.69.0 — the trip OVERALL sort is deliberately UNTOUCHED. Spec v1 §1
  // BIND: a low-wins game contributes nothing to the trip overall standings.
  // It posts its own board on the round and stops there — which is what the
  // per-round low_boards below are, and all they are.

  // v2.94.0 — FR-31: fold the GAME-DAY rail in BEFORE the sort. Every cell
  // already carries pts: 0 when the round is low-wins, so the bind above is
  // held by construction and the sort below stays byte-for-byte untouched.
  const _rail = await tripEventRail(env, tripId);
  for (const st of standings) {
    for (const cell of (_rail.cells[st.trip_player_id] || [])) {
      st.total_pts   += cell.pts   || 0;
      st.total_gross += cell.gross || 0;
      st.total_net   += cell.net   || 0;
      st.rounds.push(cell);                                   // tagged source:'event'
    }
  }
  standings.sort((a, b) => b.total_pts - a.total_pts);

  const low_boards = {};                                                  // v2.69.0 — keyed by trip_round id
  for (const r of rounds) {
    const _g    = _gamesParse(r.games);
    const _list = _gamesList(_g, r.format || null);
    const _lows = _list.filter(k => LOW_WINS_FORMATS.has(k));
    if (!_lows.length) continue;
    const rows = [];
    for (const st of standings) {
      const rb = st.rounds.find(x => x.round_number === r.round_number);
      if (rb) rows.push({ key: st.trip_player_id, name: st.name, net: rb.net, gross: rb.gross, holes: rb.holes });
    }
    const board = {};
    for (const k of _lows) board[k] = lowBoard(rows, k === 'low_net' ? 'net' : 'gross', 'holes');
    low_boards[r.id] = board;
  }
  // v2.94.0 — FR-31: a low-wins GAME round posts its board here too, keyed
  // 'e:<round_id>' so the existing trip_round keys are untouched.
  for (const er of _rail.rounds) {
    if (!er.low_keys.length) continue;
    const rows = [];
    for (const st of standings) {
      const rb = st.rounds.find(x => x.source === 'event' && x.event_round_id === er.event_round_id);
      if (rb) rows.push({ key: st.trip_player_id, name: st.name, net: rb.net, gross: rb.gross, holes: rb.holes });
    }
    const board = {};
    for (const k of er.low_keys) board[k] = lowBoard(rows, k === 'low_net' ? 'net' : 'gross', 'holes');
    low_boards['e:' + er.event_round_id] = board;
  }

  return json({
    trip: { id: trip.id, name: trip.name, destination: trip.destination, start_date: trip.start_date, end_date: trip.end_date,
            trip_code: codeOk || pinOk ? trip.trip_code : null },          // v2.35.0
    standings,
    rounds: rounds.map(r => ({ id: r.id, round_number: r.round_number, date: r.date, course_name: r.course_name, label: r.label, tee_name: r.tee_name, status: r.status, tee_times: (ttSetByRound[r.id] ? ttSetByRound[r.id].size : 0), // v2.60.0 — status + tee-time count for the ticker gate
      format: r.format || null, games: _gamesParse(r.games), games_list: _gamesList(_gamesParse(r.games), r.format || null) })), // v2.69.0
    low_boards,                                                           // v2.69.0
    game_rounds: _rail.rounds,                                            // v2.94.0 — FR-31: the game-day rail, for the client's day grouping
    unlinked_players: _rail.unlinked,                                     // ⭐ count of event players with NULL roster_id — show "N players not linked"
    unlinked_names: _rail.unlinked_names,                                 // ⭐ who, so the organizer can fix them
    rail_ok: _rail.ok,                                                    // false = the game-day rail failed soft; casual board is still correct
  }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.35.0 — public 6-char trip watch-code lookup (mirrors handleTripByViewerPin;
// trip_code was previously generated-but-unused). 404 by design when the code
// is not a trip — the client Watch cascade falls through on 404 ONLY (v243.1 lesson).
async function handleTripWatchByCode(req, env, origin) {
  const code = (new URL(req.url).pathname.split('/').pop() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{6}$/.test(code)) return err('Invalid code format', 400, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, trip_code FROM trips WHERE UPPER(trip_code) = ?').bind(code).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  return json({ trip_id: trip.id, trip_code: trip.trip_code }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleListTripNassau(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT n.*, ra.name AS player_a_name, rb.name AS player_b_name, tr.round_number, tr.date
     FROM trip_nassau n
     JOIN trip_players tpa ON tpa.id = n.player_a_id JOIN roster ra ON ra.id = tpa.roster_id
     JOIN trip_players tpb ON tpb.id = n.player_b_id JOIN roster rb ON rb.id = tpb.roster_id
     JOIN trip_rounds tr ON tr.id = n.trip_round_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id = ? ORDER BY tr.round_number, n.id`
  ).bind(tripId).all();
  return json({ nassau: results }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleUpsertTripNassau(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.trip_round_id || !body.player_a_id || !body.player_b_id) return err('trip_round_id, player_a_id, player_b_id required', 400, origin);
  const result = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trip_nassau (trip_round_id, player_a_id, player_b_id, bet_amount, currency,
       front_winner, back_winner, overall_winner, front_net, back_net, overall_net, press_count)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
     ON CONFLICT(trip_round_id, player_a_id, player_b_id) DO UPDATE SET
       bet_amount=excluded.bet_amount, currency=excluded.currency,
       front_winner=excluded.front_winner, back_winner=excluded.back_winner,
       overall_winner=excluded.overall_winner, front_net=excluded.front_net,
       back_net=excluded.back_net, overall_net=excluded.overall_net,
       press_count=excluded.press_count
     RETURNING *`
  ).bind(
    body.trip_round_id, body.player_a_id, body.player_b_id,
    body.bet_amount || null, body.currency || 'CAD',
    body.front_winner || null, body.back_winner || null, body.overall_winner || null,
    body.front_net || null, body.back_net || null, body.overall_net || null,
    body.press_count || 0
  ).first();
  return json({ nassau: result }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetTripRoundScores(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const round = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_rounds WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?').bind(roundId, tripId).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT ts.*, r.name AS player_name, tp.current_index
     FROM trip_scores ts
     JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ts.trip_player_id
     JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     WHERE ts.trip_round_id = ? ORDER BY r.name, ts.hole`
  ).bind(roundId).all();
  return json({ scores: results }, 200, origin);
}

// ═══ FR-28 (v3.00.0) — GET /api/join-trip/:code ════════════════════════════
// The trip twin of handleJoinRound. Code-authenticated exactly like the trip
// score rail: possession of the foursome code IS the credential, no session,
// no org token — that is what lets Lite reach it.
//
// ⭐ EVERY SELECT HERE IS COPIED FROM A PROVEN ORGANIZER ROUTE, not composed
// from a schema guess. trip_tee_times / trip_rounds+courses+course_tees /
// trip_tee_time_players+trip_players+roster are the same statements
// handleGetTripTeeTimes already runs. Inventing a column here would 500 on a
// live trip, and there is no way to test that from the sandbox.
//
// ⛔ ADDITIVE ONLY. This route touches nothing that already exists — no shared
// helper is modified, no existing handler is edited. A worker carrying it
// behaves identically for every client that never calls it.
async function handleJoinTrip(request, env, origin, code) {
  const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM trip_tee_times WHERE foursome_code = ?'
  ).bind(code).first();
  if (!tt) return err('Invalid foursome code', 404, origin);

  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.*, c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.id = ?`
  ).bind(tt.trip_round_id).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);

  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT ttp.*, tp.roster_id, tp.current_index, tp.ghin,
            r.name, r.handicap, r.home_club,
            ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
     JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ttp.trip_player_id
     JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = ttp.tee_box_id
     WHERE ttp.trip_tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY ttp.slot_order`
  ).bind(tt.id).all();

  // Scores already posted, so a phone that rejoins mid-round paints the card it
  // left rather than an empty one — the same contract handleJoinRound honours.
  const ids = (players || []).map(p => p.trip_player_id).filter(x => x != null);
  const existingScores = {};
  if (ids.length) {
    const { results: sc } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT trip_player_id, hole, gross_score FROM trip_scores
       WHERE trip_round_id = ? AND trip_player_id IN (${ids.map(() => '?').join(',')})`
    ).bind(round.id, ...ids).all();
    for (const s of (sc || [])) {
      if (!existingScores[s.trip_player_id]) existingScores[s.trip_player_id] = {};
      existingScores[s.trip_player_id][s.hole] = s.gross_score;
    }
  }

  return json({
    foursome: {
      id: tt.id,
      code: tt.foursome_code,
      start_time: tt.start_time ?? null,
      hole: tt.hole ?? null,
      trip_id: round.trip_id,
      round_id: round.id,
      round_number: round.round_number ?? null,
      date: round.date ?? null,
      course_id: round.course_id ?? null,
      course_name: round.course_name ?? null,
      tee: round.tee_name ?? null,
      format: round.format ?? null,
      // ⭐ The flag Lite branches on. A trip card posts to
      // /api/trips/:trip_id/rounds/:round_id/scores with trip_player_id;
      // an event card posts to /api/scores/round with player_name. Same
      // screen, two rails — and the payload says which.
      trip: true,
    },
    players: players || [],
    existing_scores: existingScores,
  }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleSubmitTripScores(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.foursome_code || !body?.trip_player_id || !Array.isArray(body.scores)) {
    return err('foursome_code, trip_player_id, and scores[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, trip_round_id FROM trip_tee_times WHERE foursome_code = ? AND trip_round_id = ?'
  ).bind(body.foursome_code.toUpperCase(), roundId).first();
  if (!tt) return err('Invalid foursome code', 404, origin);
  const tp = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_players WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?').bind(body.trip_player_id, tripId).first();
  if (!tp) return err('Player not on this trip', 404, origin);
  const stmts = body.scores.map(s =>
    env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO trip_scores (trip_round_id, trip_player_id, hole, gross_score, stableford_pts, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
       ON CONFLICT(trip_round_id, trip_player_id, hole)
       DO UPDATE SET gross_score=excluded.gross_score, stableford_pts=excluded.stableford_pts, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
    ).bind(roundId, body.trip_player_id, s.hole, s.gross_score, s.stableford_pts ?? null)
  );
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  // Auto-settle bets if applicable
  await maybeAutoSettleBetsRace(env, null, null, tripId, roundId);
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.92.0 — FR-25: casual-round side-game money → the trip ledger.
// Code-authenticated like scores (the foursome code must belong to THIS
// round). Upsert per (round, player): a re-save REPLACES, never doubles —
// D83's spirit: the ledger reports what the games computed, it never invents
// or moves money. Body: { foursome_code, money: [{trip_player_id, cents, detail?}] }
async function handleSubmitTripRoundMoney(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.foursome_code || !Array.isArray(body.money)) {
    return err('foursome_code and money[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id FROM trip_tee_times WHERE foursome_code = ? AND trip_round_id = ?'
  ).bind(String(body.foursome_code).toUpperCase(), roundId).first();
  if (!tt) return err('Invalid foursome code', 404, origin);
  const stmts = [];
  for (const m of body.money) {
    if (!m || !m.trip_player_id || !Number.isFinite(Number(m.cents))) continue;
    const tp = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trip_players WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?').bind(m.trip_player_id, tripId).first();
    if (!tp) continue;
    const detail = m.detail == null ? null
      : (typeof m.detail === 'string' ? m.detail : JSON.stringify(m.detail)).slice(0, 4000);
    stmts.push(env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO trip_round_money (trip_round_id, trip_player_id, cents, detail, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
       ON CONFLICT(trip_round_id, trip_player_id)
       DO UPDATE SET cents=excluded.cents, detail=excluded.detail, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
    ).bind(roundId, m.trip_player_id, Math.round(Number(m.cents)), detail));
  }
  if (stmts.length) await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  return json({ ok: true, rows: stmts.length }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.93.0 — FR-26 phase 1: game-day (event) round side-game money → the trip
// ledger. Twin of handleSubmitTripRoundMoney riding the EVENT rail: the group
// code is the scores rail's tee_time_groups.code (uppercased) and must belong
// to THIS round; players are validated against THIS event, the same scoping
// the scores rail uses. Upsert per (round, player): a re-save REPLACES, never
// doubles — D83's spirit: the ledger reports what the games computed, it
// never invents or moves money.
// Body: { group_code, money: [{player_id, cents, detail?}] }
async function handleSubmitEventRoundMoney(req, env, origin, eventId, roundId) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.group_code || !Array.isArray(body.money)) {
    return err('group_code and money[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  const ttg = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT ttg.id FROM tee_time_groups ttg JOIN tee_times tt ON tt.id = ttg.tee_time_id WHERE ttg.code = ? AND tt.round_id = ?'
  ).bind(String(body.group_code).toUpperCase(), roundId).first();
  if (!ttg) return err('Invalid foursome code', 404, origin);
  const stmts = [];
  for (const m of body.money) {
    if (!m || !m.player_id || !Number.isFinite(Number(m.cents))) continue;
    const p = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM players WHERE id = ? AND event_id = ?').bind(m.player_id, eventId).first();
    if (!p) continue;
    const detail = m.detail == null ? null
      : (typeof m.detail === 'string' ? m.detail : JSON.stringify(m.detail)).slice(0, 4000);
    stmts.push(env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO event_round_money (round_id, player_id, cents, detail, updated_at)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, datetime('now'))
       ON CONFLICT(round_id, player_id)
       DO UPDATE SET cents=excluded.cents, detail=excluded.detail, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
    ).bind(roundId, m.player_id, Math.round(Number(m.cents)), detail));
  }
  if (stmts.length) await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  return json({ ok: true, rows: stmts.length }, 200, origin);
}

/**
 * v2.98.0 — GET /api/events/:eventId/rounds/:roundId/money   (FR-42 §E)
 *
 * Read side of the FR-26 phase-1 write. Returns this ROUND's side-game money,
 * scoped by the round belonging to the event, so a report that only knows
 * (eventId, roundId) can render the day's side games without loading a trip.
 *
 * Same SELECT shape the trip GET uses (v2.97.0, B-116): player_name rides ON
 * the row via JOIN players, so no consumer depends on a name cache. One query
 * shape, two callers — a second projection is how the two would drift.
 * Read-only and it invents nothing: no round, no rows, or a pre-migration DB
 * all return { money: [] }. A caller cannot tell "none" from "table missing",
 * and must not — either way the honest render is no section at all.
 */
async function handleGetEventRoundMoney(req, env, origin, eventId, roundId) {
  let money = [];
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT erm.round_id, r.event_id, erm.player_id, p.name AS player_name,
       erm.cents, erm.detail, erm.updated_at
       FROM event_round_money erm
       JOIN rounds r ON r.id = erm.round_id
       JOIN players p ON p.id = erm.player_id
       WHERE erm.round_id = ? AND r.event_id = ?
       ORDER BY erm.cents DESC`
    ).bind(roundId, eventId).all();
    money = results || [];
  } catch (e) { money = []; }
  return json({ money }, 200, origin);
}

/**
 * v2.41.0 — POST /api/trips/:id/rounds/:rid/refresh-handicaps
 * Code-authenticated (no organizer session). The scorer's phone has already
 * pulled fresh Golf Canada indexes for the whole group; this writes them
 * through so the join payload, live scoring and the leaderboard agree.
 *
 * Body: { foursome_code, players: [{ trip_player_id, handicap }] }
 * Auth: the code must belong to THIS round, and each trip_player_id must
 *       already be in that code's foursome. Anything else is skipped.
 */
async function handleRefreshTripHandicaps(req, env, origin, tripId, roundId) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.foursome_code || !Array.isArray(body.players)) {
    return err('foursome_code and players[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  const round = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, tee_box_id FROM trip_rounds WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?'
  ).bind(roundId, tripId).first();
  if (!round) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);

  const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id FROM trip_tee_times WHERE foursome_code = ? AND trip_round_id = ?'
  ).bind(String(body.foursome_code).toUpperCase(), roundId).first();
  if (!tt) return err('Invalid foursome code', 404, origin);

  // The code may only touch players in its OWN foursome.
  const { results: slots } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT ttp.trip_player_id, ttp.tee_box_id, tp.roster_id, tp.current_index
     FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
     JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ttp.trip_player_id AND tp.trip_id = ?
     WHERE ttp.trip_tee_time_id = ?`
  ).bind(tripId, tt.id).all();
  const allowed = new Map(slots.map(r => [Number(r.trip_player_id), r]));

  const teeCache = new Map();
  const getTee = async (id) => {
    if (id == null) return null;
    if (!teeCache.has(id)) {
      teeCache.set(id, await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT rating, slope, par FROM course_tees WHERE id = ?'
      ).bind(id).first());
    }
    return teeCache.get(id);
  };

  const updated = [], skipped = [];
  for (const entry of body.players) {
    const tpid = Number(entry?.trip_player_id);
    // NB: Number(null) === 0 and JSON turns NaN into null — a missing handicap
    // must NOT quietly become a 0 index. Reject blanks explicitly.
    const raw  = entry?.handicap;
    const idx  = (raw === null || raw === undefined || raw === '') ? NaN : Number(raw);
    const slot = allowed.get(tpid);
    if (!slot) { skipped.push({ trip_player_id: entry?.trip_player_id, reason: 'not_in_foursome' }); continue; }
    if (!Number.isFinite(idx)) { skipped.push({ trip_player_id: tpid, reason: 'bad_handicap' }); continue; }

    const teeBoxId = slot.tee_box_id || round.tee_box_id || null;
    const teeBox   = await getTee(teeBoxId);
    let courseHcp = null;
    if (teeBox) courseHcp = calcCourseHandicapFull(idx, teeBox.slope, teeBox.rating, teeBox.par);

    const stmts = [
      env.DB.prepare("UPDATE trip_players SET current_index = ?, last_index_pull = datetime('now') WHERE id = ? AND trip_id = ?").bind(idx, tpid, tripId),
      env.DB.prepare('UPDATE trip_tee_time_players SET course_handicap = ?, playing_handicap = ? WHERE trip_tee_time_id = ? AND trip_player_id = ?')
        .bind(courseHcp, courseHcp, tt.id, tpid),
    ];
    if (slot.roster_id != null) {
      stmts.push(env.DB.prepare('UPDATE roster SET handicap = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(idx, slot.roster_id));
    }
    await env.DB.batch(stmts);
    updated.push({ trip_player_id: tpid, handicap: idx, course_handicap: courseHcp, previous_index: slot.current_index });
  }
  return json({ ok: true, updated, skipped }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.79.0 — D13/D25e delivery, the s66 debt ("a dead switch is worse than no
// switch"). trips.config exists since the s66 ALTER; handleUpdateTrip's
// whitelist keeps it unwritable by accident, so this is THE writer. Same
// _cfgMerge merge-patch contract as handlePatchLeagueConfig: null deletes a
// key, everything else replaces wholesale.
async function handlePatchTripConfig(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, config FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(body)) return err('Body must be a JSON object', 400, origin);
  const keys = Object.keys(body);
  if (!keys.length) return err('Nothing to change', 400, origin);
  const current = _cfgParse(trip.config);
  const [merged, changed] = _cfgMerge(current, body, ['scorecard_ticker']);
  await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE trips SET config = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(JSON.stringify(merged), id).run();
  return json({ ok: true, trip_id: id, changed, config: merged }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleJoinTrip(req, env, origin, code) {
  const tt = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT ttt.*, tr.trip_id, tr.round_number, tr.course_id, tr.tee_box_id, tr.date, tr.label, tr.format,
            t.name AS trip_name, t.destination, t.viewer_pin, t.config AS trip_config,
            c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par,
            ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si
     FROM trip_tee_times ttt
     JOIN trip_rounds tr ON tr.id = ttt.trip_round_id
     JOIN trips t ON t.id = tr.trip_id
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE ttt.foursome_code = ?`
  ).bind(code).first();
  if (!tt) return err('Invalid code', 404, origin);
  // v2.79.0 — D13/D25e: a league round IS a trip record (league_rounds.trip_id),
  // so the league's config rides the same lookup. Fail-soft: no league, or a
  // pre-config schema, simply yields null.
  let leagueCfg = null;
  try {
    const lr = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT l.config FROM league_rounds lr JOIN leagues l ON l.id = lr.league_id WHERE lr.trip_id = ? LIMIT 1'
    ).bind(tt.trip_id).first();
    if (lr && lr.config) leagueCfg = JSON.parse(lr.config);
  } catch (_lc) { leagueCfg = null; }
  let tripCfg = null;
  try { tripCfg = tt.trip_config ? JSON.parse(tt.trip_config) : null; } catch (_tc) { tripCfg = null; }
  const pin = req.headers.get('X-Team-Pin') || '';
  const pinValid = pin && String(tt.foursome_pin) === String(pin);
  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT ttp.trip_player_id, ttp.tee_box_id, ttp.course_handicap, ttp.playing_handicap, ttp.slot_order,
            r.name, r.handicap, tp.current_index, tp.ghin, r.ghin AS roster_ghin, -- v2.41.0
            ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
     JOIN trip_players tp ON tp.id = ttp.trip_player_id
     JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = ttp.tee_box_id
     WHERE ttp.trip_tee_time_id = ? ORDER BY ttp.slot_order`
  ).bind(tt.id).all();
  const existingScores = {};
  for (const p of players) {
    const { results: scores } = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT hole, gross_score FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_round_id = ? AND trip_player_id = ? ORDER BY hole'
    ).bind(tt.trip_round_id, p.trip_player_id).all();
    existingScores[p.trip_player_id] = {};
    scores.forEach(s => { existingScores[p.trip_player_id][s.hole] = s.gross_score; });
  }
  return json({
    foursome: {
      id: tt.id, code: tt.foursome_code, start_time: tt.start_time, hole: tt.hole,
      trip_round_id: tt.trip_round_id, round_number: tt.round_number, date: tt.date, label: tt.label,
      trip_id: tt.trip_id, trip_name: tt.trip_name, destination: tt.destination,
      format: tt.format || null, viewer_pin: tt.viewer_pin || null,
      course_id: tt.course_id, course_name: tt.course_name, tee_name: tt.tee_name,
      rating: tt.rating, slope: tt.slope, tee_par: tt.tee_par,
      pars: tt.pars ? JSON.parse(tt.pars) : null,
      si:   tt.si   ? JSON.parse(tt.si)   : null,
      trip_config: tripCfg,     // v2.79.0 — D13/D25e (parsed trips.config or null)
      league_config: leagueCfg, // v2.79.0 — D13/D25e (parsed leagues.config or null)
    },
    players, existing_scores: existingScores, pin_valid: pinValid,
  }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleTripByViewerPin(req, env, origin) {
  const pin = new URL(req.url).pathname.split('/').pop();
  if (!pin || !/^\d{4}$/.test(pin)) return err('Invalid PIN format', 400, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE viewer_pin = ?').bind(pin).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  return json({ trip_id: trip.id }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DUPLICATE (v2.53.0) — event / trip / league quick-starts
// ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Bruce's ask: next year's Club Championship / Last Call / league
// season / Scottsdale trip is 90% the same people. Duplicate copies
// PEOPLE + SETTINGS ONLY into a fresh draft with fresh codes:
//   event  → new events row (status draft, new event_code/viewer_pin,
//            config copied verbatim — event_roster/scoring/flights ride
//            along) + every group with its roster links, FRESH group
//            codes/team pins. NO scores, players rows, tee shots,
//            rounds, tee sheets, leaderboard_cache, bets races.
//            ineligible_reason is deliberately NOT copied — eligibility
//            is re-earned each year.
//   trip   → new trips row (draft, new trip_code/viewer_pin) +
//            trip_players (roster link, ghin, current_index — indexes
//            refresh on the next GHIN pull). NO rounds/tee times/scores/
//            nassau.
//   league → new leagues row (new watch_code) + league_members.
//            NO league_rounds (weeks are played, not copied).
// Body {name?, date?/start_date?/end_date?} — name defaults to
// "<original> (copy)" so a fat-finger never collides with the source.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function handleDuplicateEvent(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!ev) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const event_code = await uniqueEventCode(env);
  const viewer_pin = generatePin();
  const name = String(body.name || (ev.name + ' (copy)')).trim();
  const date = body.date || ev.date;
  const newEv = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO events (organizer_id, name, course_id, course_name, tee, date, format, status, config, event_code, viewer_pin, rules)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft', ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(account.id, name, ev.course_id, ev.course_name, ev.tee, date, ev.format,
         ev.config || '{}', event_code, viewer_pin, ev.rules || null).first();
  // Teams carry the people on scramble-family events — copy each group and
  // its roster links with fresh codes/pins. Sequential uniqueCode calls keep
  // the same collision guarantee as handleCreateGroup.
  const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM groups WHERE event_id = ? ORDER BY id').bind(id).all();
  let copiedGroups = 0, copiedMembers = 0;
  for (const g of groups) {
    const code = await uniqueCode(env);
    const ng = await env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO groups (event_id, name, code, team_pin, start_hole, course_id, tee, organizer_id, team_handicap)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING id`
    ).bind(newEv.id, g.name, code, generatePin(), g.start_hole, g.course_id, g.tee, g.organizer_id ?? null, g.team_handicap ?? null).first();
    const { results: members } = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT roster_id, sort_order FROM group_roster_players WHERE group_id = ? ORDER BY sort_order'
    ).bind(g.id).all();
    for (const m of members) {
      await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO group_roster_players (group_id, roster_id, sort_order) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')
        .bind(ng.id, m.roster_id, m.sort_order).run();
      copiedMembers++;
    }
    copiedGroups++;
  }
  return json({ event: newEv, copied: { groups: copiedGroups, members: copiedMembers } }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleDuplicateTrip(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const trip_code = await uniqueTripCode(env);
  const viewer_pin = generatePin();
  const name = String(body.name || (trip.name + ' (copy)')).trim();
  const newTrip = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trips (organizer_id, name, destination, start_date, end_date, trip_code, viewer_pin, status)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft') RETURNING *`
  ).bind(account.id, name, trip.destination || null,
         body.start_date || trip.start_date || null, body.end_date || trip.end_date || null,
         trip_code, viewer_pin).first();
  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT roster_id, ghin, current_index, last_index_pull FROM trip_players WHERE trip_id = ? ORDER BY id'
  ).bind(id).all();
  for (const p of players) {
    await env.DB.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO trip_players (trip_id, roster_id, ghin, current_index, last_index_pull) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)'
    ).bind(newTrip.id, p.roster_id ?? null, p.ghin || null, p.current_index ?? null, p.last_index_pull || null).run();
  }
  return json({ trip: newTrip, copied: { players: players.length } }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleDuplicateLeague(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const watch_code = await uniqueWatchCode(env);
  const name = String(body.name || (league.name + ' (copy)')).trim();
  const newLeague = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO leagues (organizer_id, name, description, home_course_id, watch_code) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(account.id, name, league.description || null, league.home_course_id ?? null, watch_code).first();
  const { results: members } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT roster_id FROM league_members WHERE league_id = ? ORDER BY id'
  ).bind(id).all();
  for (const m of members) {
    await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO league_members (league_id, roster_id) VALUES (?, ?)').bind(newLeague.id, m.roster_id).run();
  }
  return json({ league: newLeague, copied: { members: members.length } }, 201, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// LEAGUE HANDLERS (v2.29.0)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function handleCreateLeague(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.name) return err('name required', 400, origin);
  const watch_code = await uniqueWatchCode(env);                                  // v2.36.0
  const league = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO leagues (organizer_id, name, description, home_course_id, watch_code) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(account.id, body.name.trim(), body.description || null, body.home_course_id || null, watch_code).first();
  return json({ league }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleListLeagues(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const { results: leagues } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT l.*, c.name AS home_course_name,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM league_members lm WHERE lm.league_id = l.id) AS member_count,
      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM league_rounds lr WHERE lr.league_id = l.id) AS round_count,
      (SELECT MIN(lr.round_date) FROM league_rounds lr WHERE lr.league_id = l.id) AS first_round_date,
      (SELECT MAX(lr.round_date) FROM league_rounds lr WHERE lr.league_id = l.id) AS last_round_date
     FROM leagues l LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = l.home_course_id
     WHERE l.organizer_id = ? ORDER BY l.created_at DESC`
  ).bind(account.id).all();
  return json({ leagues }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetLeague(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT l.*, c.name AS home_course_name FROM leagues l
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = l.home_course_id
     WHERE l.id = ? AND l.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: members } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT lm.id, lm.roster_id, lm.joined_at, r.name, r.handicap, r.home_club, r.email, r.buddy
     FROM league_members lm JOIN roster r ON r.id = lm.roster_id
     WHERE lm.league_id = ? ORDER BY r.buddy DESC, r.name`
  ).bind(id).all();
  // v2.68.2 — lr.config rides along so a setup screen can EDIT a week instead
  // of blind-overwriting it. Named round_config to match the barnes handler's
  // existing alias rather than inventing a second name for the same column.
  const { results: rounds } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT lr.id AS league_round_id, lr.round_date, lr.trip_id, lr.config AS round_config,
            t.name AS trip_name, t.viewer_pin, t.status,
            (SELECT tr.format FROM trip_rounds tr WHERE tr.trip_id = t.id ORDER BY tr.round_number ASC LIMIT 1) AS format,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trip_players tp WHERE tp.trip_id = t.id) AS player_count,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trip_tee_times tt JOIN trip_rounds tr ON tr.id = tt.trip_round_id WHERE tr.trip_id = t.id) AS group_count,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT ts.trip_player_id FROM trip_scores ts JOIN trip_rounds tr2 ON tr2.id = ts.trip_round_id WHERE tr2.trip_id = t.id GROUP BY ts.trip_player_id HAVING COUNT(*) >= 18)) AS players_done
     FROM league_rounds lr JOIN trips t ON t.id = lr.trip_id
     WHERE lr.league_id = ? ORDER BY lr.round_date DESC, lr.created_at DESC`
  ).bind(id).all();
  // v2.91.0 — D82: linked team-night events ride BESIDE rounds, never merged
  // into them. Pre-migration (league_events table absent) this degrades to []
  // so the worker is safe to paste before the migration runs.
  let team_nights = [];
  try {
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT e.id, e.name, e.date, e.status, e.event_code, e.created_at, le.added_at, le.config AS link_config
       FROM league_events le JOIN events e ON e.id = le.event_id
       WHERE le.league_id = ? ORDER BY e.date DESC, e.created_at DESC`
    ).bind(id).all();
    team_nights = results || [];
  } catch (e) { team_nights = []; }
  return json({ league, members, rounds, team_nights }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.59.0 — rename a league (name required, description optional). The name
// is not cosmetic: every game day's hidden trip was named by
// leagueDayTripName(league.name, round_date) at birth, so a rename must
// rebuild those or season standings show the old name forever. Custom-name
// rule matches handlePatchLeagueRound's date path: a trip still named exactly
// what create wrote (OLD league name + its own round date) is rebuilt with
// the new name; anything the organizer typed himself is left alone. ONE
// batch — the league row and every trip name move together or not at all.
async function handlePatchLeague(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const name = typeof body.name === 'string' ? body.name.trim() : '';
  if (!name) return err('name required', 400, origin);
  const hasDesc = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, 'description');
  const description = hasDesc && typeof body.description === 'string' && body.description.trim() ? body.description.trim() : null;
  const { results: links } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT lr.round_date, lr.trip_id, t.name AS trip_name
     FROM league_rounds lr JOIN trips t ON t.id = lr.trip_id WHERE lr.league_id = ?`
  ).bind(id).all();
  const stmts = [
    hasDesc
      ? env.DB.prepare('UPDATE leagues SET name = ?, description = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(name, description, id)
      : env.DB.prepare('UPDATE leagues SET name = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(name, id),
  ];
  let renamed_trips = 0;
  for (const l of (links || [])) {
    if (l.trip_name === leagueDayTripName(league.name, l.round_date)) {
      stmts.push(env.DB.prepare('UPDATE trips SET name = ? WHERE id = ?').bind(leagueDayTripName(name, l.round_date), l.trip_id));
      renamed_trips++;
    }
  }
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  return json({ ok: true, league_id: id, name, renamed_trips }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDeleteLeague(req, env, origin, id) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(id, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  // Cascade every game-day TRIP as well — this used to delete only the link
  // rows, silently orphaning each week's trip (players, tee sheet, scores)
  // in D1 forever.
  const { results: links } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT trip_id FROM league_rounds WHERE league_id = ?').bind(id).all();
  const stmts = [];
  for (const l of (links || [])) stmts.push(...tripCascade(env, l.trip_id));
  stmts.push(
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM league_rounds WHERE league_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM league_members WHERE league_id = ?').bind(id),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM leagues WHERE id = ?').bind(id),
  );
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: id }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleAddLeagueMember(req, env, origin, leagueId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.roster_id) return err('roster_id required', 400, origin);
  const player = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM roster WHERE id = ?').bind(body.roster_id).first();
  if (!player) return err('Roster player not found', 404, origin);
  let member;
  try {
    member = await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO league_members (league_id, roster_id) VALUES (?, ?) RETURNING *').bind(leagueId, body.roster_id).first();
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.message && e.message.includes('UNIQUE')) return err('Player already in league', 409, origin);
    throw e;
  }
  const roster = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT name, handicap, home_club FROM roster WHERE id = ?').bind(body.roster_id).first();
  return json({ member: { ...member, ...roster } }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleRemoveLeagueMember(req, env, origin, leagueId, memberId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM league_members WHERE id = ? AND league_id = ?').bind(memberId, leagueId).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.91.0 — D82: link an existing event to a league as a team night.
// Requester must own BOTH the league AND the event. Insert only — the event
// row is untouched. league_events.event_id is UNIQUE, so a second link
// attempt (same or another league) 409s cleanly.
async function handleLinkLeagueEvent(req, env, origin, leagueId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.event_id) return err('event_id required', 400, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(body.event_id, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  let link;
  try {
    link = await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO league_events (league_id, event_id) VALUES (?, ?) RETURNING *').bind(leagueId, body.event_id).first();
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.message && e.message.includes('UNIQUE')) return err('event already linked to a league', 409, origin);
    throw e;
  }
  return json({ link }, 201, origin);
}

// v2.91.0 — D82: unlink a team night from a league.
// DELETE = UNLINK ONLY — NEVER CASCADE TO THE EVENT. A team night is a
// hand-built real event; unlinking must never touch it.
async function handleUnlinkLeagueEvent(req, env, origin, leagueId, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM league_events WHERE league_id = ? AND event_id = ?').bind(leagueId, eventId).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.92.0 — D84: link an event to a trip as a game day. Clone of the D82
// league pair with trips ownership. The ＋Game Day chain calls this right
// after event create; event_id is UNIQUE so an event belongs to at most one
// trip (and the league table already guarantees at most one league).
async function handleLinkTripEvent(req, env, origin, tripId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  if (!body.event_id) return err('event_id required', 400, origin);
  const event = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(body.event_id, account.id).first();
  if (!event) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  let link;
  try {
    link = await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO trip_events (trip_id, event_id) VALUES (?, ?) RETURNING *').bind(tripId, body.event_id).first();
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.message && e.message.includes('UNIQUE')) return err('event already linked to a trip', 409, origin);
    throw e;
  }
  return json({ link }, 201, origin);
}

// v2.92.0 — D84: unlink a game day from a trip. DELETE = UNLINK ONLY at this
// route — the client's delete-day flow unlinks FIRST, then deletes the hidden
// event itself (an orphaned hidden event is a zombie nobody can see).
async function handleUnlinkTripEvent(req, env, origin, tripId, eventId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(tripId, account.id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM trip_events WHERE trip_id = ? AND event_id = ?').bind(tripId, eventId).run();
  return json({ ok: true }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleCreateLeagueRound(req, env, origin, leagueId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => ({}));
  const round_date = body.round_date || null;
  const tripName = body.name || leagueDayTripName(league.name, round_date); // v2.57.0 — shared with the date PATCH
  const { results: existingCodes } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT trip_code FROM trips').all();
  const usedCodes = new Set(existingCodes.map(r => r.trip_code));
  let trip_code;
  do {
    trip_code = Array.from({ length: 6 }, () => CODE_CHARS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CODE_CHARS.length)]).join('');
  } while (usedCodes.has(trip_code));
  const viewer_pin = String(Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000));
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trips (organizer_id, name, destination, start_date, end_date, trip_code, viewer_pin, status)
     VALUES (?, ?, NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft') RETURNING *`
  ).bind(account.id, tripName, round_date, round_date, trip_code, viewer_pin).first();
  const tripRound = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO trip_rounds (trip_id, round_number, date, status, course_id, tee_box_id, format) VALUES (?, 1, ?, 'active', ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(trip.id, round_date, league.home_course_id || null, null, body.format || null).first();
  const leagueRound = await env.DB.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO league_rounds (league_id, trip_id, round_date) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING *'
  ).bind(leagueId, trip.id, round_date).first();
  return json({ trip, tripRound, leagueRound }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleListLeagueRounds(req, env, origin, leagueId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM leagues WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  const { results: rounds } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT lr.id AS league_round_id, lr.round_date, lr.trip_id,
            t.name AS trip_name, t.viewer_pin, t.status, t.trip_code,
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM trip_players tp WHERE tp.trip_id = t.id) AS player_count
     FROM league_rounds lr JOIN trips t ON t.id = lr.trip_id
     WHERE lr.league_id = ? ORDER BY lr.round_date DESC, lr.created_at DESC`
  ).bind(leagueId).all();
  return json({ rounds }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleLeagueRoundLeaderboard(req, env, origin, leagueId, roundId) {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const pin = url.searchParams.get('pin') || req.headers.get('X-Team-Pin') || '';
  const leagueRound = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM league_rounds WHERE id = ? AND league_id = ?').bind(roundId, leagueId).first();
  if (!leagueRound) return err('Round not found', 404, origin);
  const trip = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM trips WHERE id = ?').bind(leagueRound.trip_id).first();
  if (!trip) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  if (String(trip.viewer_pin) !== String(pin)) return err('Invalid PIN', 401, origin);
  const tripRound = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.*, c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.par AS tee_par, ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si, ct.rating, ct.slope
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id = ? ORDER BY tr.round_number LIMIT 1`
  ).bind(leagueRound.trip_id).first();
  const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tp.id AS trip_player_id, tp.current_index, r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap, r.home_club
     FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     WHERE tp.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
  ).bind(leagueRound.trip_id).all();
  const standings = [];
  const skinsIn  = [];                                                   // v2.58.0
  for (const player of players) {
    const ttp = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT ttp.playing_handicap, ttp.course_handicap, ttp.tee_box_id,
              ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si, ct.par AS tee_par, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope
       FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
       JOIN trip_tee_times tt ON tt.id = ttp.trip_tee_time_id
       LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = ttp.tee_box_id
       WHERE tt.trip_round_id = ? AND ttp.trip_player_id = ?`
    ).bind(tripRound ? tripRound.id : 0, player.trip_player_id).first();
    const { results: scores } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT hole, gross_score, stableford_pts FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_round_id = ? AND trip_player_id = ? ORDER BY hole`
    ).bind(tripRound ? tripRound.id : 0, player.trip_player_id).all();
    if (!scores.length) {
      standings.push({ trip_player_id: player.trip_player_id, name: player.name, handicap: player.current_index ?? player.handicap, tee_name: ttp?.tee_name || null, holes_played: 0, total_pts: 0, f9_pts: 0, b9_pts: 0, total_gross: 0, f9_gross: 0, b9_gross: 0, total_net: 0, f9_net: 0, b9_net: 0, hole_pts: [], hole_gross: [], hole_net: [] });
      // Still a contender on paper — carried into the skins engine so the
      // board lists him on 0 rather than dropping him off the night.
      skinsIn.push({ key: player.trip_player_id, name: player.name, playing_handicap: 0, si: null, hole_gross: [] });
      continue;
    }
    const holePars = ttp?.pars ? JSON.parse(ttp.pars) : (tripRound?.pars ? JSON.parse(tripRound.pars) : null);
    const holeSI   = ttp?.si   ? JSON.parse(ttp.si)   : (tripRound?.si   ? JSON.parse(tripRound.si)   : null);
    const _hres = leaguePlayingHcp(                                        // v2.39.0
      ttp?.playing_handicap,
      player.current_index ?? player.handicap,
      ttp?.slope ?? tripRound?.slope, ttp?.rating ?? tripRound?.rating,
      ttp?.tee_par ?? tripRound?.tee_par);
    const playingHcp = _hres.hcp;
    const hole_pts = new Array(18).fill(null);
    const hole_gross = new Array(18).fill(null);
    const hole_net = new Array(18).fill(null);                           // v2.58.0
    for (const s of scores) {
      const h = s.hole - 1;
      if (h < 0 || h > 17) continue;
      hole_gross[h] = s.gross_score;
      if (s.gross_score > 0) {
        hole_net[h] = s.gross_score - hcpStrokesForHole(holeSI ? holeSI[h] : h + 1, playingHcp);
      }
      if (holePars) {                                                     // v2.84.0 — B-62: recompute first; stored pts only when pars are missing
        const par = holePars[h] || 4;
        const si  = holeSI ? holeSI[h] : h + 1;
        const net = s.gross_score - hcpStrokesForHole(si, playingHcp);   // v2.37.0
        const diff = par - net;
        hole_pts[h] = Math.max(0, 2 + diff);                              // v2.43.0 standard ramp
      } else if (s.stableford_pts != null && _hres.trustStored) {          // v2.84.0 — last resort (no pars to recompute from)
        hole_pts[h] = s.stableford_pts;
      } else {
        hole_pts[h] = 0;
      }
    }
    const f9_pts = hole_pts.slice(0,9).reduce((a,v)=>a+(v??0),0);
    const b9_pts = hole_pts.slice(9,18).reduce((a,v)=>a+(v??0),0);
    const f9_gross = hole_gross.slice(0,9).reduce((a,v)=>a+(v??0),0);
    const b9_gross = hole_gross.slice(9,18).reduce((a,v)=>a+(v??0),0);
    const f9_net = hole_net.slice(0,9).reduce((a,v)=>a+(v??0),0);          // v2.65.0 — low net,
    const b9_net = hole_net.slice(9,18).reduce((a,v)=>a+(v??0),0);         // same shape as gross
    standings.push({
      trip_player_id: player.trip_player_id, name: player.name,
      handicap: player.current_index ?? player.handicap, tee_name: ttp?.tee_name || null,
      course_handicap: ttp?.course_handicap || null, playing_handicap: playingHcp,
      hcp_derived: _hres.derived,                                          // v2.39.0
      holes_played: scores.length,
      total_pts: f9_pts+b9_pts, f9_pts, b9_pts,
      total_gross: f9_gross+b9_gross, f9_gross, b9_gross,
      total_net: f9_net+b9_net, f9_net, b9_net,                            // v2.65.0
      hole_pts, hole_gross, hole_net,
    });
    skinsIn.push({ key: player.trip_player_id, name: player.name, playing_handicap: playingHcp, si: holeSI, hole_gross });
  }

  // v2.58.0 — skins night. The format on the round decides; everything else
  // above is untouched, so a stableford week returns exactly what it did.
  let skins = null;
  if (tripRound && tripRound.format === 'overall_skins') {
    const sk    = computeSkins(skinsIn, tripRound.si ? JSON.parse(tripRound.si) : null);
    const byKey = new Map(sk.players.map(p => [p.key, p]));
    const field = standings.filter(s => s.holes_played > 0).length;
    const money = skinsMoney(tripRound.skins_mode, tripRound.skins_amount, field, sk.total_skins);
    for (const s of standings) {
      const p = byKey.get(s.trip_player_id);
      s.skins       = p ? p.skins : 0;
      s.skins_holes = p ? p.holes_won : [];
      s.skins_cents = (money.per_skin_cents || 0) * s.skins;
    }
    skins = { holes: sk.holes, total_skins: sk.total_skins, dead_skins: sk.dead_skins, field, money };
  }

  // ── v2.69.0 — multi-game round. Side boards for every low-wins game on the
  // round, primary or not, so a round running "stableford + low net" posts
  // both. Nothing above is touched: an old client still reads the same
  // standings rows it always did.
  const _mgGames   = _gamesParse(tripRound && tripRound.games);
  const _mgList    = _gamesList(_mgGames, tripRound ? tripRound.format : null);
  const _mgPrimary = (tripRound && tripRound.format) || null;
  const low_boards = {};
  for (const g of _mgList) {
    if (!LOW_WINS_FORMATS.has(g)) continue;
    low_boards[g] = lowBoard(
      standings.map(s2 => ({ key: s2.trip_player_id, name: s2.name,
                             total_net: s2.total_net, total_gross: s2.total_gross,
                             holes_played: s2.holes_played })),
      g === 'low_net' ? 'total_net' : 'total_gross', 'holes_played');
  }

  if (skins) {
    standings.sort((a,b) => (b.skins||0)-(a.skins||0) || a.total_gross-b.total_gross || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    // Two men on the same number of skins TIED. Skins are small integers so
    // this is the common case, not the edge case — ranking them 2 and 3 would
    // be wrong on most nights. Stableford keeps its own plain ranking.
    applyTieAwareRanks(standings, (prev, cur) => (prev.skins||0) === (cur.skins||0));
  } else if (LOW_WINS_FORMATS.has(_mgPrimary)) {
    // ⭐ v2.69.0 — the round's PRIMARY game is a low-wins one, so the round
    // board IS the low board. Most holes played first, then fewest strokes:
    // a 9-hole 36 net is not leading an 18-hole 70. Ties are shared, exactly
    // as skins does — total strokes tie constantly at 9 and 18 holes.
    const _vk = _mgPrimary === 'low_net' ? 'total_net' : 'total_gross';
    standings.sort((a,b) => (b.holes_played-a.holes_played) || (a[_vk]-b[_vk]) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    applyTieAwareRanks(standings, (prev, cur) => prev.holes_played === cur.holes_played && prev[_vk] === cur[_vk]);
  } else {
    standings.sort((a,b) => b.total_pts-a.total_pts || a.total_gross-b.total_gross);
    standings.forEach((s,i) => { s.rank = i+1; });
  }
  return json({
    league_round: { id: leagueRound.id, round_date: leagueRound.round_date, trip_id: leagueRound.trip_id, trip_name: trip.name, viewer_pin: trip.viewer_pin, course_name: tripRound?.course_name || null, format: tripRound?.format || null,
                    games: _mgGames, games_list: _mgList,                 // v2.69.0
                    season_game: resolveSeasonGame(_mgGames, _mgPrimary) },
    standings,
    skins,
    low_boards,                                                           // v2.69.0
  }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// LEAGUE WATCH LIVE & RESULTS (v2.36.0)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Public 6-char league watch-code lookup. Mirrors handleTripWatchByCode.
// 404 by design when the code is not a league — the client Watch cascade
// (events -> trips -> leagues) falls through on 404 ONLY (v243.1 lesson).
async function handleLeagueWatchByCode(req, env, origin) {
  const code = (new URL(req.url).pathname.split('/').pop() || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!/^[A-Z0-9]{6}$/.test(code)) return err('Invalid code format', 400, origin);
  const league = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, watch_code FROM leagues WHERE UPPER(watch_code) = ?'
  ).bind(code).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);
  return json({ league_id: league.id, watch_code: league.watch_code }, 200, origin);
}

// Strokes received on one hole (v2.37.0 — fixes handicaps at/above 36).
// floor(hcp/18) on EVERY hole, plus one more on the (hcp % 18) hardest holes by
// stroke index. So 36 -> 2 every hole, 27 -> 1 every hole + a 2nd on SI 1-9,
// 18 -> 1 every hole. The old form capped the base at a single stroke, which made
// a 36 play identically to an 18 in the league handlers and capped the trip
// handler at 36. Plus handicaps still receive 0 (unchanged behaviour).
function hcpStrokesForHole(si, playingHcp) {
  const hcp = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(playingHcp) || 0));
  const idx = Number(si) >= 1 ? Number(si) : 18;
  return Math.floor(hcp / 18) + (idx <= (hcp % 18) ? 1 : 0);
}

// v2.39.0 — playing handicap for league scoring. Stored stableford_pts are
// trusted ONLY when the tee-time row carries a real playing_handicap; when it
// is NULL the phone that stored the pts computed them with 0 strokes, so we
// derive the handicap from the player's index + tee and recompute from gross.
function leaguePlayingHcp(ttpHcp, index, slope, rating, par) {
  if (ttpHcp != null) return { hcp: ttpHcp, trustStored: true, derived: false };
  if (index != null && slope != null && rating != null && par != null) {
    return { hcp: calcCourseHandicapFull(index, slope, rating, par), trustStored: false, derived: true };
  }
  return { hcp: 0, trustStored: false, derived: false };
}

// Stableford for one hole. Extracted verbatim from handleLeagueRoundLeaderboard
// so season totals can never drift from the per-week league leaderboard.
function leagueHolePts(grossScore, storedPts, par, si, playingHcp) {
  if (!grossScore || grossScore <= 0) return 0;                            // v2.43.0 — no score = 0
  if (storedPts != null) return storedPts;
  if (par == null) return 0;
  const net = grossScore - hcpStrokesForHole(si, playingHcp);           // v2.37.0
  const diff = par - net;
  return Math.max(0, 2 + diff);                                            // v2.43.0 standard ramp
}

// Standard competition ranking (1, 2, 2, 4) over an already-sorted array.
function applyTieAwareRanks(rows, sameFn) {
  rows.forEach((row, i) => {
    row.rank = (i > 0 && sameFn(rows[i - 1], row)) ? rows[i - 1].rank : i + 1;
  });
  return rows;
}

/* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   BARNES WEEK & YEAR LONG  (v2.67.0)
   Spec: claude/barnes-league-spec-v1.md — every BIND below is Bruce's.

   3 teams x 6, fixed for the year. 4 per team play matches weekly (12 golfers,
   3 foursomes). Up to 6 more play for money only. Max field 18.

   ALL THREE GAMES ARE NORMALISED TO 18 POINTS ON THE TABLE, 12 THE TEAM
   CEILING. That normalisation is the design: it is what makes the three games
   interchangeable week to week, so the Year Long race stays comparable across
   a rotation. Any future change to a point value must preserve 18/12.

     game 1 — six 1v1 net Nassau, low CH to zero      6 matches x 3 x 1 = 18
     game 2 — three 2v2 HIGH-LOW Nassau, low to zero  3 matches x 3 x 2 = 18
     game 3 — three 2v2 best ball net, low to zero    3 matches x 3 x 2 = 18

   PORTED, NOT REBUILT. Games 1 and 2 are the client's existing Nassau engine
   (holeResult, client 10033) verbatim — the 1v1 branch and the
   type==='2v2-combined' branch. Game 3 is the ONLY new match-engine surface.
   ALL THREE zero off the lowest CH in the match (v2.68.3 — best ball used to
   run at full handicap and was the exception; it is not any more). Presses are OFF
   (Barnes has none), so the client's chainPress loop is simply not ported.

   ── STROKE ALLOCATION: ONE IMPLEMENTATION, AND WHY IT IS SAFE ─────────────
   The client's matchNetS allocates `playHdcp>=si+18 ? 2 : playHdcp>=si ? 1 : 0`
   — capped at 2. The worker's existing hcpStrokesForHole allocates
   `floor(h/18) + (si <= h%18)` — uncapped. Checked exhaustively over
   si 1..18 x hcp 0..60: THEY ARE IDENTICAL FOR EVERY hcp 0..36 AND FIRST
   DIVERGE AT hcp 37 (si 1: client 2, worker 3).

   So Barnes uses hcpStrokesForHole — the allocator this worker already has,
   never a second one — and REFUSES above 36 rather than silently producing a
   number the client's board would disagree with. League 10's highest FULL
   course handicap at Derrick Deck 2 is 23, so the refusal is unreachable in
   practice; it exists so that if the league ever moves to a tee or a roster
   that reaches it, the board stops instead of drifting.

   ── PLAY ORDER ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   The client indexes by PLAY ORDER and translates through actualHoleIdx() to
   survive shotgun starts. trip_scores.hole is the ACTUAL hole number 1..18, so
   no translation is ported: Front is holes 1-9 and Back is 10-18, which is what
   Barnes' Front/Back segments mean regardless of where a group teed off.
   (The suspended "start hole is wrong" hunt is NOT touched by this.)
   ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

const BARNES_GAMES = {
  '1v1_nassau':   { type: '1v1',           label: 'Six 1v1 Nassau (net, low to zero)',        perSegment: 1, maxSwing: 1 },
  '2v2_highlow':  { type: '2v2_highlow',   label: 'Three 2v2 High-Low (net, low to zero)',    perSegment: 2, maxSwing: 2 },
  '2v2_bestball': { type: '2v2_bestball',  label: 'Three 2v2 Best Ball Net (net, low to zero)', perSegment: 2, maxSwing: 1 },
};

// The slot map. Bruce's pairing list IS the foursome layout, in pairs.
// Each team enters four players in slot order [0,1,2,3] and EVERYTHING derives
// from these three rows: the foursomes, the 2v2 pairs, and the 1v1 matches.
// Storing a match table alongside the lineup would be a second implementation
// of the same fact, which is exactly where drift starts (spec §2 BIND).
//   F1: (A1,A2) v (B1,B2)   ->  1v1: A1vB1, A2vB2
//   F2: (C1,C2) v (A3,A4)   ->  1v1: C1vA3, C2vA4
//   F3: (B3,B4) v (C3,C4)   ->  1v1: B3vC3, B4vC4
// Every team appears in exactly two foursomes; every slot is used exactly once.
const BARNES_FOURSOMES = [
  { id: 1, sideA: [['A', 0], ['A', 1]], sideB: [['B', 0], ['B', 1]] },
  { id: 2, sideA: [['C', 0], ['C', 1]], sideB: [['A', 2], ['A', 3]] },
  { id: 3, sideA: [['B', 2], ['B', 3]], sideB: [['C', 2], ['C', 3]] },
];

const BARNES_MAX_HCP = 36;   // above this the two allocators disagree — refuse

// Net for one player on one hole. Mirrors the client's matchNetS, using this
// worker's single stroke allocator (see the divergence note above).
function barnesNet(p, hole, playHdcp, siArr) {
  const g = p.scores[hole];
  if (g == null || g <= 0) return null;
  return g - hcpStrokesForHole(siArr[hole - 1], playHdcp);
}

// Zero off the LOWEST course handicap in the match — the client's
// matchPlayHdcps, which is what "low handicap drops to zero" means.
function barnesPlayHdcps(players) {
  const min = Math.min(...players.map(p => p.ch));
  return players.map(p => p.ch - min);
}

// The client's matchSideNet: low ball or high ball for one side on one hole.
function barnesSideNet(side, phs, hole, ball, siArr) {
  const nets = side.map((p, i) => barnesNet(p, hole, phs[i], siArr)).filter(n => n !== null);
  if (!nets.length) return null;
  return ball === 'low' ? Math.min(...nets) : Math.max(...nets);
}

// The client's holeResult, three branches. +1 = side A wins the hole.
// High-Low returns -2..+2 because it settles TWO ball-matches on the one hole.
function barnesHoleResult(m, hole, siArr) {
  const all = [...m.sideA, ...m.sideB];
  if (all.some(p => p.scores[hole] == null || p.scores[hole] <= 0)) return null;

  // v2.68.3 (Bruce — BIND): ALL THREE games zero off the lowest course
  // handicap among the FOUR players in the match. Best Ball Net used to run at
  // full handicap and was the exception; it is not any more.
  //
  // Computed ONCE, above every branch, deliberately. When this lived below the
  // best-ball early return, "which handicap does this game use" was a property
  // of each branch — and that is exactly how best ball ended up disagreeing
  // with the other two. One subtraction, applied before anything reads a net,
  // means a new game cannot get it wrong by omission.
  //
  // ALL FOUR, not per pair: the two sides must share one subtraction or their
  // nets are not comparable.
  const allPh = barnesPlayHdcps(all);
  const phA = allPh.slice(0, m.sideA.length);
  const phB = allPh.slice(m.sideA.length);

  if (m.type === '2v2_bestball') {
    // GAME 3 — each side's hole score is the LOWER of the two partners' nets.
    const a = barnesSideNet(m.sideA, phA, hole, 'low', siArr);
    const b = barnesSideNet(m.sideB, phB, hole, 'low', siArr);
    return a < b ? 1 : b < a ? -1 : 0;
  }

  if (m.type === '2v2_highlow') {
    // The client's type==='2v2-combined' branch, verbatim. NOT aggregate net —
    // with nets A=4,7 against B=5,5 aggregate gives B the hole (10 v 11) while
    // High-Low halves it (A takes the low ball, B takes the high ball).
    const aHi = barnesSideNet(m.sideA, phA, hole, 'high', siArr);
    const bHi = barnesSideNet(m.sideB, phB, hole, 'high', siArr);
    const aLo = barnesSideNet(m.sideA, phA, hole, 'low',  siArr);
    const bLo = barnesSideNet(m.sideB, phB, hole, 'low',  siArr);
