    return (aHi < bHi ? 1 : bHi < aHi ? -1 : 0) + (aLo < bLo ? 1 : bLo < aLo ? -1 : 0);
  }

  const a = barnesNet(m.sideA[0], hole, phA[0], siArr);
  const b = barnesNet(m.sideB[0], hole, phB[0], siArr);
  if (a === null || b === null) return null;
  return a < b ? 1 : b < a ? -1 : 0;
}

// The client's scoreRange, with the press chain omitted (Barnes has no presses)
// and the early-settle test corrected for games that swing more than 1 a hole.
//
// The client's test is `Math.abs(score) > holesLeft`, which assumes each
// remaining hole swings at most 1. HIGH-LOW SWINGS UP TO 2, so on a live card
// that test can call a segment settled while it is still mathematically
// reachable. It never affects an AWARDED point — Barnes points settle only on
// completed 9s and 18s, where holesLeft === 0 — but it would misreport a board
// still in play, which is exactly what the weekly ticker will show.
function barnesScoreRange(m, startH, endH, siArr, maxSwing) {
  let score = 0, holesPlayed = 0;
  for (let h = startH; h <= endH; h++) {
    const r = barnesHoleResult(m, h, siArr);
    if (r === null) break;
    score += r;
    holesPlayed++;
  }
  const holesLeft = (endH - startH + 1) - holesPlayed;
  const settled = holesLeft === 0 || Math.abs(score) > holesLeft * maxSwing;
  return { score, holesPlayed, holesLeft, settled, complete: holesLeft === 0,
           winner: settled ? (score > 0 ? 'A' : score < 0 ? 'B' : 'tie') : null };
}

// THE $20 TEAM POT IS DECIDED BY STABLEFORD, NOT BY MATCH POINTS.
//
// BIND (Bruce, session 12, twice-corrected — read this carefully, the two wrong
// versions are both plausible):
//
//   NOT "most weekly match points" (session 11's version), and
//   NOT "add each team's best 3 PLAYERS' 18-hole totals" (the first correction).
//
//   IT IS PER HOLE: on every hole, take the BEST 3 of the team's 4 match players'
//   full-handicap net stableford points for that hole, and add them. Do that for
//   all 18 holes. That sum is the team's total. Highest takes the pot; ties share.
//
// The distinction is not cosmetic: the dropped card changes hole by hole, so a
// player who blows up twice and is otherwise solid still contributes almost
// everything, while under best-3-players he would be dropped for the whole round.
// The two rules routinely pick different winners — harness 13c exhibits a fixture
// where they disagree.
//
// A hole a player has no score for contributes NOTHING and is not a zero: it is
// simply not among the four available. If fewer than three of the four have a
// score on a hole, the team gets what is there — the same short-field
// disadvantage the forfeit rule already prices.
//
// Only the four in the matches count toward a team's pot total; the up-to-six
// extras play for the two $5 pots only.
//
// Lifted out of the handler so the harness can EXECUTE it instead of asserting on
// a regex: the rule that decides real money should not be proved at source level.
function barnesTeamPotTotals(lineup, potRows) {
  const byRid = {};
  for (const r of potRows) byRid[r.roster_id] = r;
  const totals = {}, detail = {};
  for (const t of ['A', 'B', 'C']) {
    const four = ((lineup && lineup[t]) || []).map(rid => byRid[rid]).filter(Boolean);
    const contrib = {};                       // roster_id -> {pts, holes}
    for (const p of four) contrib[p.roster_id] = { roster_id: p.roster_id, name: p.name, pts: 0, holes: 0 };
    const holes = [];
    let total = 0;
    for (let h = 0; h < 18; h++) {
      const avail = four
        .map(p => ({ rid: p.roster_id, pts: (p.hole_pts && p.hole_pts[h] != null) ? p.hole_pts[h] : null }))
        .filter(x => x.pts !== null)
        .sort((a, b) => b.pts - a.pts);
      const counted = avail.slice(0, 3);
      const sum = counted.reduce((a, x) => a + x.pts, 0);
      total += sum;
      for (const c of counted) { contrib[c.rid].pts += c.pts; contrib[c.rid].holes += 1; }
      holes.push({ hole: h + 1, pts: sum, counted: counted.length,
                   dropped: avail.slice(3).map(x => x.rid) });
    }
    totals[t] = total;
    detail[t] = { total, holes, contributions: Object.values(contrib).sort((a, b) => b.pts - a.pts) };
  }
  return { totals, detail };
}

// One match -> its three Nassau segments and the points they award.
// "Overall" is the 18-hole match IN ITS OWN RIGHT (addGame(0,17) in the client,
// confirmed by Bruce): a cumulative match over all 18 holes, not "whoever won
// more of the two nines".
//
// CORRECTION, recorded rather than left as folklore: because every segment is a
// cumulative sum of hole results, the overall margin is ARITHMETICALLY the front
// margin plus the back margin, always. So a side that loses BOTH nines can never
// take the overall — an earlier note here claimed it could, and harness check 5a
// disproves it over 120 generated cards. What Overall genuinely does is diverge
// from "won more nines": lose the front by 1, win the back by 3, and you take the
// overall while the nines are split 1-1. That is what makes it a third point
// rather than a restatement of the first two (harness 5b).
//
// Points are awarded only on a COMPLETE segment; a halved segment splits
// 0.5 / 0.5 so all 18 points stay on the table every week.
function barnesMatchSegments(m, siArr, perSegment, maxSwing) {
  const defs = [['front', 1, 9], ['back', 10, 18], ['overall', 1, 18]];
  const segments = [];
  let ptsA = 0, ptsB = 0;
  for (const [name, s, e] of defs) {
    const r = barnesScoreRange(m, s, e, siArr, maxSwing);
    let awardA = 0, awardB = 0;
    if (r.complete) {
      if (r.score > 0)      awardA = perSegment;
      else if (r.score < 0) awardB = perSegment;
      else { awardA = perSegment / 2; awardB = perSegment / 2; }   // halved — BIND
    }
    ptsA += awardA; ptsB += awardB;
    segments.push({ segment: name, ...r, value: perSegment, points_a: awardA, points_b: awardB });
  }
  return { segments, points_a: ptsA, points_b: ptsB };
}

// GET /api/leagues/:id/barnes?week=N  (or &round_id=)
// Auth mirrors handleEventGames: session first, then organizer_id on the league.
async function handleLeagueBarnes(req, env, origin, leagueId) {
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);

  const league = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT l.*, c.name AS home_course_name FROM leagues l
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = l.home_course_id
     WHERE l.id = ? AND l.organizer_id = ?`
  ).bind(leagueId, account.id).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);

  let lcfg = {};
  try { lcfg = typeof league.config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(league.config || '{}') : (league.config || {}); } catch (_) { lcfg = {}; }
  const barnes = lcfg.barnes || {};

  // OFF by default, exactly like Quota: a league that has not enabled Barnes
  // gets a clean "off", never an empty board that reads as "nobody has scored".
  if (!barnes.on) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: false, available: false, reason: 'league_off', weeks: [], standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }

  const teams = Array.isArray(barnes.teams) ? barnes.teams : [];
  if (teams.length !== 3 || teams.some(t => !Array.isArray(t.roster_ids) || t.roster_ids.length !== 6)) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: 'teams_incomplete',
                  message: 'Barnes needs exactly 3 teams of 6. Set them in league setup.', standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }

  // Per-hole pars and SI. REFUSE rather than fall back to par 4s — a plausible
  // wrong board is worse than no board (the eventHolePars lesson).
  const pars = Array.isArray(lcfg.game_pars) ? lcfg.game_pars : null;
  const si   = Array.isArray(lcfg.game_si)   ? lcfg.game_si   : null;
  if (!pars || pars.length !== 18 || !si || si.length !== 18) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: 'per_hole_pars_missing',
                  message: 'This league has no per-hole pars and stroke index yet. Set the league course and tee — every Barnes net calculation needs them.',
                  standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }

  // The week.
  const { results: weekRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT lr.id AS league_round_id, lr.round_date, lr.trip_id, lr.config AS round_config,
            t.name AS trip_name, t.trip_code, t.status
     FROM league_rounds lr JOIN trips t ON t.id = lr.trip_id
     WHERE lr.league_id = ? ORDER BY lr.round_date ASC, lr.created_at ASC`
  ).bind(leagueId).all();

  if (!weekRows.length) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: 'no_rounds',
                  message: 'This league has no game days yet.', weeks_total: 0, standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }

  const roundIdParam = Number(url.searchParams.get('round_id') || 0);
  const weekParam    = Number(url.searchParams.get('week') || 0);
  let wi = -1;
  if (roundIdParam) wi = weekRows.findIndex(w => w.league_round_id === roundIdParam);
  else if (weekParam) wi = weekParam - 1;
  else wi = weekRows.length - 1;                      // default: the latest week
  if (wi < 0 || wi >= weekRows.length) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: 'no_such_week',
                  message: `Week ${weekParam || roundIdParam} does not exist. This league has ${weekRows.length}.`,
                  weeks_total: weekRows.length, standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }
  const wk = weekRows[wi];

  let rcfg = {};
  try { rcfg = typeof wk.round_config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(wk.round_config || '{}') : (wk.round_config || {}); } catch (_) { rcfg = {}; }
  const bw = rcfg.barnes_week || {};

  // BIND — a week with no game set FAILS LOUDLY. Never default to game 1:
  // null is a real answer, and a plausible wrong board is worse than no board.
  const gameKey = bw.game;
  if (!gameKey || !BARNES_GAMES[gameKey]) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: 'no_game_set',
                  message: 'No game has been chosen for this week. The organizer picks one of the three each week — there is no default and no cycle.',
                  week: wi + 1, weeks_total: weekRows.length,
                  choices: Object.keys(BARNES_GAMES).map(k => ({ key: k, label: BARNES_GAMES[k].label })),
                  standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }
  const G = BARNES_GAMES[gameKey];

  const lineup = bw.lineup || {};
  if (!['A', 'B', 'C'].every(t => Array.isArray(lineup[t]))) {
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: 'no_lineup',
                  message: 'No lineup set for this week. Enter four players per team in slot order.',
                  week: wi + 1, weeks_total: weekRows.length, standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }

  // ── Everything below is FLAT: a fixed number of queries no matter how many
  // weeks the season has, per the v2.35.0 lesson that handleLeagueSeason learned
  // the hard way. Never N-weeks x M-players.
  const tripIds = weekRows.map(w => w.trip_id);
  const tph = tripIds.map(() => '?').join(',');

  const { results: trRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.id, tr.trip_id, tr.round_number, tr.date, tr.status,
            ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_rounds tr LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id IN (${tph}) ORDER BY tr.trip_id, tr.round_number`
  ).bind(...tripIds).all();
  const trByTrip = {};
  for (const r of trRows) if (!trByTrip[r.trip_id]) trByTrip[r.trip_id] = r;

  const { results: playerRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tp.id AS trip_player_id, tp.trip_id, tp.current_index,
            r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap
     FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id WHERE tp.trip_id IN (${tph})`
  ).bind(...tripIds).all();

  const roundIds = trRows.map(r => r.id);
  let scoreRows = [];
  if (roundIds.length) {
    const rph = roundIds.map(() => '?').join(',');
    const q = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT trip_round_id, trip_player_id, hole, gross_score FROM trip_scores
       WHERE trip_round_id IN (${rph})`
    ).bind(...roundIds).all();
    scoreRows = q.results || [];
  }

  const cPar = pars.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

  // Score ONE week from the already-fetched rows. Returns either a refusal
  // reason or a full result — the same shape whether it is the week being
  // displayed or a week being folded into the Year Long total.
  function scoreOneWeek(w) {
    let c = {};
    try { c = typeof w.round_config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(w.round_config || '{}') : (w.round_config || {}); } catch (_) { c = {}; }
    const bwk = c.barnes_week || {};
    const gk = bwk.game;
    if (!gk || !BARNES_GAMES[gk]) return { ok: false, reason: 'no_game_set' };
    const lu = bwk.lineup || {};
    if (!['A', 'B', 'C'].every(t => Array.isArray(lu[t]))) return { ok: false, reason: 'no_lineup' };
    const trw = trByTrip[w.trip_id];
    if (!trw) return { ok: false, reason: 'no_round' };

    const rating = trw.rating != null ? trw.rating : (lcfg.course && lcfg.course.rating);
    const slope  = trw.slope  != null ? trw.slope  : (lcfg.course && lcfg.course.slope);
    if (rating == null || slope == null) return { ok: false, reason: 'no_tee_rating' };

    const byRoster = {}, byTripPlayer = {};
    for (const p of playerRows) {
      if (p.trip_id !== w.trip_id) continue;
      const idx = p.current_index != null ? p.current_index : p.handicap;
      const rec = { roster_id: p.roster_id, trip_player_id: p.trip_player_id, name: p.name,
                    index: idx, ch: idx == null ? null : calcCourseHandicapFull(idx, slope, rating, cPar),
                    scores: {} };
      byRoster[p.roster_id] = rec; byTripPlayer[p.trip_player_id] = rec;
    }
    for (const s of scoreRows) {
      if (s.trip_round_id !== trw.id) continue;
      const p = byTripPlayer[s.trip_player_id];
      if (p && s.gross_score > 0) p.scores[s.hole] = s.gross_score;
    }

    // Refuse above the cap, where this worker's allocator and the client's
    // matchNetS diverge. Never quietly disagree with the in-round match board.
    const over = Object.values(byRoster).filter(p => p.ch != null && p.ch > BARNES_MAX_HCP).map(p => p.name);
    if (over.length) return { ok: false, reason: 'handicap_over_cap', over };

    const G = BARNES_GAMES[gk];
    const slot = (t, i) => { const rid = lu[t][i]; return rid == null ? null : (byRoster[rid] || null); };
    const matches = [], missing = [];
    for (const f of BARNES_FOURSOMES) {
      const A = f.sideA.map(([t, i]) => slot(t, i));
      const B = f.sideB.map(([t, i]) => slot(t, i));
      const ta = f.sideA[0][0], tb = f.sideB[0][0];
      A.forEach((p, i) => { if (!p) missing.push(`${f.sideA[i][0]}${f.sideA[i][1] + 1}`); });
      B.forEach((p, i) => { if (!p) missing.push(`${f.sideB[i][0]}${f.sideB[i][1] + 1}`); });
      if (G.type === '1v1') {
        // Slot i against slot i — the foursome's two singles matches, which is
        // why the pairing list IS the tee sheet.
        for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) matches.push({ foursome: f.id, type: '1v1', team_a: ta, team_b: tb,
                                                   sideA: A[i] ? [A[i]] : [], sideB: B[i] ? [B[i]] : [] });
      } else {
        matches.push({ foursome: f.id, type: G.type, team_a: ta, team_b: tb,
                       sideA: A.filter(Boolean), sideB: B.filter(Boolean) });
      }
    }

    // A side that cannot be fielded FORFEITS the whole match. The week is still
    // worth 18 either way, so the season race stays comparable.
    //
    // STATED CONSEQUENCE, not a silent one: one absent player costs 3 in game 1
    // and 6 in games 2/3, because in a 2v2 the absence breaks a whole pair. Same
    // absence, double the cost, purely from which game is up that week. Known
    // and priced (spec §6), and reported as `forfeit_cost` so the board can say
    // so out loud rather than let it surface as a surprise in September.
    const wantPerSide = G.type === '1v1' ? 1 : 2;
    const teamPoints = { A: 0, B: 0, C: 0 };
    const outMatches = [];
    for (const m of matches) {
      const shortA = m.sideA.length < wantPerSide, shortB = m.sideB.length < wantPerSide;
      const total = G.perSegment * 3;
      const side = s => s.map(p => ({ roster_id: p.roster_id, name: p.name, ch: p.ch }));
      if (shortA || shortB) {
        let pa = 0, pb = 0;
        if (shortA && !shortB) pb = total; else if (shortB && !shortA) pa = total;
        teamPoints[m.team_a] += pa; teamPoints[m.team_b] += pb;
        outMatches.push({ foursome: m.foursome, type: m.type, team_a: m.team_a, team_b: m.team_b,
                          side_a: side(m.sideA), side_b: side(m.sideB),
                          forfeit: shortA && shortB ? 'both' : shortA ? 'A' : 'B',
                          segments: [], points_a: pa, points_b: pb });
        continue;
      }
      const r = barnesMatchSegments(m, si, G.perSegment, G.maxSwing);
      teamPoints[m.team_a] += r.points_a; teamPoints[m.team_b] += r.points_b;
      outMatches.push({ foursome: m.foursome, type: m.type, team_a: m.team_a, team_b: m.team_b,
                        side_a: side(m.sideA), side_b: side(m.sideB), forfeit: null,
                        segments: r.segments, points_a: r.points_a, points_b: r.points_b });
    }

    // Money is per HEAD — self-balancing at any headcount and explainable
    // standing on the first tee. Everyone who teed it up is in, match player or
    // not, forfeiting or not: the money is independent of the matches.
    const stake = barnes.stake || { per_player: 30, team: 20, front: 5, back: 5 };
    const teedUp = Object.values(byRoster).filter(p => Object.keys(p.scores).length > 0);
    const heads = teedUp.length;

    // Full-handicap NET stableford per nine, RE-DERIVED FROM GROSS. The stored
    // trip_scores.stableford_pts is never read: the Quota lesson was that a
    // stored points column quietly held a different game's number, and these
    // two $5 pots are decided by it.
    const potRows = teedUp.map(p => {
      let front = 0, back = 0;
      // null on a hole the player has no score for — DISTINCT from a genuine 0.
      // The team pot takes the best 3 of 4 PER HOLE, so "didn't play" and
      // "played it and scored nothing" must not look the same to it.
      const holePts = new Array(18).fill(null);
      for (let h = 1; h <= 18; h++) {
        const g = p.scores[h];
        if (!g || g <= 0) continue;
        const pts = leagueHolePts(g, null, pars[h - 1], si[h - 1], p.ch);
        holePts[h - 1] = pts;
        if (h <= 9) front += pts; else back += pts;
      }
      return { roster_id: p.roster_id, name: p.name, ch: p.ch, front_pts: front, back_pts: back,
               total_pts: front + back, hole_pts: holePts,
               holes_played: Object.keys(p.scores).length };
    });

    // Ties SHARE, both team and individual — one behaviour across the app.
    const shareWinners = (rows, key) => {
      const scored = rows.filter(r => r.holes_played > 0);
      if (!scored.length) return { winners: [], best: null };
      const best = Math.max(...scored.map(r => r[key]));
      return { winners: scored.filter(r => r[key] === best).map(r => ({ roster_id: r.roster_id, name: r.name, pts: r[key] })), best };
    };
    const frontPot = shareWinners(potRows, 'front_pts');
    const backPot  = shareWinners(potRows, 'back_pts');

    const { totals: teamStable, detail: teamStableDetail } = barnesTeamPotTotals(lu, potRows);
    const bestTeam = Math.max(teamStable.A, teamStable.B, teamStable.C);
    const teamWinners = ['A', 'B', 'C'].filter(t => teamStable[t] === bestTeam);

    return {
      ok: true, gameKey: gk, game: G, lineup: lu, missing, teamPoints, matches: outMatches,
      awarded: teamPoints.A + teamPoints.B + teamPoints.C,
      stableford: potRows.slice().sort((a, b) => b.total_pts - a.total_pts),
      pot: {
        heads, per_player: stake.per_player,
        team:  { amount: heads * stake.team,  winners: teamWinners, points: bestTeam,
                 basis: 'per_hole_best3_of_4_stableford_of_match_players', by_team: teamStableDetail,
                 each: teamWinners.length ? (heads * stake.team) / teamWinners.length : 0 },
        front: { amount: heads * stake.front, winners: frontPot.winners, points: frontPot.best,
                 each: frontPot.winners.length ? (heads * stake.front) / frontPot.winners.length : 0 },
        back:  { amount: heads * stake.back,  winners: backPot.winners,  points: backPot.best,
                 each: backPot.winners.length  ? (heads * stake.back)  / backPot.winners.length  : 0 },
        total: heads * stake.per_player,
      },
    };
  }

  // ── YEAR LONG ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // carry_in is the pre-Barnes history: weeks 1-13 of league 10 total 178, not
  // the 234 that 18-a-week implies, because they were played under the old
  // format. It is therefore OPAQUE — added as a starting number and nothing
  // more. NEVER average across the boundary, NEVER derive a season pace or a
  // points-remaining figure that mixes normalised and pre-normalised weeks; the
  // two are not the same unit. `normalised_from_week` marks where 18/12 starts.
  const carry = barnes.carry_in || null;
  const yearLong = { A: 0, B: 0, C: 0 };
  if (carry && carry.points) for (const t of ['A', 'B', 'C']) yearLong[t] += Number(carry.points[t] || 0);
  const weekSummaries = [], skipped = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < weekRows.length; i++) {
    const r = scoreOneWeek(weekRows[i]);
    if (!r.ok) { skipped.push({ week: i + 1, reason: r.reason }); continue; }
    for (const t of ['A', 'B', 'C']) yearLong[t] += r.teamPoints[t];
    weekSummaries.push({ week: i + 1, round_date: weekRows[i].round_date, game: r.gameKey,
                         team_points: r.teamPoints, awarded: r.awarded, complete: r.awarded === 18 });
  }

  // The requested week, in full.
  const detail = scoreOneWeek(wk);
  if (!detail.ok) {
    const msgs = {
      no_game_set:  'No game has been chosen for this week. The organizer picks one of the three each week — there is no default and no cycle.',
      no_lineup:    'No lineup set for this week. Enter four players per team in slot order.',
      no_round:     'This game day has no round behind it yet.',
      no_tee_rating:'No rating/slope for this round\'s tee, so course handicaps cannot be computed.',
      handicap_over_cap: `Course handicap above ${BARNES_MAX_HCP} for: ${(detail.over || []).join(', ')}. Above that this board and the in-round match board allocate strokes differently, so it refuses rather than show a number the scorer would disagree with.`,
    };
    return json({ game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: false, reason: detail.reason,
                  message: msgs[detail.reason] || detail.reason,
                  choices: detail.reason === 'no_game_set'
                    ? Object.keys(BARNES_GAMES).map(k => ({ key: k, label: BARNES_GAMES[k].label })) : undefined,
                  week: wi + 1, weeks_total: weekRows.length,
                  year_long: { carry_in: carry, points: yearLong, weeks: weekSummaries, skipped,
                               normalised_from_week: (carry && carry.normalised_from_week) || 1 },
                  standings: [] }, 200, origin);
  }

  // Starts per player across the whole season. ADVISORY ONLY — the app tracks
  // starts so Bruce can see who is owed a game. It never assigns, never blocks.
  const starts = {};
  for (const w of weekRows) {
    let c = {};
    try { c = typeof w.round_config === 'string' ? JSON.parse(w.round_config || '{}') : (w.round_config || {}); } catch (_) { c = {}; }
    const lu = (c.barnes_week && c.barnes_week.lineup) || {};
    for (const t of ['A', 'B', 'C']) for (const rid of (lu[t] || [])) if (rid != null) starts[rid] = (starts[rid] || 0) + 1;
  }

  const teamMeta = {};
  for (const t of teams) teamMeta[t.id] = { id: t.id, name: t.name, roster_ids: t.roster_ids };

  return json({
    game: 'barnes', enabled: true, available: true,
    league: { id: league.id, name: league.name, home_course_name: league.home_course_name,
              tee: (lcfg.course && lcfg.course.tee) || null,
              teams_provisional: !!barnes.teams_provisional },
    week: wi + 1, weeks_total: weekRows.length,
    round: { league_round_id: wk.league_round_id, round_date: wk.round_date, trip_id: wk.trip_id,
             trip_name: wk.trip_name, trip_code: wk.trip_code, status: wk.status },
    week_game: { key: detail.gameKey, label: detail.game.label, points_per_segment: detail.game.perSegment },
    // The normalisation, asserted on every response rather than assumed.
    points: { on_the_table: 18, team_ceiling: 12, awarded: detail.awarded,
              complete: detail.awarded === 18, forfeit_cost: detail.game.perSegment * 3 },
    teams: teamMeta, lineup: detail.lineup, missing_slots: detail.missing,
    team_points: detail.teamPoints,
    matches: detail.matches,
    pot: detail.pot,
    stableford: detail.stableford,
    year_long: { carry_in: carry, points: yearLong, weeks: weekSummaries, skipped,
                 normalised_from_week: (carry && carry.normalised_from_week) || 1 },
    starts,
  }, 200, origin);
}

// GET /api/leagues/:id/season?code=XXXXXX
// Whole-season payload: meta, week-by-week player results (hole detail included),
// weekly winners, and season standings.
//
// Flattened from day one (v2.35.0 lesson): SIX queries total regardless of how many
// weeks or players the league has — never N-weeks x M-players.
//
// NOTE: trip_players rows are per-trip, so the SAME human has a different
// trip_player_id every week. Season aggregation keys on roster_id.
async function handleLeagueSeason(req, env, origin, leagueId) {
  const url  = new URL(req.url);
  const code = (url.searchParams.get('code') || '').trim().toUpperCase();

  // Q1 — league.
  const league = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT l.*, c.name AS home_course_name FROM leagues l
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = l.home_course_id WHERE l.id = ?`
  ).bind(leagueId).first();
  if (!league) return err('League not found', 404, origin);

  // Auth: watch code, or the organizer's own session.
  const codeOk = code !== '' && String(league.watch_code || '').toUpperCase() === code;
  if (!codeOk) {
    const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
    if (!account || account.id !== league.organizer_id) return err('Invalid code', 401, origin);
  }

  // Q2 — weeks, oldest first (week 1 = first played).
  const { results: weekRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT lr.id AS league_round_id, lr.round_date, lr.trip_id,
            t.name AS trip_name, t.trip_code, t.status
     FROM league_rounds lr JOIN trips t ON t.id = lr.trip_id
     WHERE lr.league_id = ? ORDER BY lr.round_date ASC, lr.created_at ASC`
  ).bind(leagueId).all();

  if (!weekRows.length) {
    return json({
      league: { id: league.id, name: league.name, description: league.description,
                watch_code: league.watch_code, home_course_name: league.home_course_name,
                season_start: null, season_end: null, weeks_total: 0, weeks_scored: 0 },
      weeks: [], standings: [], extras: { best_round: null, most_wins: [] },
    }, 200, origin);
  }

  const tripIds = weekRows.map(w => w.trip_id);
  const tph = tripIds.map(() => '?').join(',');

  // Q3 — the trip_round behind each week (league rounds create exactly one, #1).
  const { results: trRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tr.id, tr.trip_id, tr.round_number, tr.date, tr.status, tr.format,
            tr.skins_mode, tr.skins_amount, tr.games,
            c.name AS course_name, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si,
            ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
     FROM trip_rounds tr
     LEFT JOIN courses c ON c.id = tr.course_id
     LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = tr.tee_box_id
     WHERE tr.trip_id IN (${tph}) ORDER BY tr.trip_id, tr.round_number`
  ).bind(...tripIds).all();
  const trByTrip = {};
  for (const tr of trRows) if (!trByTrip[tr.trip_id]) trByTrip[tr.trip_id] = tr;

  // Q4 — every player of every week, with their roster identity.
  const { results: playerRows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    `SELECT tp.id AS trip_player_id, tp.trip_id, tp.current_index,
            r.id AS roster_id, r.name, r.handicap
     FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id
     WHERE tp.trip_id IN (${tph}) ORDER BY r.name`
  ).bind(...tripIds).all();
  const playersByTrip = {};
  for (const pl of playerRows) (playersByTrip[pl.trip_id] = playersByTrip[pl.trip_id] || []).push(pl);

  const roundIds = trRows.map(r => r.id);
  const ttpByKey = {}, scoresByKey = {};
  if (roundIds.length) {
    const rph = roundIds.map(() => '?').join(',');
    // Q5 — tee assignment (playing handicap + the tee's pars/SI) per player per week.
    const { results: ttps } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT tt.trip_round_id, ttp.trip_player_id, ttp.playing_handicap, ttp.course_handicap,
              ct.pars, ct.handicaps AS si, ct.name AS tee_name, ct.rating, ct.slope, ct.par AS tee_par
       FROM trip_tee_time_players ttp
       JOIN trip_tee_times tt ON tt.id = ttp.trip_tee_time_id
       LEFT JOIN course_tees ct ON ct.id = ttp.tee_box_id
       WHERE tt.trip_round_id IN (${rph})`
    ).bind(...roundIds).all();
    for (const t of ttps) ttpByKey[t.trip_round_id + ':' + t.trip_player_id] = t;
    // Q6 — every score of the whole season.
    const { results: allScores } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT trip_round_id, trip_player_id, hole, gross_score, stableford_pts
       FROM trip_scores WHERE trip_round_id IN (${rph}) ORDER BY hole`
    ).bind(...roundIds).all();
    for (const sc of allScores) {
      const k = sc.trip_round_id + ':' + sc.trip_player_id;
      (scoresByKey[k] = scoresByKey[k] || []).push(sc);
    }
  }

  const season = {}; // roster_id -> accumulator
  const weeks  = [];

  weekRows.forEach((w, idx) => {
    const tr = trByTrip[w.trip_id] || null;
    const roundPars = tr && tr.pars ? JSON.parse(tr.pars) : null;
    const roundSI   = tr && tr.si   ? JSON.parse(tr.si)   : null;
    const entries = [];

    for (const pl of (playersByTrip[w.trip_id] || [])) {
      const key    = (tr ? tr.id : 0) + ':' + pl.trip_player_id;
      const ttp    = ttpByKey[key] || null;
      const scores = scoresByKey[key] || [];
      const pars   = ttp && ttp.pars ? JSON.parse(ttp.pars) : roundPars;
      const si     = ttp && ttp.si   ? JSON.parse(ttp.si)   : roundSI;
      const _hres = leaguePlayingHcp(                                      // v2.39.0
        ttp?.playing_handicap,
        pl.current_index ?? pl.handicap,
        ttp?.slope ?? (tr ? tr.slope : null), ttp?.rating ?? (tr ? tr.rating : null),
        ttp?.tee_par ?? (tr ? tr.tee_par : null));
      const playingHcp = _hres.hcp;

      const entry = {
        trip_player_id: pl.trip_player_id, roster_id: pl.roster_id, name: pl.name,
        handicap: pl.current_index ?? pl.handicap,
        playing_handicap: playingHcp, hcp_derived: _hres.derived, tee_name: ttp?.tee_name || (tr ? tr.tee_name : null),
        holes: scores.length, gross: 0, net: 0, pts: 0,               // v2.65.0 — net beside gross
        hole_scores: {}, hole_pts: new Array(18).fill(null), pars, si,
      };
      for (const sc of scores) {
        const h = sc.hole - 1;
        if (h < 0 || h > 17) continue;
        const par = pars ? pars[h] : null;
        const shi = si   ? si[h]   : h + 1;
        const pts = leagueHolePts(sc.gross_score, (par == null && _hres.trustStored) ? sc.stableford_pts : null, par, shi, playingHcp);  // v2.84.0 — B-62: recompute; stored only when pars missing
        entry.hole_scores[sc.hole] = sc.gross_score;
        entry.hole_pts[h] = pts;
        entry.gross += sc.gross_score;
        if (sc.gross_score > 0) entry.net += sc.gross_score - hcpStrokesForHole(shi, playingHcp); // v2.65.0
        entry.pts   += pts;
      }
      entries.push(entry);
    }

    // v2.58.0 — a skins week scores in skins. Same engine as the weekly
    // leaderboard, so the two can never disagree. hole_pts carries skins won
    // per hole, which keeps the sum(hole_pts) === pts invariant the scorecard
    // relies on. Guests are eligible and accrue exactly as they already do for
    // stableford — this changes the unit, not who counts.
    // ── v2.69.0 — which game feeds the season points column this week.
    // Bruce, s18: the ORGANIZER picks, per round. The pick is constrained to
    // HIGH_WINS at write time (_gamesValidate), which is where §1 BIND lives.
    // With games NULL this resolves to exactly today's behaviour.
    const _mgGames   = _gamesParse(tr && tr.games);
    const _mgList    = _gamesList(_mgGames, tr ? tr.format : null);
    const _seasonGame = resolveSeasonGame(_mgGames, tr ? tr.format : null);

    let weekSkins = null;
    if (tr && _mgList.indexOf('overall_skins') !== -1) {
      const sk = computeSkins(entries.map(e => ({
        key: e.trip_player_id, name: e.name, playing_handicap: e.playing_handicap, si: e.si,
        hole_gross: Array.from({ length: 18 }, (_, h) => e.hole_scores[h + 1] ?? null),
      })), roundSI);
      const byKey = new Map(sk.players.map(p => [p.key, p]));
      // ⭐ v2.69.0 — hole_pts and pts move TOGETHER or not at all. The
      // scorecard relies on sum(hole_pts) === pts. Rewriting the strip in
      // skins while pts stayed on the stableford ramp would break that
      // invariant silently, which is worse than not showing skins at all.
      // So the strip is only converted when skins is the SEASON game.
      if (_seasonGame === 'overall_skins') {
        for (const e of entries) {
          const p = byKey.get(e.trip_player_id);
          e.hole_pts = new Array(18).fill(null);
          for (const hw of (p ? p.holes_won : [])) e.hole_pts[hw.hole - 1] = hw.skins;
          // A hole he played but did not win reads 0, not blank — a gap in the
          // strip means "no score", and he made one.
          for (let h = 0; h < 18; h++) if (e.hole_scores[h + 1] != null && e.hole_pts[h] == null) e.hole_pts[h] = 0;
          e.skins = p ? p.skins : 0;
          e.pts   = e.skins;
        }
      } else {
        // Skins is a side board this week — the count is reported, the
        // scorecard strip and pts are left on stableford.
        for (const e of entries) { const p = byKey.get(e.trip_player_id); e.skins = p ? p.skins : 0; }
      }
      weekSkins = {
        holes: sk.holes, total_skins: sk.total_skins, dead_skins: sk.dead_skins,
        field: entries.filter(e => e.holes > 0).length,
        money: skinsMoney(tr.skins_mode, tr.skins_amount, entries.filter(e => e.holes > 0).length, sk.total_skins),
      };
    }

    // ── v2.69.0 — low-wins side boards for the week. Computed for every
    // low-wins game on the round, primary or not. These NEVER feed the season
    // accumulator below (spec v1 §1 BIND) — they post and stop.
    const low_boards = {};
    for (const g of _mgList) {
      if (!LOW_WINS_FORMATS.has(g)) continue;
      low_boards[g] = lowBoard(
        entries.map(e => ({ key: e.trip_player_id, name: e.name, net: e.net, gross: e.gross, holes: e.holes })),
        g === 'low_net' ? 'net' : 'gross', 'holes');
    }

    // ⭐ v2.69.0 — season_game null means the week accrues NOTHING: no points,
    // no weeks_played, no win. It posts its boards and stops. Every other
    // value walks the same accumulator it always did.
    if (_seasonGame) for (const entry of entries) {
      if (!entry.holes) continue;
      const acc = season[entry.roster_id] || (season[entry.roster_id] = {
        roster_id: entry.roster_id, name: entry.name, total_pts: 0, total_gross: 0, total_net: 0, // v2.65.0
        weeks_played: 0, wins: 0, best_pts: null, best_week: null,
      });
      acc.total_pts   += entry.pts;
      acc.total_gross += entry.gross;
      acc.total_net   += entry.net;                                        // v2.65.0
      acc.weeks_played += 1;
      if (acc.best_pts === null || entry.pts > acc.best_pts) { acc.best_pts = entry.pts; acc.best_week = idx + 1; }
    }

    // Weekly result: points desc, low gross breaks the tie for display order —
    // but a WIN is shared on equal points (Bruce: call out ties).
    entries.sort((a, b) => (b.pts - a.pts) || (a.gross - b.gross) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    applyTieAwareRanks(entries, (prev, cur) => prev.pts === cur.pts);

    const scoredEntries = entries.filter(e => e.holes > 0);
    // v2.69.0 — a week that does not feed the season has no season winner
    // either. wins is a season standings column, so it obeys the same gate.
    const topPts  = (_seasonGame && scoredEntries.length) ? scoredEntries[0].pts : null;
    const winners = _seasonGame ? scoredEntries.filter(e => e.pts === topPts) : [];
    for (const win of winners) if (season[win.roster_id]) season[win.roster_id].wins += 1;

    weeks.push({
      league_round_id: w.league_round_id, week_number: idx + 1,
      date: w.round_date || (tr ? tr.date : null), trip_id: w.trip_id, trip_code: w.trip_code,
      trip_name: w.trip_name, status: tr ? tr.status : w.status, format: tr ? tr.format : null,
      games: _mgGames, games_list: _mgList, season_game: _seasonGame, low_boards, // v2.69.0
      course_name: tr ? tr.course_name : null, tee_name: tr ? tr.tee_name : null,
      scored: scoredEntries.length > 0,
      players_scored: scoredEntries.length, players_total: entries.length,
      winner_pts: topPts,
      winners: winners.map(e => ({ roster_id: e.roster_id, name: e.name, pts: e.pts, gross: e.gross, holes: e.holes })),
      skins: weekSkins,
      players: entries,
    });
  });

  const standings = Object.values(season).map(a => ({
    ...a,
    avg_pts: a.weeks_played ? Math.round((a.total_pts / a.weeks_played) * 10) / 10 : 0,
  }));
  standings.sort((a, b) => (b.total_pts - a.total_pts) || (b.weeks_played - a.weeks_played) || a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  applyTieAwareRanks(standings, (prev, cur) => prev.total_pts === cur.total_pts);

  // Extras — Bruce explicitly asked for volume.
  let bestRound = null;
  for (const w of weeks) {
    for (const e of w.players) {
      if (!e.holes) continue;
      if (!bestRound || e.pts > bestRound.pts || (e.pts === bestRound.pts && e.gross < bestRound.gross)) {
        bestRound = { name: e.name, roster_id: e.roster_id, pts: e.pts, gross: e.gross,
                      holes: e.holes, week_number: w.week_number, date: w.date, course_name: w.course_name };
      }
    }
  }
  const maxWins = standings.reduce((m, s2) => Math.max(m, s2.wins), 0);
  const mostWins = maxWins > 0 ? standings.filter(s2 => s2.wins === maxWins)
    .map(s2 => ({ name: s2.name, roster_id: s2.roster_id, wins: s2.wins })) : [];

  const dates = weeks.map(w => w.date).filter(Boolean).sort();
  return json({
    league: {
      id: league.id, name: league.name, description: league.description,
      watch_code: league.watch_code, home_course_name: league.home_course_name,
      season_start: dates[0] || null, season_end: dates[dates.length - 1] || null,
      weeks_total: weeks.length, weeks_scored: weeks.filter(w => w.scored).length,
    },
    weeks, standings,
    extras: { best_round: bestRound, most_wins: mostWins },
  }, 200, origin);
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PARDS BETS HANDLERS (v2.30.0)
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function handleCreateBetsRace(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  if (!body?.race_label) return err('race_label required', 400, origin);
  if (!body.event_id && !body.trip_id) return err('event_id or trip_id required', 400, origin);

  // Verify ownership
  if (body.event_id) {
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(body.event_id, account.id).first();
    if (!ev) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
  }
  if (body.trip_id) {
    const tr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(body.trip_id, account.id).first();
    if (!tr) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
  }

  // v2.49.0 — a race may now be pinned to one round of a multi-round event and
  // may choose its own runner shape. Both are optional: omitted, this behaves
  // exactly as v2.48.0 did (event-wide race, runner shape follows the event).
  let roundId = null;
  if (body.round_id != null && body.round_id !== '') {
    if (!body.event_id) return err('round_id requires event_id', 400, origin);
    roundId = Number(body.round_id);
    if (!Number.isFinite(roundId)) return err('round_id must be a number', 400, origin);
    // The round must belong to THIS event — otherwise a race could be pinned to
    // another organizer's round and auto-populate would scope off it.
    const rd = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM rounds WHERE id = ? AND event_id = ?')
      .bind(roundId, Number(body.event_id)).first();
    if (!rd) return err('Round not found for this event', 400, origin);
  }
  const runnerMode = (body.runner_mode === 'players' || body.runner_mode === 'groups')
    ? body.runner_mode : 'auto';

  const race = await env.DB.prepare(
    `INSERT INTO bets_races (event_id, trip_id, race_label, race_type, payout_mode, house_pct, status, round_id, runner_mode, auto_settle)
     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'open', ?, ?, ?) RETURNING *`
  ).bind(
    body.event_id || null, body.trip_id || null,
    body.race_label.trim(),
    body.race_type || 'round',
    body.payout_mode || 'simplified',
    body.house_pct ?? 0,
    roundId,
    runnerMode,
    body.auto_settle ? 1 : 0
  ).first();
  return json({ race }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleListBetsRaces(req, env, origin) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const eventId = url.searchParams.get('event_id');
  const tripId  = url.searchParams.get('trip_id');
  if (!eventId && !tripId) return err('event_id or trip_id required', 400, origin);

  let races;
  if (eventId) {
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(Number(eventId), account.id).first();
    if (!ev) return err('Event not found', 404, origin);
    // v2.49.0 — counts for the multi-race list (see note at handleListBetsRaces).
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.*,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bets_teams   t WHERE t.race_id = r.id) AS runner_count,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bets_bettors b WHERE b.race_id = r.id) AS bettor_count,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bets_wagers  w WHERE w.race_id = r.id) AS wager_count
       FROM bets_races r WHERE r.event_id = ? ORDER BY r.created_at ASC`
    ).bind(Number(eventId)).all();
    races = results;
  } else {
    const tr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(Number(tripId), account.id).first();
    if (!tr) return err('Trip not found', 404, origin);
    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT r.*,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bets_teams   t WHERE t.race_id = r.id) AS runner_count,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bets_bettors b WHERE b.race_id = r.id) AS bettor_count,
              (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bets_wagers  w WHERE w.race_id = r.id) AS wager_count
       FROM bets_races r WHERE r.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.created_at ASC`
    ).bind(Number(tripId)).all();
    races = results;
  }
  return json({ races }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleGetBetsRace(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const access = await betsRaceAccess(env, req, origin, raceId);
  if (access.error) return access.error;
  const race = access.race;

  const { results: teams }   = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY id').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: bettors } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY name').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: wagers }  = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_wagers WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY created_at').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: places }  = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_places WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY position').bind(raceId).all();

  // v2.50.0 — SEND THE PINNED ROUND. Purely additive: a new top-level `round`
  // key, NULL for an overall race. `race` itself is untouched, so a pre-v2.50.0
  // tote ignores this and behaves exactly as before.
  //
  // WHY: the tote printed a hardcoded "Texas Scramble" sub-header and a
  // hardcoded "TEAM NAME" column on every betting sheet, including the four
  // individual races whose runners are the 40 players. It also minted every
  // ticket as LCI-2026-0001, so all nine races on event 49 issued the same ref.
  // The tote already receives runner_mode / race_type / round_id via
  // `SELECT *`, so shape was always available — what it could NOT derive was
  // the round NUMBER (round_id 22 is not "R4") or the round's format. Both live
  // on `rounds`, so one indexed lookup by primary key supplies the ticket
  // prefix (R4G-) and an honest format line in the same trip.
  let round = null;
  if (race.round_id) {
    round = await env.DB.prepare(
      'SELECT id, round_number, format, date, session FROM rounds WHERE id = ?'
    ).bind(race.round_id).first();
  }

  return json({ race, round, teams, bettors, wagers, places }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleDeleteBetsRace(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const race = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_races WHERE id = ?').bind(raceId).first();
  if (!race) return err('Race not found', 404, origin);
  if (race.event_id) {
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.event_id, account.id).first();
    if (!ev) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  } else if (race.trip_id) {
    const tr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.trip_id, account.id).first();
    if (!tr) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  }
  await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_places  WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_wagers  WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_teams   WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId),
    env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_races   WHERE id = ?').bind(raceId),
  ]);
  return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: raceId }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleBetsAutoPopulate(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const race = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_races WHERE id = ?').bind(raceId).first();
  if (!race) return err('Race not found', 404, origin);

  // Verify ownership
  if (race.event_id) {
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.event_id, account.id).first();
    if (!ev) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  } else if (race.trip_id) {
    const tr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.trip_id, account.id).first();
    if (!tr) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  }

  let teamsAdded = 0, bettorsAdded = 0;

  if (race.event_id) {
    // v2.49.0 — RUNNER SHAPE IS A PROPERTY OF THE RACE, NOT OF THE EVENT.
    //
    // v2.47.0 made runners follow the event's own shape (individual -> PLAYERS,
    // team -> GROUPS), which fixed both of Bruce's real event shapes but still
    // assumed ONE race per event. Event 49 (Randy Goulet Memorial) runs NINE:
    // individual day money on R1/R2/R4 with the 40 players as runners, team day
    // money on all four rounds with the 10 groups, plus an overall of each. One
    // event therefore needs BOTH runner shapes live at the same time, so
    // `bets_races.runner_mode` decides it per race. 'auto' reproduces v2.47.0
    // exactly, and is what a pre-v2.49.0 client gets by omitting the field.
    //
    // The PLAYER source is new too. v2.47.0 read individual runners from
    // `config.event_roster`. Event 49's entire config is {"pards_bets":false} —
    // no roster_mode, no event_roster — so that path would have inserted ZERO
    // runners. This event keeps its 40 in `group_roster_players`, so that is now
    // the fallback, exactly as it already was for bettors.
    //
    // Bettors are now decoupled from runner shape completely: every race takes
    // the whole field. Resolution order is unchanged in effect (event_roster,
    // then group roster, then roster_event_links) — a team event with an empty
    // event_roster, which is every team event in live data, resolves to the same
    // people it did before.
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, name, config FROM events WHERE id = ?').bind(race.event_id).first();
    let cfg = {};
    try { cfg = ev && ev.config ? (typeof ev.config === 'object' ? ev.config : JSON.parse(ev.config)) : {}; } catch (e) { cfg = {}; }
    const eventRoster = Array.isArray(cfg.event_roster) ? cfg.event_roster : [];
    const runnerMode = (race.runner_mode === 'players' || race.runner_mode === 'groups')
      ? race.runner_mode
      : (cfg.roster_mode === 'individual' ? 'players' : 'groups');

    // ── The field: every person in this event, whichever table holds them ─
    let people = eventRoster.filter(p => p && p.id != null).map(p => ({ roster_id: p.id, name: p.name }));
    if (!people.length) {
      const { results: gm } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT DISTINCT r.id AS roster_id, r.name
         FROM group_roster_players grp
         JOIN groups g ON g.id = grp.group_id
         JOIN roster r ON r.id = grp.roster_id
         WHERE g.event_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
      ).bind(race.event_id).all();
      people = gm || [];
    }
    if (!people.length) {
      const { results: linked } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT DISTINCT r.id AS roster_id, r.name
         FROM roster_event_links rel JOIN roster r ON r.id = rel.roster_id
         WHERE rel.event_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
      ).bind(race.event_id).all();
      people = linked || [];
    }

    // ── Runners ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if (runnerMode === 'players') {
      for (const p of people) {
        if (!p || p.roster_id == null) continue;
        const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ? AND source_roster_id = ?').bind(raceId, p.roster_id).first();
        if (!exists) {
          await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO bets_teams (race_id, name, source_roster_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(raceId, p.name, p.roster_id).run();
          teamsAdded++;
        }
      }
    } else {
      const { results: groups } = await env.DB.prepare(
        'SELECT id, name FROM groups WHERE event_id = ? ORDER BY start_hole, name'
      ).bind(race.event_id).all();
      for (const g of groups) {
        const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ? AND source_group_id = ?').bind(raceId, g.id).first();
        if (!exists) {
          await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO bets_teams (race_id, name, source_group_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(raceId, g.name, g.id).run();
          teamsAdded++;
        }
      }
    }

    // ── Bettors: the whole field, on every race ───────────────────────────
    for (const p of people) {
      const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ? AND source_roster_id = ?').bind(raceId, p.roster_id).first();
      if (!exists) {
        await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO bets_bettors (race_id, name, source_roster_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(raceId, p.name, p.roster_id).run();
        bettorsAdded++;
      }
    }
  } else if (race.trip_id) {
    // Teams: trip tee times (foursomes) for round races, or individual players for individual races
    if (race.race_type === 'round' || race.race_type === 'overall_team') {
      // Use trip tee time group names (foursome label from tee sheet)
      const { results: tts } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT tt.id, tt.foursome_code, tt.hole, tr.round_number
         FROM trip_tee_times tt JOIN trip_rounds tr ON tr.id = tt.trip_round_id
         WHERE tr.trip_id = ? ORDER BY tr.round_number, tt.hole, tt.start_time`
      ).bind(race.trip_id).all();
      for (const tt of tts) {
        const label = `Hole ${tt.hole} Group`;
        const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ? AND source_group_id = ?').bind(raceId, tt.id).first();
        if (!exists) {
          await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO bets_teams (race_id, name, source_group_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(raceId, label, tt.id).run();
          teamsAdded++;
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Individual race: each trip player is a "team"
      const { results: players } = await env.DB.prepare(
        `SELECT tp.id, r.name FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id WHERE tp.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
      ).bind(race.trip_id).all();
      for (const p of players) {
        const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ? AND source_roster_id = ?').bind(raceId, p.id).first();
        if (!exists) {
          await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO bets_teams (race_id, name, source_roster_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(raceId, p.name, p.id).run();
          teamsAdded++;
        }
      }
    }
    // Bettors: all trip players
    const { results: tripPlayers } = await env.DB.prepare(
      `SELECT tp.id, r.id AS roster_id, r.name FROM trip_players tp JOIN roster r ON r.id = tp.roster_id WHERE tp.trip_id = ? ORDER BY r.name`
    ).bind(race.trip_id).all();
    for (const p of tripPlayers) {
      const exists = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ? AND source_roster_id = ?').bind(raceId, p.roster_id).first();
      if (!exists) {
        await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO bets_bettors (race_id, name, source_roster_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(raceId, p.name, p.roster_id).run();
        bettorsAdded++;
      }
    }
  }

  const { results: teams }   = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY id').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: bettors } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY name').bind(raceId).all();
  return json({ ok: true, teams_added: teamsAdded, bettors_added: bettorsAdded, teams, bettors }, 200, origin);
}

// v2.79.0 — B-29 (spec: _11\bug-log-addendum-s69.md). The tote's ➕ Add
// Bettor had NO server half: addBettor() pushed a local uid string, ticket
// submit did Number(uid) → NaN → 400 "bettor_id and wagers[] required" and an
// outsider's wagers could never save. Auth = betsRaceAccess, the same gate the
// wagers POST uses, so the race-scoped tote token works. Dup guard NOCASE →
// return the existing row (200) — re-adding "Alison Sails" twice must not
// split her tickets across two ids. New row → 201.
async function handleBetsAddBettor(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const access = await betsRaceAccess(env, req, origin, raceId);
  if (access.error) return access.error;
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  const name = body && typeof body.name === 'string' ? body.name.trim() : '';
  if (!name) return err('name required', 400, origin);
  if (name.length > 80) return err('name too long (80 max)', 400, origin);
  const existing = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ? AND name = ? COLLATE NOCASE'
  ).bind(raceId, name).first();
  if (existing) return json({ bettor: existing, existed: true }, 200, origin);
  const bettor = await env.DB.prepare(
    'INSERT INTO bets_bettors (race_id, name, source_roster_id) VALUES (?, ?, NULL) RETURNING *'
  ).bind(raceId, name).first();
  return json({ bettor, existed: false }, 201, origin);
}

async function handleBetsPlaceWagers(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const access = await betsRaceAccess(env, req, origin, raceId);
  if (access.error) return access.error;
  const race = access.race;
  // Auth first, THEN the open-check: previously a non-owner probing a
  // non-open race learned its status via 409; now everyone unauthorized
  // gets the same 401.
  if (race.status !== 'open') return err('Race is not open for betting', 409, origin);

  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  // body: { bettor_id, ticket_ref, wagers: [{team_id, type, amount}] }
  if (!body?.bettor_id || !Array.isArray(body.wagers) || !body.wagers.length) {
    return err('bettor_id and wagers[] required', 400, origin);
  }
  const bettor = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_bettors WHERE id = ? AND race_id = ?').bind(body.bettor_id, raceId).first();
  if (!bettor) return err('Bettor not found in this race', 404, origin);

  // v2.48.0 — FIELD WAGERS + REAL VALIDATION.
  //
  // The tote offers a "🌐 Field" bet (a unit staked on EVERY runner) and sent
  // it as team_id:'FIELD'. `bets_wagers.team_id` was INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES
  // bets_teams(id), so the insert could never succeed — and the tote's
  // d1SubmitWagers only console.error'd, so it failed SILENTLY: ticket printed,
  // cash taken, nothing in D1. team_id is now nullable with an is_field flag
  // (CHECK-constrained so exactly one of the two is set), and the unit +
  // runner-count are stored so the tote can rebuild the wager exactly.
  //
  // Everything is validated up front and rejected as a clean 400 — a bad
  // team_id used to surface as an FK failure inside a batch, i.e. a 500.
  const { results: raceTeams } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId).all();
  const teamIds = new Set((raceTeams || []).map(t => Number(t.id)));
  const ALLOWED_TYPES = new Set(['win', 'place', 'show']);

  const rows = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < body.wagers.length; i++) {
    const w = body.wagers[i] || {};
    const where = `wagers[${i}]`;
    if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.has(w.type)) {
      return err(`${where}: type must be win, place or show (got ${JSON.stringify(w.type)})`, 400, origin);
    }
    const amount = Number(w.amount);
    if (!isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) return err(`${where}: amount must be a positive number`, 400, origin);

    const isField = w.is_field === true || w.is_field === 1 || w.team_id === 'FIELD';
    if (isField) {
      const unit = Number(w.unit_amount);
      const count = Number(w.field_team_count);
      if (!isFinite(unit) || unit <= 0) return err(`${where}: a field wager needs a positive unit_amount`, 400, origin);
      if (!isFinite(count) || count < 1) return err(`${where}: a field wager needs field_team_count >= 1`, 400, origin);
      rows.push({ team_id: null, is_field: 1, unit_amount: unit, field_team_count: count, type: w.type, amount });
    } else {
      const teamId = Number(w.team_id);
      if (!teamIds.has(teamId)) return err(`${where}: team_id ${JSON.stringify(w.team_id)} is not a runner in this race`, 400, origin);
      rows.push({ team_id: teamId, is_field: 0, unit_amount: null, field_team_count: null, type: w.type, amount });
    }
  }

  const stmts = rows.map(r =>
    env.DB.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO bets_wagers (race_id, bettor_id, team_id, is_field, unit_amount, field_team_count, type, amount, ticket_ref)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
    ).bind(raceId, body.bettor_id, r.team_id, r.is_field, r.unit_amount, r.field_team_count, r.type, r.amount, body.ticket_ref || null)
  );
  await env.DB.batch(stmts);

  const { results: wagers } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT * FROM bets_wagers WHERE race_id = ? AND bettor_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC'
  ).bind(raceId, body.bettor_id).all();
  return json({ ok: true, wagers }, 201, origin);
}

// v2.54.0 — void one ticket. The tote's removeBet() ceremony (3 confirms for a
// locked ticket) calls this FIRST and only touches local state on success, so
// the screen can never claim a void D1 didn't perform. Whole-ticket only:
// paper slips are voided as a unit and the bettor resubmits — there is no
// single-bet surgery on a locked ticket, matching the tote UI.
async function handleBetsVoidTicket(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const access = await betsRaceAccess(env, req, origin, raceId);
  if (access.error) return access.error;
  const race = access.race;
  // Settled = payouts computed and cash (possibly) paid. Voiding now would
  // silently change money already handed out — refuse; the race-level DELETE
  // stays the nuclear option for a truly wrong settled race.
  if (race.status === 'settled') {
    return err('Race is settled — payouts are final. Void before settling, or delete the race.', 409, origin);
  }
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  const ticketRef = (url.searchParams.get('ticket_ref') || '').trim();
  if (!ticketRef) return err('ticket_ref query param required', 400, origin);
  const { results: rows } = await env.DB.prepare(
    'SELECT id, amount FROM bets_wagers WHERE race_id = ? AND ticket_ref = ?'
  ).bind(raceId, ticketRef).all();
  if (!rows || !rows.length) return err('No wagers on this race with that ticket_ref', 404, origin);
  const voidedTotal = rows.reduce((s, w) => s + (Number(w.amount) || 0), 0);
  await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM bets_wagers WHERE race_id = ? AND ticket_ref = ?').bind(raceId, ticketRef).run();
  return json({ ok: true, ticket_ref: ticketRef, deleted_count: rows.length, voided_total: voidedTotal }, 200, origin);
}

// Mint a race-scoped tote token. FULL session required — resolveSession
// directly, NOT betsRaceAccess, so a race token can never mint a fresh one
// and outlive its TTL. Ownership checked the same way every organizer-only
// bets handler checks it.
async function handleBetsMintRaceToken(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const race = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_races WHERE id = ?').bind(raceId).first();
  if (!race) return err('Race not found', 404, origin);
  if (race.event_id) {
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.event_id, account.id).first();
    if (!ev) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  } else if (race.trip_id) {
    const tr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.trip_id, account.id).first();
    if (!tr) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  }
  const token = generateToken();
  await env.SESSIONS.put(`racetok:${token}`, JSON.stringify({ race_id: raceId, account_id: account.id }), { expirationTtl: RACE_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS });
  return json({ ok: true, token, race_id: raceId, expires_in: RACE_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleBetsSetPlaces(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const access = await betsRaceAccess(env, req, origin, raceId);
  if (access.error) return access.error;
  const race = access.race;
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null);
  // body: { p1: team_id, p2: team_id, p3: team_id }
  if (!body?.p1 || !body?.p2 || !body?.p3) return err('p1, p2, p3 (team_ids) required', 400, origin);

  await env.DB.batch([
    env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO bets_places (race_id, position, team_id, settled_at) VALUES (?, 1, ?, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(race_id, position) DO UPDATE SET team_id=excluded.team_id, settled_at=excluded.settled_at`).bind(raceId, body.p1),
    env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO bets_places (race_id, position, team_id, settled_at) VALUES (?, 2, ?, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(race_id, position) DO UPDATE SET team_id=excluded.team_id, settled_at=excluded.settled_at`).bind(raceId, body.p2),
    env.DB.prepare(`INSERT INTO bets_places (race_id, position, team_id, settled_at) VALUES (?, 3, ?, datetime('now')) ON CONFLICT(race_id, position) DO UPDATE SET team_id=excluded.team_id, settled_at=excluded.settled_at`).bind(raceId, body.p3),
  ]);
  // Mark race as settled
  await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE bets_races SET status = 'settled' WHERE id = ?").bind(raceId).run();
  const { results: places } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_places WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY position').bind(raceId).all();
  return json({ ok: true, places }, 200, origin);
}

async function handleBetsResults(req, env, origin, raceId) {
  const account = await resolveSession(env, req);
  if (!account) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  const race = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_races WHERE id = ?').bind(raceId).first();
  if (!race) return err('Race not found', 404, origin);
  if (race.event_id) {
    const ev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM events WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.event_id, account.id).first();
    if (!ev) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  } else if (race.trip_id) {
    const tr = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id FROM trips WHERE id = ? AND organizer_id = ?').bind(race.trip_id, account.id).first();
    if (!tr) return err('Unauthorized', 401, origin);
  }

  const { results: teams }   = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_teams WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: bettors } = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_bettors WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: wagers }  = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_wagers WHERE race_id = ?').bind(raceId).all();
  const { results: places }  = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM bets_places WHERE race_id = ? ORDER BY position').bind(raceId).all();

  if (places.length < 3) {
    return json({ race, teams, bettors, wagers, places, payouts: null, settled: false }, 200, origin);
  }

  const p1id = places.find(p => p.position === 1)?.team_id;
  const p2id = places.find(p => p.position === 2)?.team_id;
  const p3id = places.find(p => p.position === 3)?.team_id;

  // Compute pools per wager type
  const types = ['win', 'place', 'show', 'exacta', 'trifecta'];
  const pools = {};
  for (const type of types) {
    pools[type] = wagers.filter(w => w.type === type).reduce((s, w) => s + w.amount, 0);
  }
  const totalPool = Object.values(pools).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
  const houseTake = Math.round(totalPool * (race.house_pct / 100) * 100) / 100;

  // Build payout map per bettor
  const payoutMap = {};
  for (const b of bettors) payoutMap[b.id] = { bettor: b, total_bet: 0, total_payout: 0, wagers: [] };

  for (const w of wagers) {
    const bettor = payoutMap[w.bettor_id];
    if (!bettor) continue;
    bettor.total_bet += w.amount;
    let payout = 0;
    const netPool = (pool) => pool * (1 - race.house_pct / 100);

    if (race.payout_mode === 'simplified') {
      // Simplified: fixed multipliers
      if (w.type === 'win'      && w.team_id === p1id) payout = w.amount * 3;
      if (w.type === 'place'    && (w.team_id === p1id || w.team_id === p2id)) payout = w.amount * 2;
      if (w.type === 'show'     && (w.team_id === p1id || w.team_id === p2id || w.team_id === p3id)) payout = w.amount * 1.5;
      if (w.type === 'exacta'   && w.team_id === p1id) {
        // exacta: bettor must have also bet p2 in their slip — simplified: just pay 5x if they picked winner
        payout = w.amount * 5;
      }
      if (w.type === 'trifecta' && w.team_id === p1id) payout = w.amount * 10;
    } else {
      // True parimutuel
      const winPool = netPool(pools['win']);
      const placePool = netPool(pools['place']);
      const showPool = netPool(pools['show']);
      const stakesWin   = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='win'   && x.team_id===p1id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
      const stakesP1P   = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='place' && x.team_id===p1id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
      const stakesP2P   = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='place' && x.team_id===p2id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
      const stakesP1S   = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='show'  && x.team_id===p1id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
      const stakesP2S   = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='show'  && x.team_id===p2id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
      const stakesP3S   = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='show'  && x.team_id===p3id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
      if (w.type === 'win' && w.team_id === p1id && stakesWin > 0)
        payout = w.amount * (winPool / stakesWin);
      if (w.type === 'place') {
        const placeHalf = placePool / 2;
        if (w.team_id === p1id && stakesP1P > 0) payout = w.amount * ((placeHalf / stakesP1P) + 1);
        if (w.team_id === p2id && stakesP2P > 0) payout = w.amount * ((placeHalf / stakesP2P) + 1);
      }
      if (w.type === 'show') {
        const showThird = showPool / 3;
        if (w.team_id === p1id && stakesP1S > 0) payout = w.amount * ((showThird / stakesP1S) + 1);
        if (w.team_id === p2id && stakesP2S > 0) payout = w.amount * ((showThird / stakesP2S) + 1);
        if (w.team_id === p3id && stakesP3S > 0) payout = w.amount * ((showThird / stakesP3S) + 1);
      }
      if (w.type === 'exacta' && w.team_id === p1id) {
        const exactaPool = netPool(pools['exacta']);
        const exactaStakes = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='exacta' && x.team_id===p1id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
        if (exactaStakes > 0) payout = w.amount * (exactaPool / exactaStakes);
      }
      if (w.type === 'trifecta' && w.team_id === p1id) {
        const trifPool = netPool(pools['trifecta']);
        const trifStakes = wagers.filter(x => x.type==='trifecta' && x.team_id===p1id).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
        if (trifStakes > 0) payout = w.amount * (trifPool / trifStakes);
      }
    }
    payout = Math.round(payout * 100) / 100;
    bettor.total_payout += payout;
    bettor.wagers.push({ ...w, payout });
  }

  const payouts = Object.values(payoutMap).map(b => ({
    bettor_id: b.bettor.id, bettor_name: b.bettor.name,
    total_bet: Math.round(b.total_bet * 100) / 100,
    total_payout: Math.round(b.total_payout * 100) / 100,
    net: Math.round((b.total_payout - b.total_bet) * 100) / 100,
    wagers: b.wagers,
  }));
  payouts.sort((a, b) => b.total_payout - a.total_payout);

  return json({
    race, teams, bettors, wagers, places, payouts,
    summary: {
      total_pool: Math.round(totalPool * 100) / 100,
      house_take: houseTake,
      net_pool: Math.round((totalPool - houseTake) * 100) / 100,
      pools,
      p1: teams.find(t => t.id === p1id) || null,
      p2: teams.find(t => t.id === p2id) || null,
      p3: teams.find(t => t.id === p3id) || null,
    },
    settled: true,
  }, 200, origin);
}
